# Code map

Where things live in this repository, so a change starts from the right file. Read this first,
then open the files and symbols it names. Find a symbol with `rg -n <symbol> <path>`. Line
numbers are left out on purpose, because they drift.

A line of the form "- `path`: what it is. Key symbols: `a`, `b`." is checked by
`codemap.test.ts` at the root: the path has to exist, and each key symbol has to occur in that
file as a whole word. A change that adds, moves or removes a file or a key symbol updates its line
here in the same change.

`ARCHITECTURE.md` says how the client is built and why. `docs/design-system.md` says how it looks.
`AGENTS.md` says how to run things. This file only says where.

## The shape in one paragraph

One SvelteKit app and five packages. `apps/local` is the client a person runs: a SvelteKit server
on localhost with adapter-node, the pages, every `/api` route, and the run loop in
`apps/local/src/lib/loop.ts`, one loop per project, over the one scheduler per data folder that every
project shares (`apps/local/src/lib/shared-scheduler.ts`). `packages/core` holds
the rules (boards, stages, models, settings, queue, run fold, Scout folds) as pure code with no Node built-ins,
plus the sqlite layer in `packages/core/src/node`. `packages/protocol` holds the
shared types and the wire to the hub, and imports nothing. `packages/runner` spawns the engine
CLIs, parses their lines, and does all git work. `packages/ui` holds the Svelte components and
their pure rules, and imports only protocol. `packages/skills` holds the phase skills an engine
runs. The data lives in `~/.nolzu` (`~/.nolzu-fake` with `NOLZU_FAKE_AGENT=1`): the project in
`nolzu.db`, and the machine settings, the project registry and the writer lock in `machine.db`. The browser never
holds busy state: it reads one server sent event stream of whole snapshots.

## Config, build and CI

- `package.json`: root scripts `dev`, `build`, `test`, `typecheck`, `lint`, `skills:lint`, `smoke`, `pack:check`. Key symbols: `typecheck`, `packageManager`, `smoke`.
- `pnpm-workspace.yaml`: the workspace, `packages/*` and `apps/*`, with the Node version enforced. Key symbols: `engineStrict`.
- `vitest.config.ts`: the one test config, as inline projects: one per package, `@nolzu/ui-mounted` for `*.mount.test.ts` in jsdom, `workspace` for the root tests and `test/`, `@nolzu/local` and `@nolzu/local-mounted` for the app. Aliases `$lib` and `$app/*` to the stubs. 20 second timeout. Key symbols: `svelteProjects`, `MOUNTED`, `appAliases`, `testTimeout`, `globalSetup`, `setupFiles`.
- `vitest.global-setup.ts`: runs once before the workers. Temp `HOME`, `NOLZU_DATA_DIR` inside it, `NOLZU_FAKE_AGENT` removed, `TZ=UTC`, and the real data folder handed to the workers as `liveDataDir`. Key symbols: `setup`, `liveDataDir`.
- `vitest.setup.ts`: every test file. Wraps `resolveDataDir` so a test that reaches the live data folder throws, and refuses fetches to the time tracker hosts. Key symbols: `resolveDataDir`, `TRACKERS`.
- `tsconfig.base.json`: strict base for everything. Key symbols: `noUncheckedIndexedAccess`, `verbatimModuleSyntax`, `allowImportingTsExtensions`.
- `tsconfig.json`: the root typecheck: `packages`, `apps/local/scripts`, `test` and the root `*.ts`. The app's own source is checked by svelte-check through its `check` script. Key symbols: `include`, `exclude`.
- `eslint.config.js`: ESLint for TypeScript and Svelte, plus the import boundaries: protocol imports nothing, core imports protocol (no Node outside `src/node`, no spawn anywhere), runner imports protocol and core, ui and skills import protocol, the app imports all. It skips `.vitest`, the scratch folder the wall tests write, so a leftover fixture cannot fail the lint. Key symbols: `boundary`, `pureCore`, `pureProtocol`, `noSpawn`, `nodeGlobals`.
- `.github/workflows/ci.yml`: on pull requests and on `main` only. Install, lint, typecheck, test, smoke on the fake engine, build, pack check, skills lint, a dash check over `*.md`, `*.svelte` and `*.html`, then a gitleaks scan of the whole history. Nothing is published from CI, no job gets `packages: write`, and no checkout keeps the token. Key symbols: `checks`, `gitleaks`.
- `.github/pull_request_template.md`: what an outside pull request fills in: the issue it closes, what changed, how it was tested, which AI tools helped, and two ticks, that the author can explain the change and that every commit is signed off. `CONTRIBUTING.md` holds the rules behind it.
- `apps/local/package.json`: the app, packed as the `nolzu` package. `build` runs vite twice: the server, then `vite.bin.config.ts`. Key symbols: `check`, `files`.
- `apps/local/svelte.config.js`: SvelteKit with adapter-node. Key symbols: `adapter`.
- `apps/local/vite.config.ts`: SvelteKit plus the fonts folder of `@nolzu/ui` on the dev server allow list, and `__NOLZU_BUILD__`, the version, short commit (null on a release tag or with no git) and date read at build time, so the app runs no git. `buildDefine` hands the same define to `vite.bin.config.ts`. Key symbols: `fonts`, `git`, `build`, `buildDefine`, which comes from `readBuild`.
- `apps/local/vite.bin.config.ts`: bundles `bin/nolzu.js` and everything it imports into `dist/nolzu.js`, with its chunks (`start.js` among them) beside it in `dist/` and not in `assets/`, because Node strips no TypeScript under `node_modules`, with the app's `buildDefine` so `--version` prints the line the rail shows. Its target is `node22.16`, the user floor in `engines`. It writes `dist/THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.md` last. Key symbols: `outDir`, `noExternal`, `buildDefine`, `target`.
- `apps/local/notices.ts`: the third-party notices. `recordPackages` lists each package a build puts in its output, plus every import it left for adapter-node with that import's dependencies, in `.svelte-kit/output/bundled-packages.json`; `writeNotices` writes each one's name, version, license and license text. Key symbols: `recordPackages`, `writeNotices`, `ADAPTER_INSIDE`.
- `apps/local/tsconfig.json`: extends the generated SvelteKit config. Key symbols: `extends`.
- `packages/core/tsconfig.build.json`: what `pnpm build` emits into `packages/core/dist` for publishing. Key symbols: `outDir`.
- `packages/protocol/tsconfig.build.json`: the same for protocol. Key symbols: `outDir`.
- `packages/ui/tsconfig.json`: the svelte-check config of ui over all of `src`, the mounted tests included, which the root typecheck leaves out. Key symbols: `include`.

## The local app (`apps/local`)

### Launcher and server start

- `apps/local/bin/nolzu.js`: the `nolzu` command's entry. Before any other import it reads `engines.node` from package.json and stops an older Node with one sentence (`nodeRefusal` in `apps/local/bin/node-check.js`, plain JavaScript with no import, so Node 18 reads it), drops Node 22's one experimental warning for `node:sqlite` (`quietSqliteWarning`), then loads `start.js`. Key symbols: `nodeRefusal`, `quietSqliteWarning`.
- `apps/local/bin/start.js`: the rest of the command. It first stops on native Windows and WSL1 with the WSL2 line (`platformRefusal`, from `/proc/sys/kernel/osrelease`). `--version` prints `versionOutput` and leaves, asking git only in a clone. Account commands (`login`, `logout`, `status`) run here and then sync or remove the paid package. `serve` finds a free port, refuses a second server on the same data folder, starts `build/index.js` and opens the browser. Key symbols: `parseCommand`, `accountCommand`, `syncPackage`, `removePackages`, `freePort`, `alreadyRunning`, `writeRecord`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/bin.ts`: the pure half of the launcher: argument parsing, the port range, the running record, the host checks the server hook reuses, the platform refusal (`platformRefusal`), the `--version` line (`versionOutput`, the build's `__NOLZU_BUILD__` in the packed command, `readBuild` with git in a clone), and the one second shutdown wait (`shutdownDefault`, so Ctrl+C does not wait 30 seconds on an open page's stream). `crossSiteWrite` also reads `Origin` when `Sec-Fetch-Site` is missing; `crossSiteRead` is what a page load asks before it runs or writes anything. `crossSiteFetch` refuses another site's background GET and lets only a navigation to a page through. `crossSiteFetch` lets a style, image or font of a card file (`/api/card/<key>/files/...`) through, because the Files tab frames a design screen in an empty sandbox. Key symbols: `PORT_RANGE`, `parseCommand`, `versionOutput`, `shutdownDefault`, `USAGE`, `freePort`, `startLine`, `alreadyRunning`, `platformRefusal`, `readRecord`, `writeRecord`, `runningAt`, `localhostRedirect`, `crossSiteWrite`, `foreignHost`, `crossSiteRead`, `crossSiteFetch`.
- `apps/local/src/hooks.server.ts`: `init` builds the loop and starts the hub link once at server start, and only once the terms are accepted (`startWhenAccepted`; before that it takes the writer lock alone), and through `stopOnSignals` calls `shutdown` once on SIGINT, SIGTERM or SIGHUP (a closed terminal): a repeat is nothing, a second Ctrl+C quits with 130, a stop longer than `FORCE_QUIT_MS` quits with a line, a failed stop prints one line, (sending the signal again when nothing else listens, as under the dev server). `handle` first answers Chrome's DevTools probe (`/.well-known/appspecific/com.chrome.devtools.json`) with an empty 404 and never serves the file (under vite dev `quietProbe` in `$lib/devtools-probe.ts`, a middleware vite.config.ts puts before SvelteKit's, answers it the same way), then refuses a foreign host (DNS rebinding), redirects to localhost, refuses a cross site write, sends a page to `/accept` and refuses an endpoint until the terms are accepted (`termsGate`), and sends `/scout` and `/evidence` to Now while `SCOUT_AND_EVIDENCE` is off. `handle` wraps that `guard` and puts `X-Frame-Options: DENY` and `frame-ancestors 'none'` on every response through `framed`, a refusal and a redirect included, keeping a response's own `frame-ancestors` (card files say `'self'`). The Scout and Evidence gate reads the decoded path (`decodedPath`). A card screen's stylesheet from its sandboxed frame passes `crossSiteFetch`. Key symbols: `init`, `handle`, `guard`, `framed`, `stopOnSignals`, `termsGate`, `workStarted`, `FORCE_QUIT_MS`, `startHubLink`, `closeHubLink`, `shutdown`, `foreignHost`, `crossSiteWrite`.
- `apps/local/src/app.html`: the page template. Sets the stored theme before the first paint, and on `/b/<board>` marks `<html>` with `data-board-view` when that board was left on List, so the server's Board press never shows. Key symbols: `localStorage`, `boardView`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/db.ts`: `database(project?)`, a second sqlite connection per project for page loads and plain reads. It is not the loop's connection. A route gets the project it names or the current one (the machine key `currentProject`), as `chosenProject` picks it by the rules of `activeProject`. `forgetProject` drops the connection of a project New project took back, whose id the next one gets. `openFirstThatOpens` opens the first project, or, when its file is damaged (`damagedDatabase`: SQLite errcode 26 or 11), the next registered project that does (a next project's error that is no damaged file stops the search too); any other refusal, such as a second Nolzu holding the folder, a busy or newer database, passes through. `standInOf` names the stand-in. `loop` and `loopOf` reach the machine through it, kept as its stand-in for the process; with none it throws a sentence naming the data folder. and so does `database`. Key symbols: `database`, `chosenProject`, `forgetProject`, `openFirstThatOpens`, `damagedDatabase`, `standInOf`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/home-path.ts`: a folder with the home folder shown as `~`. Key symbols: `homePath`.

### Page routes

- `apps/local/src/routes/accept/+page.server.ts`: the terms on the first start and after they change, with no rail. The load sends an accepted machine on; the default action needs the box ticked, stores the version and the time (`acceptTerms`) and goes where the person was going (`afterAccept`). Key symbols: `load`, `actions`, `acceptTerms`, `afterAccept`, `termsState`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/+layout.server.ts`: every page. On `/accept` it reads nothing and hands back `HELD`. Sends a machine with no project yet to `/new`, reads the signed display document, dials the hub again when the plan changed, and passes the project, the key prefix, the ticket system, off boards, rail usage, the offline banner, the workspace offer Now draws, the first run's guidance for the rail's first paint, every project the selector lists with its counts (`projectChoices`), the project files status (`projectFilesNotice`, kept per project until a write), and the Workspace rail count from the last read (`workspaceCount`, no git on the load). Key symbols: `load`, `onboardRedirect`, `currentDisplay`, `dialHubAgain`, `offlineBanner`, `railUsage`, `workspaceNote`, `firstRun`, `projectChoices`, `projectFilesNotice`, `workspaceCount`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/+layout.svelte`: the shell. Opens the one `/api/now` stream into `nowLive`, mounts `Rail`, `Palette`, `Keyboard`, `ToastCorner` and the feature panels, and imports `/api/package/entry` after putting `registerFeature` and `loadFeature` on `globalThis.nolzu`. The rail folds on a board, except while Getting started shows (`firstRun`, off the stream or the load). Draws the question at the first QA (`QaAskDialog`) while a screen holds a move open in `qaAsk`, and the bell's panel (`NotificationsPanel`) while `noticesOpen`, with the unread count and the newest few off the stream (`notices`). Raises the run, Scout and near-limit toasts off the same stream into `toastStack` (`nowToasts`, with a fresh `Heard` per stream). Hands the rail Scout's new-findings dot off the stream (`scoutNew`), and the selector's projects the busiest first, the current one counted off the stream (`selectorRows`), the count after each board (`railCounts`), and registers `scout:check`, the palette's "Check every Scout source now", which opens Scout and presses its Check all sources (`checkScout`). Draws `ProjectFilesNotice` in the offline banner's status region on every page with the rail but Settings, Project, and in the same region on the boards and on Now the banner of Set up the project (`SetupSteps`) off the stream's `setup`. Draws the Learn dialog (`SetupGateDialog`) while a screen holds a first run open in `setupGate`. Key symbols: `SetupSteps`, `SetupGateDialog`, `setupGate`, `ProjectFilesNotice`, `connectBoard`, `browserStream`, `nowLive`, `nowToasts`, `toastStack`, `registerFeature`, `loadFeature`, `waitingCount`, `firstRun`, `QaAskDialog`, `qaAsk`, `scoutNew`, `checkScout`, `selectorRows`, `switched`, `NotificationsPanel`, `noticesOpen`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/+page.svelte`: Now, at `/`. Draws `NowPage` from `nowLive`. Key symbols: `NowPage`, `nowLive`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/+error.svelte`: the error page with padding.
- `apps/local/src/routes/b/[board]/+page.ts`: `/b/<board>`. 404 for a name that is no board, the off state for a board the project turned off, and the old `/b/investigations` and `/b/questions` sent on to Research. Key symbols: `load`, `isResearchKind`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/b/research/+page.ts`: `/b/research`, the Research board. Off with both its kinds, read off the layout's `offBoards`. Key symbols: `load`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/b/research/+page.svelte`: draws `BoardPage` with `research`, or `BoardOff` for Research. Key symbols: `BoardPage`, `BoardOff`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/b/[board]/+page.svelte`: draws `BoardPage`, or `BoardOff` for an off board. Key symbols: `BoardPage`, `BoardOff`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/t/[key]/+layout.server.ts`: one card. Reads the ticket with its size and its tabs (`ticketLayout`), the time the page is drawn with (`now`), which the trail's times ago start from, what "Run on" can offer, the card's own model rows for the decision panel's picker (`settingsFor`), and what each tab holds (`tabCounts`, and on a board with a Changes tab `changesCount`, which asks git and is returned as a promise SvelteKit streams (`changes`), so no tab waits on git, except on a closed card, `closed`, whose tag the page reads off its pull request), and there the count of diff comments not sent yet (`openComments`), which Send back with comments counts. Key symbols: `load`, `ticketLayout`, `runOnOffer`, `settingsFor`, `tabCounts`, `changesCount`, `changes`, `openCommentCount`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/t/[key]/+layout.svelte`: the card page shell. The head scrolls with the page: the breadcrumb (Research for a question or an investigation, `choiceOf`), K and J to the cards beside it in board order (`besideCard`), the title, the pill in the board card's words (`cardPill` over `liveOf` with a clock and the card's `column`), the stage and size chips, Minimize by the title on every tab (`minimize`, M presses it, Restore once pressed, none on a closed card), and `TabBar` with counts, the Changes count added once git answers (`streamed`); a closed Delivery card's Changes tag reads "merged" once its pull request does (`readCardPulls`, `pullRead`), and the same read gives the record its Pull request row (`pullNotes`, `pullTrail`). On the overview it draws two columns: the tab on the left, and on the right `DecisionPanel`, the trail (`PhaseStrip`, with who moved the card into each phase, the wait time `waitAt` and run end times drawn as times ago on the page's `clock`, which starts at the layout's `now`, the server's time, and ticks every half minute, every second while the card runs) and the facts, read off the overview's page data. The facts are streamed, so they draw in `Streamed` with a placeholder, and a fact with a file (the discoveries) links to it on the Files tab, and Fix it's session comes off the overview's `implementSession`, never off the facts. The Review tab of a pull request card has the two columns too, with the panel alone on the right: why another round makes sense while the next step is Agent pre-review (`reason`), and the shown round for Copy all findings (`conventionalComments`, `copyText`). A closed card draws its record there instead (`CardRecord`: the totals and every move). Reads the card off the Now stream, or its own board stream for a board that is off (`streamAddress`). A blocked card's wait time comes off the stream's `waiting` (`waitSince`), and the panel's phone line off the display document's plan. The panel's props come from `nextStepOf` over the open comment count (`step`), and its moves, retries, Run on, stop, minimize, Fix it and Send back with comments from `nextStepWrites` (`writes`), both shared with the peek; the picker's row writes (`pickRow`, the engine and the model in one write) stay here. The run keys press the panel's buttons, or send the same write off the overview (`act`). Delete card from the panel's More actions, or the red button under a closed card's record, opens `DeleteCard` (`deleting`) and goes back to the board once the card is gone (`toBoard`). Key symbols: `DeleteCard`, `deleting`, `toBoard`, `connectBoard`, `streamAddress`, `DecisionPanel`, `CardRecord`, `TabBar`, `Streamed`, `streamed`, `StartConfirm`, `keyWrite`, `nextStepOf`, `nextStepWrites`, `step`, `writes`, `besideCard`, `cardPill`, `column`, `pullRead`, `pullNotes`, `waitSince`, `pickRow`, `act`, `conventionalComments`, `copyText`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/t/[key]/+page.server.ts`: `/t/<key>` redirects to the overview tab. Key symbols: `load`, `TAB_REDIRECT`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/t/[key]/[tab]/+page.server.ts`: one tab. Awaits only the database and the card folder: every part that runs git or a project command is returned as a promise SvelteKit streams (`groups`, `facts`, `qaStatus`, `knowledgePatches`), and so are the discussion's tool calls, read from each run's log (`discussionTools`), so the page navigates at once. Reads only what the open tab shows: overview with the facts of the right column (`cardFacts`, with the models for the next step's engine), whether the card waits in QA on a FAIL (`qaFailed`, read off its stage too) and whether it sits in a QA stage (`qaStage`) and, on a closed card, the wrap-up's knowledge change with its patch (`cardKnowledge`), QA panels, files, changes (git) with the card's diff comments and the Implement session a send back goes on in (`implementSession`), discussion, log, review, settings, repositories, and the card's pull requests on PRs (`cardPullRows`, no gh call). An overview that shows a plain summary marks Getting started's "Read what changed". On the overview of a Delivery card from Review to Wrap-up, why each open pull request cannot merge as the last read stored it (`merge`, off `cardPullRows`, no gh call). Key symbols: `load`, `ticketTab`, `overviewFor`, `cardFacts`, `qaFailed`, `qaStage`, `cardKnowledge`, `markStep`, `qaInputFor`, `stackStatusFor`, `qaStatus`, `knowledgePatches`, `discussionTools`, `withTools`, `filesFor`, `changeGroups`, `commentsOf`, `implementSession`, `discussionFor`, `logFor`, `reviewFor`, `settingsFor`, `cardReposFor`, `merge`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/t/[key]/[tab]/+page.svelte`: draws the tab component and does its writes. The Changes groups draw in `Streamed`; the QA panels draw from the load's input at once and add the status command's answer when it lands (`stackStatus`), and a closed card's knowledge rows add their patches and the discussion its tool calls the same way (`patches`, `tools`, merged by id with `mergeById`). A git write or a stack command waits for the new streamed parts before it answers (`reread`). Answer later on a question links to the card's board (`laterHref`), and a QA FAIL hands the panels the QA run off the trail. On a closed card the Overview asks for the pull request state once it has drawn (`readPulls`, the read the head's Changes tag shares through `readCardPulls`), Revert posts the change (`revert`), and the page hands it the checkouts section as a snippet. An epic's task table reads the Delivery board off the Now stream (`nowLive`), and Add sends each task's key, description and plan step (`addTasks`). A diff comment, its edit and its delete go to the comment routes and read the page again (`commentWrite`). The Changes view comes from the address or the stage (`viewOf`, `changesView`), the switch puts it in the address (`pickView`), and a commit of the list reads its files (`commitDiff`). While a comment is open the Changes tab hands `ChangeGroups` a `next` snippet with `SendBackPanel`, which holds on the card of the Delivery snapshot and posts `sendBackRun`; a refusal goes to the toast stack (`refused`). The Log tab's Stop reads the card off its board in the Now stream (`logCard`; a board that is off is not there, so no Stop) and posts the stop (`stopRun`). The Settings tab's pick, Reset and preset switch go to the card's models route (`models`). The PRs tab's attach and detach go to the links route (`pullLink`), Read comments posts the comments route and shows its sentence (`readComments`), Mark done, Reopen and Needs action go to the discussions route (`markComment`), and the tab marks the messages seen as it opens (`seenComments`). Run triage posts the triage route (`runTriage`), held on the card of the Delivery snapshot, and Copy puts a drafted reply on the clipboard (`copyReply`). Fix posts the fix route and reads the rows again on a refusal (`fixComment`), and Discuss posts the discuss route and opens the Discussion tab on the ref (`discussComment`). - `apps/local/src/routes/settings/+page.svelte`: `/settings`, which draws nothing and moves on in the browser, because a server redirect cannot see the hash: a plain `/settings` to Project, an old `#<id>` link to its section's page or where an old section went, and any other hash to that feature block's anchor on Project. - `apps/local/src/routes/settings/[section]/+page.svelte`: the forms of Engines, Runs and Cloud, each under its `section-head` title, and `ModelsSection` with the answer of the `models` action. Runs is one form per row that saves on change: the slot stepper, whose buttons post the count they lead to and stop at `AGENT_SLOTS`, with `SlotsMeter` off the Now stream (`nowLive`); the timeout; the retry switch and, while it is on, three minute boxes (`RETRY_BOXES`), each its own form posting `wait1` to `wait3`; the quiet time; keep awake, which names the systems it works on only where this one cannot (`awake`). The switches are checkboxes drawn as `Switch`, cream when on. "Start Nolzu when I log in" on Runs posts `{ on }` to `/api/login-item` and reads the disk again (`startAtLogin`). The Knowledge switch sits at the foot of Runs at `#knowledge` and posts to `knowledge`. Both end with `DataSync`, the box What goes to a hub.
