- `apps/local/src/routes/settings/project/+page.server.ts`: `/settings/project`, the first settings section, which was `/projects`. The load hands the page the project row, the client and rate (`billingFor`), and, for a project with a repository table, the repositories found on this machine it has not got (`machineRepos` through `foundToAdd`, not awaited). Actions `bill` (the client, the rate and the currency together through `saveBilling`, as Evidence saves them), `save` (a field, a board switch, or one command chip added or removed), `addCommands` (the commands ticked under Propose again, which the load hands as `proposal`, through `addCommands`), `confirmCommands` ("The list is right"; it and any change to the command list mark the Confirm the run commands step done with `markSetup`), `tickets` (local or Jira, both ways, then `checkKeyClash`; the Jira login is on Settings, Integrations), `qaRepo` (Edit on a row of Start the app: one repository's answer, or `*`'s, through `answerQa`), `qaClear` (Ask again on a row, `clearAnswer`), `qa` (Ask again for all, which takes back every QA answer), the QA browser row's `browserSetup` (Nolzu's browser into the MCP list through `setUpBrowser`), `browserOff` (`turnOffBrowser`) and `browserTest` (`testBrowser` on a config `runMcp` writes into a temp folder that goes right after), `remove` (the delete pass), `add` (its answer names the folder it cloned as `learn`), `learn` (Learn this project for that repository, `startLearnPost`), `clone` (one row's folder, or every missing one), `reveal` (the project folder in the file manager), `rescan`, `convert`, `files` (its answer also carries how many files it wrote and how many the person changed by hand, for the project files notice, which posts here too), `take` and `keep` (one file changed by hand, `takeShipped` and `keepMine`; a refused take with `ask` is the server asking once more), `checkouts`. The load also hands the files changed by hand with their diffs (`handEditedFiles`) and origin's branches for the base branch boxes (`projectBranches`), both not awaited, so the page draws before git answers. Key symbols: `load`, `actions`, `projectRow`, `projectBranches`, `foundToAdd`, `machineRepos`, `billingFor`, `saveBilling`, `saveProjectField`, `rescanRepos`, `addRepository`, `cloneMissing`, `removeRepository`, `revealProject`, `makeThisAWorkspace`, `applySetup`, `setupSaid`, `removeClosedCheckouts`, `fillAllowedCommands`, `checkKeyClash`, `switchTickets`, `setUpBrowser`, `turnOffBrowser`, `runMcp`, `answerQa`, `clearAnswer`, `testBrowser`, `startLearnPost`, `markSetup`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/settings/phases/+page.server.ts`: `/settings/phases`, Phase instructions. The load reads each phase's file in the project folder off the disk (`readPhaseInstructions`), with its label and path, and leaves out Assessment while `SCOUT_AND_EVIDENCE` is off. Action `save` writes one phase's file, or deletes it on an empty text, through `savePhaseInstructions`, and sends a refusal back with what was typed. Key symbols: `load`, `actions`, `INSTRUCTION_PHASES`, `savePhaseInstructions`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/settings/mcp/+page.server.ts`: `/settings/mcp`, MCP servers. The load is `mcpPage`; actions `save`, `delete`, `phase`, `secret` and `test` call `mcp-page.ts` (`test` saves and commits nothing and is never a refusal), `find` sends back what `findImports` found by name and kind only, and `import` calls `importServers`; all with `appKeychain` and tell the Now stream. A typed secret is never sent back. Key symbols: `load`, `actions`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/settings/mcp/+page.svelte`: one lead line, the Cursor and Codex notes on what their own configs add (only while a profile runs on them, `cursor` and `codex` from `mcpPage`), one row per server (kind, command or address, the tools line, the read-only warning, the Cursor warning on a server with a secret, the Codex SSE note on an SSE server while a Codex profile exists, the folder note on a local server with a folder, a "Needs secret" field per missing secret, Test with its answer on the row, Edit and Delete), Import from my engines with the found servers to pick and each value to mark plain, the add and edit form (name, description, local or remote, command and arguments or address and transport, folder, allowed tools, which a form Test that lists the tools turns into a box per tool with a saved tool the server dropped kept and marked, the form's Test with its answer, env and header rows each plain or secret), and the grid of servers by phase, one checkbox form per cell, fitting the page at 1280 and scrolling in its own box at a narrow width with the server column held and a shade on the side with more, then "How each engine gets these servers", closed (`notes`: the shared file, the keychain, what each engine does with the list, no OAuth). A server that misses a secret says a secret is asked again when its command, arguments, folder, address or a plain value changes. Key symbols: `editing`, `draft`, `open`, `saving`, `testRow`, `formTest`, `values`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/settings/phases/+page.svelte`: the page's explanation in one `InfoBox` under the title, then one form per phase: the label with a `HelpTip` (its `PHASE_HELP` line, then `PHASE_INSTRUCTION_HELP`: what it writes, what an instruction is good for, an example), a textarea, the file's path alone under it (where the files live, Commit them and an empty Save are said once in the `InfoBox`), all rows in one box, Save, and the refusal or "Saved." under the phase it belongs to. Each form posts with `enhance` and no reset (`keep`), so a Save far down the list does not jump to the top. Key symbols: `keep`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/settings/project/+page.svelte`: the Project section: its title, `ProjectsList` (handed the found repositories and the billing), the paid package's `projects` panel slot, which kept its name, and at its foot the feature settings blocks under their anchors. Key symbols: `ProjectsList`, `FeaturePanels`, `FeatureSettings`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/settings/integrations/+page.server.ts`: `/settings/integrations`, Integrations, the machine's logins. The load hands the gh login (`ghLogin`, not awaited) and the Jira site and email, never the token. Actions `jira` (Connect and Edit: `connectJira`, then `checkKeyClash` for the open project) and `disconnect` (`disconnectJira`). Key symbols: `load`, `actions`, `ghLogin`, `connectJira`, `disconnectJira`, `checkKeyClash`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/settings/integrations/+page.svelte`: one Logins box: GitHub (signed in as, not signed in with `gh auth login`, or not installed), Jira ("Connected to" the host "as" the email with Edit and Disconnect, or Not connected with Connect), the three fields that Edit, Connect or a refusal opens (`open`, focus to the site box through `edit`), the Disconnect confirm in a `Dialog` that posts the hidden form, with the focus on Keep it (`asking`, `unplug`). No time tracker row: those logins go with Evidence. Tested in `page.test.ts` and `page.mount.test.ts` next to it. The token hint says a new Jira site needs the token again. Key symbols: `edit`, `disconnect`, `saved`, `open`, `asking`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/settings/wall/+page.server.ts`: `/settings/wall`, Wall. The load hands the page the wall's fixed rules in groups for this system (`wallGroups`). No action: nothing on the page saves. Key symbols: `load`, `wallGroups`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/settings/wall/+page.svelte`: Always hidden, one folded row per group (its name, its line and its path count in the `summary`, the paths inside), with a link to the Walls box on Project, then what leaves this machine another way: push and pull request, the timesheet export while `SCOUT_AND_EVIDENCE` is on, sign in while `CLOUD_OPEN` is on, and agent runs. Tested in `page.test.ts` next to it. Key symbols: `groups`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/new/+page.server.ts`: New project, the first run and every later project on a paid plan. The heading says "Make your first project" only while the machine has one registered project (`first`). The load scans the PATH for an engine that runs every phase on this machine (`everyPhaseEngines` over `wallHere`: any of the four where the wall starts, Claude Code or Codex where it cannot) and, with none there and no fake mode, hands the page the install rows (`tools`), that answer (`everyPhase`) and the engines it found (`found`). Each repository of the project being made carries origin's branches (`remoteBranches`) for its base select. Actions `add` (the first one makes the workspace), `github` (Put on GitHub, which also makes the workspace first), `stop`, `remove` (only a repository of the project being made), `base` (a row's base branch, saved as Settings, Project saves it, which makes the local branch of one only origin has), `make` (saves each row's base picked and not saved, sent as `base:<repo>` fields, then needs one repository, writes the key prefix from the name, then the first task). Key symbols: `load`, `actions`, `first`, `registeredProjects`, `addToNewProject`, `putOnGitHub`, `finishNewProject`, `projectInProgress`, `machineRepos`, `ghLogin`, `stopClone`, `enginesOnPath`, `wallHere`, `everyPhaseEngines`, `ENGINE_INSTALLS`, `isFakeMode`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/new/+page.svelte`: the screen with no rail; the logo links back home on a later project before its first Add (`logo`). With no agent tool the main column shows `NoAgentTool` in place of the form until a look finds one. Otherwise the step line "1 of 2", name, Folder with Browse (`FolderBrowser`): the project's own folder, which follows the name until typed (`folderTyped`), with "Goes in" under it (`shownFolder`), the repository rows with the clones from `/api/clones`, each with its base branch select of origin's branches and a Save that shows once the pick differs (`picked`, which Make the project also sends), On this machine with Add or Put on GitHub (a `Skeleton` while it looks, absent when it finds nothing, names on one line, "added" in place of Add for a remote that is a row or a clone already, `taken`), This machine with the gh wait (`gh auth login` with Copy, read again on `/api/gh` until signed in) and no project limit line. The header's Free line ends "· no account" while no verified display document is held. No hint under the name field. Beside Make the project, the link to Start from an idea, which carries the name and a typed folder (`carried`) and reads them back off the address, and the line that a repository is needed while none is listed. Key symbols: `EventSource`, `FolderBrowser`, `engineRows`, `Skeleton`, `machineHead`, `makeForm`, `picked`, `taken`, `waitForGh`, `copyText`, `putting`, `NoAgentTool`, `waiting`, `toolFound`, `folderTyped`, `browsed`, `carried`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/new/task/+page.server.ts`: the first task at `/new/task`, step 2 of 2. The load sends a machine with no project, or one still without a repository, to `/new`, and a finished project to its board, and hands the page the steps of Set up the project (`setupFor`). Action `card` makes the first card (Make card, Ask about the project, or an epic) and opens its board. `key` saves the card key prefix as Settings, Project does (`saveProjectField`), `reveal` opens the folder (`revealProject`), and `skip` ends the first run with no card (`endFirstRun`) and opens Delivery. Key symbols: `load`, `actions`, `firstTask`, `makeFirstCard`, `endFirstRun`, `saveProjectField`, `revealProject`, `setupFor`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/new/task/+page.svelte`: the banner "<name> is ready" with the folder, the repository count and the card keys, whose prefix Change opens in place (`said` reads each banner form's answer, `inPlace` keeps the box), then the engine line with a link to Settings, Models, and the files line with Open the folder. A confetti burst once per project, thrown up and out from the middle of the window for about 2.5 s with the pieces `confettiPieces` picks, in CSS only (`party`, `PIECES`; none with reduced motion). Below, two equal choices: Give it a task (the task box, Make card with the next key and ⌘↵, and "Plan a bigger feature (Epic board)" with its line) and Ask about the project (the read-only question card), then the list of Set up the project (`SetupSteps`) and Skip, go to the board. Nothing amber. Key symbols: `SetupSteps`, `makeForm`, `making`, `pageKeys`, `said`, `inPlace`, `party`, `PIECES`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/new/idea/+page.server.ts`: Start from an idea at `/new/idea`. The load reads the gh login, the owners once gh is signed in, and the estimate of a drag to Refine from the Epics board. Action `create` waits for the push and the clone, then opens `/b/epics`. Key symbols: `load`, `actions`, `startFromIdea`, `githubOwners`, `ghLogin`, `estimateShort`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/new/idea/+page.svelte`: the idea, name, Folder with Browse (as on New project, with the name and folder New project carried, and carried back on "I have repositories already"), "Anything already decided?", the owner menu and repository name with "Available" from `/api/gh/name` once typing pauses, the gh wait, and the four steps of What happens next. Key symbols: `checked`, `repoTyped`, `folderTyped`, `carried`, `waitForGh`, `makeForm`, `creating`, `proposePrefix`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/onboard/+page.server.ts`: the old address of the setup wizard, sent on to `/new`. Key symbols: `load`, `redirect`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/projects/+page.server.ts`: the old address of the project's settings, sent on to `/settings/project` with 308, fragment and all. Key symbols: `load`, `redirect`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/projects/+page.svelte`: never drawn, because the load always redirects.
- `apps/local/src/routes/knowledge/+page.server.ts`: `/knowledge`. Actions `approve` (the Apply button), `approveAll` (Apply all), `return`, `revert`, `optimize`, `learn` and `answer` (Learn this project and its question, `startLearnPost`, `answerLearnPost`), `learnRepos` (Learn your repositories on the ticked repositories, through `startLearnRepos`, whose start marks that step of Set up the project done), and `review`, the switch at the foot of the screen, which writes the same setting as Settings, Runs through `postKnowledge`. Busy state comes from the snapshot (`knowledgeLane`). The load hands the page the view not awaited, because the folder's dates and each change's patch come from git, the kept copy of a waiting change as Apply would write it (`readHeldNow`), the review setting (`knowledgeFor`), the question Learn this project waits on (`learnAsking`) and what Learn your repositories offers (`learnReposOffer`). Key symbols: `load`, `actions`, `knowledgePage`, `knowledgeLane`, `answerApprove`, `answerReturn`, `answerRevert`, `answerOptimize`, `startLearnPost`, `answerLearnPost`, `learnReposOffer`, `learnAsking`, `readHeldNow`, `knowledgeFor`, `postKnowledge`, `startLearnRepos`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/knowledge/+page.svelte`: reads the page again when the rail's Now stream says the knowledge lane changed hands (`holder`), posts Apply, Apply all and Return in place (`onpost`), and draws `KnowledgeBrowser` with the review switch inside `Streamed` (until the view lands, the browser itself with no view: the header and Search, grey shapes in the panes), and hands the layout's key handler "/" for Search knowledge (`pageKeys`, `focusSearch`). Key symbols: `KnowledgeBrowser`, `pageKeys`, `focusSearch`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/workspace/+page.svelte`: `/workspace`, with no load of its own, so opening it never waits on git. Draws `WorkspacePage` with the four fetches to `/api/workspace/*` and calls `invalidateAll` after every read, so the layout runs again and the rail count follows. Key symbols: `WorkspacePage`, `send`, `invalidateAll`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/evidence/+page.server.ts`: `/evidence`, the day timeline with the phase of each run block (`runPhases`), and on `?range=week` the seven days of the day's week summed (`period`, `weekEvidence`), the week timesheet (the day's own week when the link names none), the standup, the server's clock for the now line, whether this is the first visit that asks "Bill this project?" (`billingPrompt`, which records the visit), and the client and rate. Actions `export`, `exporter`, `bill` (the three billing fields together, on every plan; the prompt and `#billing` both post it). Key symbols: `load`, `actions`, `evidenceFor`, `weekEvidence`, `runPhases`, `timesheetFor`, `standupFor`, `exportWeek`, `canExport`, `billingFor`, `billingPrompt`, `saveBilling`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/evidence/+page.svelte`: the Evidence screen. The top bar: the Day | Week links (`weekView`; Week is locked on Free like the arrow back), the day or the week with its arrows (`dayShift`, `dayLabel`, `weekLabel`), locked backwards on Free with the one Solo line "History is part of Solo" read off the display document (`HISTORY_FEATURE`), `DatePick` on Solo, and Copy standup, which presses the box's own copy (`composer`). Then the stat tiles (`dayStats`) and `EvidenceDay` of the day or the summed week (`shown`), with each lane's dot off the Now stream (`laneStates`, `nowLive`), and two columns: the plain Standup heading with Copy, and `StandupComposer`, new boxes per day; the "Bill this project?" prompt on the first visit (`prompt`, hidden by Not now through `notNow`), `TimesheetWeek` with the week links, `TimesheetExport`, and after the first visit the same three fields folded in a `details` at `#billing`. Key symbols: `shows`, `EXPORT_FEATURE`, `HISTORY_FEATURE`, `weekView`, `shown`, `billed`, `billForm`, `prompt`, `notNow`, `composer`, `dayStats`, `laneStates`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/scout/+page.server.ts`: `/scout`, the Scout page with the finding seen last on show (`scoutPage`). Holds the four finding writers, the source actions that left Settings (`scout`, `check`, `checkTickets`, `checkSentry`, `checkLogs`, `schedule`, which no screen draws while the cloud schedule is hidden), `test` (Test the connection, `testConnection`), `firstPaste` (the first visit's paste box, `checkFirstPaste`) and `checkAll` (Check all sources, `checkAllSources`, which the palette presses too). An add posted with `check=now`, the dialog's "Add and check now", reads the new source once (`saveAndCheck`). `/scout/f/[id]` re-exports these actions. Key symbols: `load`, `actions`, `scoutActions`, `scoutPage`, `checkFirstPaste`, `checkAllSources`, `saveAndCheck`, `testConnection`, `saveScoutSchedule`, `hubDeps`, `afterCheck`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/scout/+page.svelte`: draws `ScoutPage` with the finding the load put on show. Key symbols: `ScoutPage`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/scout/f/[id]/+page.server.ts`: the Scout page with one finding selected, 404 for an id no finding has. Every action is the Scout page's. Key symbols: `load`, `actions`, `scoutFindingDetail`, `scoutPage`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/scout/f/[id]/+page.svelte`: draws `ScoutPage` with this finding selected, titled by its id and never its line. Key symbols: `ScoutPage`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/team/+page.ts`: `/team`, 404 without the team feature. Key symbols: `load`, `webTeamHref`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/team/+page.svelte`: links to the team screens on the web app.
- `apps/local/src/routes/f/[...path]/+page.svelte`: a route a feature package registered. Key symbols: `FeaturePage`.

### `/api` routes

Board writes and streams. Each route is a thin wrapper over `apps/local/src/routes/api/board/writes.ts` or `stream.ts`.

- `apps/local/src/routes/api/board/writes.ts`: every board write, over the loop. `moveTo` is the one move writer: a drag, Add and start, a hub `card.move` job and the chain all call it. Answers 409 with core's sentence on a refusal, 500 when the move committed and the run did not start. A move may carry one line of `reason` (Accept anyway on a QA FAIL), kept on the move for the trail; a blank one is refused with a sentence, and one over `REASON_LIMIT` too. It refuses a move into QA while the project has not answered the question at the first QA (`stackAnswered`), so no sender starts QA unasked, and a forward move that starts a phase while the phase that ran last has a `blocks: yes` question with no choice yet (`waitsOn`), unless it runs that same phase again, and the first run a person starts while Learn your repositories holds it back (`learnFirst`). A Delivery card with no linked pull request does not go from Review to Pull request or from Pull request to Wrap-up while `unsentWork` counts files not committed or commits not pushed (`unsentRefusal`). `moveBackWithNote` is Send back from the web or the phone (a hub `card.move` with a note): a move back with the note as its reason, which starts nothing; a blank note is a plain move back, and a note on a forward move or over `REASON_LIMIT` is refused. Key symbols: `move`, `REASON_LIMIT`, `stopThenMove`, `moveTo`, `moveBackWithNote`, `minimize`, `stop`, `promote`, `answer`, `retry`, `fix`, `sendBack`, `triage`, `fixFeedback`, `restarted`, `stackAnswered`, `answerFirst`, `learnFirst`. `fixFeedback` answers 400 with no ref, 409 with the loop's sentence, and 500 when the start failed, whose reason is on the row.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/board/stream.ts`: the server sent event stream. Each unnamed event is a whole snapshot with `id: <version>`, then named `activity` and `limits` events and a heartbeat. `events` is shared with `/api/now`. Key symbols: `stream`, `events`, `HEARTBEAT_MS`, `LIMITS_EVENT`, `ACTIVITY_EVENT`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/board/+server.ts`: `GET ?board=<kind>`, one board's stream. Used by the card page. Key symbols: `GET`, `stream`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/board/move/+server.ts`: `POST { key, to, stop?, reason? }`. The four board writes and the card writes Now sends (answer, fix, retry, send back) take `?project=` through `loopOf`, so an item of another project acts on that project's card. Key symbols: `POST`, `move`, `loopOf`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/board/stop/+server.ts`: `POST { runId }`. Key symbols: `POST`, `stop`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/board/promote/+server.ts`: `POST { runId }`, moves a waiting job to the front. Key symbols: `POST`, `promote`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/board/minimize/+server.ts`: `POST { key, minimized }`. Key symbols: `POST`, `minimize`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/board/pulls/+server.ts`: reads open pull requests for the PR reviews board, or PR watch state for Delivery with `?board=delivery`. Key symbols: `POST`, `postPullRefresh`, `postDeliveryPulls`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/now/+server.ts`: `GET`, the Now stream: `events` over `nowSnapshot`, every board that is on in one event, of the current project only, with the project it serves and `switched` once another project became current. The layout opens it once. Key symbols: `GET`, `events`, `nowSnapshot`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/chain/+server.ts`: `POST`, turn the chain on or off for a board, or Not now. Key symbols: `POST`, `writeChain`.

Cards.

- `apps/local/src/routes/api/cards/+server.ts`: `POST`, Add card. Hands `moveTo` in as the start, so Add and start has the drop's rules. A new project's first card made here ends its first run too (`endFirstRun`); its project files are there since the workspace was made. Key symbols: `POST`, `postCard`, `moveTo`, `endFirstRun`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/cards/next/+server.ts`: `GET ?board=`, the key the next local card on that board gets, a Research kind under its own prefix. Key symbols: `GET`, `nextCardKey`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/cards/context/+server.ts`: `GET`, cards whose extra context Add card can copy. Key symbols: `GET`, `contextSources`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/tickets/jira/+server.ts`: `POST { site, email, token }`, Connect Jira in Add card. The token goes to this data folder's keychain, the project reads Jira from then on and its key prefix is checked (`checkKeyClash`), and the answer is a sentence or nothing. Key symbols: `POST`, `postJira`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/card/[key]/answer/+server.ts`: `POST { answer }`, resumes a blocked card. Key symbols: `POST`, `answer`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/card/[key]/fix/+server.ts`: `POST`, Fix it on a QA FAIL: back to Implement, then QA once Implement ends ready. 409 with the loop's sentence when there is no QA FAIL. Key symbols: `POST`, `fix`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/card/[key]/fix-ci/+server.ts`: `POST`, Fix CI on a card whose pull request has failed checks: the failed jobs' logs into `ci/` in the card folder, then back to Implement with no QA after (`fixCi` in `writes.ts`, `Loop.fixCi`). 409 with the loop's sentence when the card is busy, has no failed check or no readable log, or gh cannot read. Key symbols: `POST`, `fixCi`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/card/[key]/send-back/+server.ts`: `POST`, Send back with comments: back to Implement with every open diff comment. 409 with the loop's sentence when there is none open or the card cannot go back. Key symbols: `POST`, `sendBack`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/card/[key]/retry/+server.ts`: `POST`, Retry, or "Run on" with `{ engine }`. Key symbols: `POST`, `retry`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/card/[key]/thread/+server.ts`: `PATCH { thread, subject }`, renames an Ask discussion, which marks the name the person's. Key symbols: `PATCH`, `renameThread`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/card/[key]/message/+server.ts`: `POST`, a discussion message, which resumes that phase's session, or in an ask discussion starts or resumes its read-only session. Key symbols: `POST`, `sendMessage`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/card/[key]/models/+server.ts`: `PATCH`, the card's own model rows: `{ action, engine, model }` together, `{ action, effort }`, `{ action, reset: true }`, or `{ preset }`. Calls `changed` so open boards redraw. Key symbols: `PATCH`, `saveCardModels`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/card/[key]/repos/+server.ts`: `PATCH`, the card's repository list. Key symbols: `PATCH`, `saveCardRepos`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/card/[key]/chain/+server.ts`: `PATCH`, the card's tick "Go on to Implement after Plan". Key symbols: `PATCH`, `saveCardChain`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/card/[key]/context/+server.ts`: `PATCH { text }`, extra context. Key symbols: `PATCH`, `saveContext`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/card/[key]/choice/+server.ts`: `POST { id, answer? }`, the choice on a question that blocks the next move: no answer takes the default. Key symbols: `POST`, `settleQuestion`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/card/[key]/link/[other]/+server.ts`: `DELETE`, unlink two cards. Key symbols: `DELETE`, `unlink`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/card/[key]/research/+server.ts`: `POST { action }`, Make a Delivery card from a Research card (`deliver`). Key symbols: `POST`, `researchAction`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/card/[key]/tasks/+server.ts`: `POST { titles, keys?, descriptions?, steps?, refs?, notes? }`, an epic's Delivery tasks, each the card of the ticket its row linked, or under the key its row sent or the next local key. Key symbols: `POST`, `postTasks`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/tickets/read/+server.ts`: `GET ?ref=`, Link on an epic's task row: the ticket's key and its own title, or the sentence it was refused with. Writes nothing. Key symbols: `GET`, `getTicket`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/tickets/mine/+server.ts`: `GET`, Your tickets in Add card: the tickets assigned to the person with no card yet, and a sentence for each source that failed. Writes nothing. Key symbols: `GET`, `myTickets`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/card/[key]/peek/+server.ts`: `GET`, what the peek sheet shows. A finished run's plain summary marks Getting started's "Read what changed". Key symbols: `GET`, `peekFor`, `markStep`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/card/[key]/file/+server.ts`: `GET ?path=`, one file of the card folder, and with `&download=1` the Files tab's Download. A path outside the card folder is refused with a sentence. Key symbols: `GET`, `fileResponse`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/card/[key]/files/[...path]/+server.ts`: the same file at an address shaped like its path, which the Files tab shows and frames, so a design screen's relative link to `tokens.css` lands on the file beside it. Same reader, jail and `FILE_POLICY`. Key symbols: `GET`, `fileResponse`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/card/[key]/log/+server.ts`: `GET`, a run's events, parsed with the engine its row names, one JSON object per line. Key symbols: `GET`, `logResponse`, `parseRunLine`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/card/[key]/log/raw/+server.ts`: `GET ?run=`, Download raw, the run's own log file. Key symbols: `GET`, `rawLog`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/card/[key]/patch/+server.ts`: `GET`, one file's patch for the Changes tab, `group=branch` for the base to the folder Whole card draws, `uncommitted` for Not committed, and `commit` with `commit=<id>` for one commit of the list. Key symbols: `GET`, `patchFor`, `patchAsk`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/card/[key]/commit-diff/+server.ts`: `GET ?commit=&repo=`, the files one commit of the card's branch changed, for the commit list of Not committed. Key symbols: `GET`, `commitChanges`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/card/[key]/comments/+server.ts`: `POST { repo, file, line, lineText, text }`, a comment on one line of the card's diff. 422 with a sentence for a repository the card does not work in or a file its change does not touch. Key symbols: `POST`, `addComment`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/card/[key]/comments/[id]/+server.ts`: `PATCH { text }` and `DELETE`, one of the card's comments, 404 for an id of another card. An edit of a comment sent back already is 409. Key symbols: `PATCH`, `DELETE`, `editComment`, `deleteComment`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/card/[key]/commit/+server.ts`: `POST`, commit in the card's checkout. A direct git action, not a run. Key symbols: `POST`, `commitFor`, `commitAsk`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/card/[key]/commit-message/+server.ts`: `POST`, asks the engine for a message (`Loop.writeMessage`), with the model and the cost the pass reported. Key symbols: `POST`, `messageFor`, `messageAsk`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/card/[key]/push/+server.ts`: `POST { confirmed, repo }`, and with `{ message, include }` a commit before the push (`commitAndPush`), which the Changes tab no longer sends. A push that went tells the loop, because Getting started counts it, and asks the loop to look for the card's pull request at once (`watchPulls`). Key symbols: `POST`, `pushFor`, `commitAndPush`, `commitAsk`, `changed`, `watchPulls`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/card/[key]/pull/+server.ts`: `POST`, pull the branch into the checkout after a rejected push. Key symbols: `POST`, `pullFor`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/card/[key]/stack/+server.ts`: `POST { action }`, Start, Stop and Force on the App line (`load`, `unload`, `force`). Key symbols: `POST`, `pressStack`, `isStackAction`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/card/[key]/branch/+server.ts`: `POST { name }`, renames the card branch before a push (`renameCardBranch`), 422 with the reason. Key symbols: `POST`, `renameCardBranch`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/card/[key]/checkouts/+server.ts`: `POST`, removes a closed card's checkouts. Key symbols: `POST`, `removeCardCheckouts`, `checkoutsSaid`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/card/[key]/delete/+server.ts`: Delete card. `GET` is the confirm's check, read now, and `POST { folder? }` deletes. Key symbols: `GET`, `POST`, `deleteCheck`, `deleteCard`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/card/[key]/pr/+server.ts`: `GET`, a closed card's pull request state, read through gh on each ask and written nowhere. Key symbols: `GET`, `cardPulls`, `ghRunner`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/card/[key]/pr/links/+server.ts`: `POST { link }` attaches a pull request to a Delivery card from Pull request on, by its link or owner/repo#N, read through `getPull` first, and `DELETE { link }` detaches one a person attached. Key symbols: `POST`, `DELETE`, `postPullLink`, `deletePullLink`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/card/[key]/pr/triage/+server.ts`: `POST`, Run triage and Triage again, which start the feedback run (`triage` in `writes.ts`). Key symbols: `POST`, `triage`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/card/[key]/pr/fix/+server.ts`: `POST` `{ ref }`, Fix and Fix again on a PRs tab row (`fixFeedback` in `writes.ts`). Key symbols: `POST`, `fixFeedback`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/card/[key]/pr/discuss/+server.ts`: `POST` `{ ref }`, Discuss on a PRs tab row: opens the thread under the ref (`openDiscussion`) and answers it, or 404 for a ref the card does not have. Starts no run. Key symbols: `POST`, `openDiscussion`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/card/[key]/pr/seen/+server.ts`: `POST`, which the PRs tab sends as it opens, marks every message of the card seen. Key symbols: `POST`, `postDiscussionsSeen`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/card/[key]/pr/discussions/+server.ts`: `PATCH { ref, action }`, Mark done (`done`) or Reopen and Needs action (`reopen`) on one discussion, kept locally. Key symbols: `PATCH`, `patchDiscussion`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/card/[key]/pr/comments/+server.ts`: `POST`, "Read comments" on a Delivery card, which reads and stores its pull request discussions. Key symbols: `POST`, `postCardDiscussions`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/card/[key]/knowledge/+server.ts`: `POST { change }`, Revert on a closed card: the Knowledge screen's revert, for a change this card made only. Key symbols: `POST`, `revertKnowledge`, `knowledgeLane`.

Everything else.

- `apps/local/src/routes/api/workspace/+server.ts`: `GET`, the Workspace page's status, read when the page opens and on Check again, never on a timer. Key symbols: `GET`, `workspaceStatus`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/workspace/patch/+server.ts`: `GET ?repo=&path=`, one changed file's patch, the path checked against the folder's status. Key symbols: `GET`, `workspacePatch`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/workspace/commit/+server.ts`: `POST { repo, message, paths, hooks }`, commit the ticked files of one folder, answered with the status read after it. Key symbols: `POST`, `workspaceCommit`, `workspaceCommitAsk`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/workspace/push/+server.ts`: `POST { repo, confirmed, hooks }`, push one folder's branch to its upstream, answered with the status read after it. Key symbols: `POST`, `workspacePush`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/evidence/timesheet/+server.ts`: `GET ?week=`, the week as CSV. Key symbols: `GET`, `timesheetFor`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/remove-check/+server.ts`: `GET ?repo=<folder>`, the Remove confirm of one repository with its checks run now, which the Remove dialog asks for as it opens. Reads only. Key symbols: `GET`, `removeConfirmNow`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/projects/+server.ts`: `GET`, the account's projects for the paid panel. Key symbols: `GET`, `projectList`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/current-project/+server.ts`: `POST` a form with `project`, the selector's pick: opens its loop first and answers 409 with the reason when it cannot open, then writes the machine key `currentProject` and answers 303 to Now, so the whole page loads again on that project and its Now stream. It tells the old project's loop too (`changed`), so a tab still open on the old project reads `switched` and loads again. Never locked, so Free can change its one open project. Key symbols: `POST`, `currentProject`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/package/entry/+server.ts`: `GET`, the installed paid package as a module, or an empty module. Key symbols: `GET`, `installedEntry`, `EMPTY`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/health/+server.ts`: `GET`, the data folder this server serves. A second `nolzu` asks it. Key symbols: `GET`, `resolveDataDir`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/clones/+server.ts`: `GET`, the project's clones as an event stream for New project: each event is the whole list of `CloneJob`s. Key symbols: `GET`, `cloneStream`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/tools/+server.ts`: `GET`, which engines have their binary on the PATH the engines run with. A file check, no run; New project's no-tool block asks it every two seconds. Key symbols: `GET`, `enginesOnPath`, `loginShellPath`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/gh/+server.ts`: `GET`, the gh login New project reads every two seconds while gh is missing or signed out. Key symbols: `GET`, `ghLogin`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/gh/name/+server.ts`: `GET ?owner=&name=`, why GitHub would refuse that repository, or null when the name is free (`gh repo view`, read only). Key symbols: `GET`, `repoNameProblem`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/folders/+server.ts`: `GET ?path=`, Browse on New project: the folder names inside one folder under the home folder. Read only. Key symbols: `GET`, `listFolders`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/notes/+server.ts`: `POST`, Got it on a coach note, and Not now on the start-at-login offer. Key symbols: `POST`, `closeNote`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/setup/+server.ts`: Set up the project. `GET`, what the Learn dialog shows before a press (`learnGateOffer`). `POST { step, mark }` stores a step's mark, and `POST { then: { key, to } }` is Learn and then start, whose held move the loop makes through `moveTo` (`postSetup`, `followLearn`). Key symbols: `GET`, `POST`, `learnGateOffer`, `postSetup`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/qa-setup/+server.ts`: `GET ?key=`, what the question shows for one card: the card's first repository with no answer, read from the card's copy too. `POST { key, choice, address, browser? }`, Start QA in the question at the first QA. Saves the answer and tells the streams; the page then sends the move it held back. Key symbols: `GET`, `POST`, `qaSetupFor`, `answerQa`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/login-item/+server.ts`: `POST`, Turn on under "Start Nolzu when you log in?" (`{}`) and the switch in Settings, Runs (`{ on }`). The one place the login item is made or removed. Key symbols: `POST`, `turnOnAtLogin`, `turnOffAtLogin`, `systemLoginItem`.

### `lib`: the run loop

- `apps/local/src/lib/loop.ts`: one `Loop` per project, cached in `open` by the data folder and the project (`options.project`, a registry id; left out, the first project, which keeps the data folder's key also when named by its id, through `firstIds`). Owns the project's database connection, its registry id (`Loop.project`), its view of the shared scheduler, the listeners and the in memory activity. Its run files go to `projectFiles`: the data folder for the first project. `loop(env, project?)` is what routes call: the first project's loop opens first, then the loop of the project `chosenProject` picks; a current project that cannot open falls back to the first one, which becomes current, with a line on stderr. `loopOf(url)` is the loop of the project a write's `?project=` names, refused with a sentence (400 or 409) for no project or another project on Free. `createLoop(dir, env, options)` is what tests call; `options.wall` pins the wall probe (`wallNow`), which `buildJob`, where a Codex run starts and the snapshot all read. `adapter` hands every real engine's spawn the job with the project's `wallLists` on it, read at spawn time. Its `snapshot` hands core `planSteps`, so an epic in In delivery counts the steps of its plan not added yet. `buildJob` reads the project's instructions for the phase off the project folder, never the card's checkout (`readPhaseInstructions`), and hands them to the prompt. It names the card folder files the earlier phases wrote (`readFirstPaths`), and for Plan the project's Implement and QA instruction files that exist, on `Job.readFiles` too. It hands the prompt what the person said on the card (`personsWords`), with the `qaApp` answer and the allowed commands for a run with a shell (a list never filled is filled there from the stacks, `fillAllowedCommands`), and the wrap-up the card's history (`cardHistory`). It builds the phase method for every engine with `phaseInstructions`, taking a skill file the person edited in one of the project's skills folders (`personsSkillEdit`), and refuses a phase whose skill it cannot read. Every run that ends has the tool lines taken out of the shared files first (`stripSharedAttribution`), and one that ended ready gets the output check (`checkRunOutputs`): the phase folder's Markdown files, the paths `checkedPaths` names and, for a knowledge run, each note it added or changed as `knowledge/<path>` (`writtenNotes`, read before a hold puts the folder back, through `readCardText` so no link is followed and a pipe answers at once) go to `outputCheck`, and the result is stored with `saveOutputCheck`, naming the project's instruction file for the phase when there is one. `triage` is Run triage on the PRs tab: the feedback phase on the card where it sits, refused off Delivery, while a run or a question holds the card, or with no open discussion; the run writes the comments file before the engine (`writeFeedbackFile`) and applies the triage file when it ends ready (`applyTriageFile`), noting a refusal in the run log. A triage is a side run: it holds the card while it goes, puts back the state the card had when it ends, whatever it ended with (`triageFrom`, `triageBack`), and gets no plain summary and no chain, so it never reads as a stage run waiting for approval. `retry` refuses a card whose failed run is a triage (one that could not start, or outlived a restart), which Run triage starts again, because Retry starts the stage. `fixFeedback` is Fix on a PRs tab row: refused off Delivery, while a run or a question holds the card, on a done card, and by `beginFix` for a done row or while any row of the card is fixing; the row goes fixing, the newest implement session goes on (a fresh run with none) with `fixFeedbackMessage`, on the column where the card sits, and the run row takes the ref in `thread`. It is a side run as a triage is (`fixes`, `fixOf`): when it ends the card is back in its state, with no chain, and `endFix` sets the row fixed with the first line of the plain summary (`fixOutcome`) or new with the reason; a stop while it waits and a start that failed do the same (`fixEnded`). A fix that ends blocked with nothing moved keeps the row fixing and lands the card blocked, its question in `fixes` as `asked`; `answer` goes on with it as the same fix (the new run takes the ref and the `fixes` entry; after a restart dropped the entry, it takes the ref alone and lands as a plain run), and `dropUnanswered`, called by `changed`, sets the row new with the question once the card leaves blocked any other way. Right before the engine starts any Implement run, a fix or a resumed run too, `startJob` reads each card checkout's HEAD and upstream (`headsAtStart`, `readHeads`), and at its end, however it ended, a move or an unreadable checkout writes `runMoved`'s sentence to the run log. A fix then sets its row new with it; any other Implement run ends as it would and keeps it on the run (`saveCommitWarning`) for the card page. `discuss` sends a message in a PR comment thread (`isFeedbackRef`) to `ask`, whose new session opens with the discussion Discuss stored first. `watchPulls` looks for those pull requests every `PULL_WATCH_MS` (3 minutes) for each card once per interval, or at once for one card after its push, quietly and never in fake mode without an injected gh; `close` stops its timer. `readBefore` names the repositories of the read-only check with `recordedRepos`; every read and write it makes in a card folder goes through the card folder helpers of `card-files.ts`, and the knowledge sweep runs through `sweptAfter`. `comparedAround` neither puts back nor marks when another run of the card is queued or running at the start, or at the end is queued, running or ended since this run's start (`otherRunOfCard` with `since`), and a clean compare of a run that is no pass clears the card's earlier marks (`clearReadOnlyBreaks` in `markBreak`). The same interval reads the linked open pull requests again (`rereadOpenPulls`), once per interval, so why each cannot merge stays fresh. `fixCi` is Fix CI: refused off Delivery and by `goesBack`, the guard it shares with `sendBack` (a busy card, one before Implement or on the last column), then with no gh in fake mode; it saves the failed jobs' logs into the card folder (`saveCiLogs`) and goes back to Implement through `backToImplement` with no QA after. Key symbols: `Loop`, `watchPulls`, `PULL_WATCH_MS`, `triage`, `triageFrom`, `triageBack`, `fixFeedback`, `fixes`, `fixOf`, `fixEnded`, `fixOutcome`, `dropUnanswered`, `loop`, `createLoop`, `LoopOptions`, `planSteps`, `buildJob`, `start`, `restart`, `answer`, `discuss`, `retry`, `fix`, `sendBack`, `fixCi`, `goesBack`, `backToImplement`, `fixing`, `optimize`, `assess`, `ask`, `answerIn`, `readsInPlace`, `readsUnlisted`, `stopRun`, `shutdown`, `writeMessage`, `failStart`, `landCard`, `chainOn`, `chaining`, `keepNamedRepos`, `takeRefinedTitle`, `renameAfterRefine`, `writtenSince`, `openKnowledge`, `engineSettings`, `markStack`, `heard`, `changed`, `checkRunOutputs`, `writtenNotes`, `nolzuWrites` (what a read-only run's compare leaves out as Nolzu's own writes: card folders, checkouts, knowledge, `.nolzu/`, and the `.cursor/mcp.json` merged at the project folder with its `.gitignore`, unless the repository tracks them), `startWriting`, `sayCutOff`, `schedulers`, `firstIds`, `chosenProject`, `loopOf`.

What each function in `loop.ts` does:

  - `build` wires everything once. `boardNow` and `boardOf` read a board, `profilesNow` the engine profiles, `adapter` and `engineFor` build the engine of a profile (the one shared fake in fake mode), `offered` lists models per vendor, `modelsFor` resolves card over project over the card's preset (`cardPreset`) with `resolveModels`.
  - `buildJob` is the one gate every run passes: missing profile, `vendorRefusal`, gone model, plain key, no project, knowledge folder (`knowledgeFolder`), repository list, run guard (the guard file in the project), and the settings file every run gets (`engineSettings`, with attribution off, the hook only where the guard file is). It picks `cwd` (the card checkout, for implement and QA the checkout of the one repository a card's list holds, so commands need no `cd`, the project, or the data folder; `startJob` checks it again against the list, and `ownFolder` keeps own commands under `*` in the checkout root), `qaCopy` for QA on a project (`qaCopyPath`; the prompt then names every path outside the copy by its physical full path with `fullPaths`, so the card folder it names is the real one), `taskDir` (`<project>/.tasks/<key>`), the lane, the tools and the prompt (`buildPrompt`, with the servers of the MCP list the phase is on for; the job itself carries no MCP config). For a task of an epic (`epicOfTask`) it hands the prompt and `Job.epicFiles` the epic's documents that exist.
  - `start` writes the run row (`startRun`), names the log file, and hands the job to `scheduler.add`. A resume with a null session is a fresh run told the message after the card. `restart` sets the card queued first and fails it with the reason on a throw. A card's knowledge run (the wrap-up) while a knowledge change waits is built first with the waiting change left out of the checks (`aheadOfReview`), then gets a run row in `waiting_review` and waits in `waitingReview`; `reviewAnswered`, called after Apply, Apply all and Return on `/knowledge`, after a team approve in `hub-jobs.ts` and on every scheduler change while a card waits, drops the oldest one's row and starts it once nothing waits and no knowledge run is queued (`knowledgeBusy`); `stopRun` ends such a row as stopped, and `stopWaiting` does it for every waiting card at `shutdown`. `answer`, `discuss`, `retry` and `fix` build on `restart`. `fix` is Fix it after a QA FAIL: `moveTo` back to Implement, the newest implement session resumed with `fixMessage` (or a fresh run), and the run noted in `fixing` with the QA stage it goes on to. `sendBack` is Send back with comments on the same path (`backToImplement`, which skips the move when the card sits in Implement): the open diff comments read with `commentsOf`, the message from `sendBackMessage` with each line found again in the checkout (`lineNow`), and `sent_at` written on the comments it took once the run is queued. No QA after it. `fixCi` is Fix CI on the same path, with the failed checks' logs saved into the card folder first (`saveCiLogs`). No QA after it either. `optimize`, `learn` and `assess` start runs that belong to no card (`belongsToNoCard`). `learn` is Learn this project, the `orient` phase under the reserved key: `buildJob` gives it the `LEARN_TITLE` card with `learnAsk` naming the repository it starts with, which travels where Optimize's file does, checked against the list. `answer` on the reserved key goes to `answerLearn`, which resumes the newest run when it ended with a question (`learnAsking`), and builds the job first, so a refusal leaves the question waiting. It keeps the folder state of a run that asked apart from Optimize's (`beforeFile`), and a returned note reaches only the next run of its own phase (`returnedKnowledgeNote`). Its writing is held for review whatever `reviewKnowledge` says, because its policy row says `alwaysHeld`. `learnRepos` is Learn your repositories: one `learn-repo` run per repository, each under its own key (`learnRepoKey`), so the queue takes them all at once and the knowledge lane runs them one after another; every job is built first, so one refusal (a repository not in the list, one queued already, knowledge still waiting for review) starts none. `buildJob` reads the repository off the key, checks it against the list, and hands the prompt its folder (`learnRepo`); its writing is held like Learn this project's.
  - `ask` is a message in an ask discussion, which `discuss` hands on whatever the card is doing. It builds an `ask` job (`buildJob` with `askPrompt`, the ask row, the read-only `ASK_POLICY`, started where `readsInPlace` says; `readsUnlisted` lets Research, Investigate and their wrap-up start the same way on a card with no repository list), writes the run row with the discussion in `runs.thread`, and runs it outside the scheduler in `answerIn` (inside `comparedAround`), which posts the answer (`answerOf`) into the discussion, or why none came (`noAnswer`); a run a restart cut off gets that sentence at the next boot. It never calls `restart`, `markRunning` or `landCard`, so the card's state, stage and queue place stay as they were. A second message waits while the discussion's run goes.
  - `runPass` runs a pass on the plain summary row with its own run row (cost, model, Log tab): `writeMessage` for a commit message, and `titleThread`, which `answerIn` starts after an answer in an Ask discussion with no subject and no subject pass yet (`wantsSubject`), in a scratch folder of the data folder with no git compare (`scratch`). Its `keep` stores the subject before the run's end is announced, and the page reads the card again while `titling`.
  - `comparedAround` wraps every read-only run (a policy with `readOnly`, Ask and the passes; never implement) in the git compare: `gitBefore` on `job.cwd` (for QA the real checkout, not the copy) right before the process starts, `gitAfter` once its last line is in, before a knowledge commit. After a run outside Nolzu's own wall (`wallOf` is neither `macos` nor `bubblewrap`), in a checkout under `worktreesFolder` it puts back what changed; behind a wall, where the run could not write, and in the person's folder it touches nothing and leaves ignored files out, as it does while a QA app or stack runs. `markBreak` saves the mark on the run row (`saveReadOnlyBreak`) and notes it in the run log. `startJob`, the summary pass's `extract`, `answerIn` and `writeMessage` all run through it.
  - `startJob` (the scheduler port) runs one job of this project, which the shared scheduler hands back here by `job.project`: `markRunning`, the checkout (`cardBranch`, `ensureWorktree`), the pull request diff (`writePrDiff`) with the head the last read saw (`seenHead`) and the newest round file (`roundsIn`), the knowledge folder (`openKnowledge`), the QA evidence folder (`qa/evidence/`, made before the engine starts), the QA app start (the `qaApp` answer of each repository of the card (`qaReposOf`, `answerFor`), in order, own commands under `*` first and once in the checkout root (`ownStarts`, `ownFolder`): for a start command, in the card's checkout of that repository (or the folder one level down its answer names), `scriptsChanged` on the card's branch, `installApp` when `installCommand` names one, then an app started with `startApp` inside `appWall`; for own commands, `startApp` with `mayExit` outside the wall with `cardStackEnv`, its named lines or address to `qa/addresses.md`; an address the person runs; or no app), then the QA copy (`makeQaCopy`, with the method and its time noted in the run log), then the run's MCP servers (`runMcp`, each one left out noted in the run log, the servers in memory on `job.mcp`, the prompt's server lines swapped for the ones the run got, Cursor merging only where `cursorMergesHere` says: a run started in QA's copy or under `worktrees/`, whose Cursor root lies inside the copy or the project and does not track `.cursor/mcp.json`), then `runWithRetries` over the engine with `saveLimit` on rate limits. The QA copy goes after the run however it ended (`removeQaCopy`), and so does the run's MCP folder, after `restoreCursorMcp`; the boot of the data folder, under its lock, runs `sweepMcpRuns` for every registered project, and every loop then moves the old QA browser setting into the list (`migrateQaBrowser`). After it: knowledge commit or hold, `releaseHeld` (a hand Start of the card), each app stopped and then its Stop run (`runStackHook`), `foldRun`, QA `extractVerdict`, `keepNamedRepos`, a review's new round and the head it read (`savePullRound`), `takeRefinedTitle` (a local card takes the title on the first heading of `refined.md` a refine that completed wrote), `renameAfterRefine` (the checkout's branch takes the template's name once Refine wrote its `Kind:` and `Branch:` lines, through `renameFreshBranch`; a name the person typed is what `templateBranch` gives, so it stays), `saveQuestionOptions`, `saveRun`, `saveVerdict`, `recordSummary`, `recordPhaseSummary`, `landCard`.
  - `onChange` (the scheduler port, told on a change in any project's runs, since slots and places are shared) bumps the board version, tells the listeners (`changed`), and asks `chainOn` for each card in `chaining` that has left the queue. A run `fixing` names that ended ready carries its QA stage as `then`, and `chainOn` moves the card there through `moveTo` with no opt-in, because the Fix it press asked for it. A stop, a failure or a question never reaches `chaining`, so it ends Fix it there.
  - `stopRun` aborts a running job, or closes the row of a waiting one itself. `shutdown` is Ctrl+C for every project, through the shared scheduler: no slot left, each project's `stopRun` for its jobs in the queue, then it resolves once no job runs, so each stop is written before the process leaves. An Ask run and a commit message pass are outside the queue, so each project's `leaving` aborts them, and it waits for each Ask run and each commit message pass in `asking`. `writeMessage` runs a commit message pass outside the scheduler.
  - `onChange`, `onActivity`, `activity` and `listeners` on the returned object are what streams and the hub link subscribe to. `close` closes the database and drops the cache entry.
  - The boot, last in `createLoop`: `startWriting` takes the writer lock in `machine.db` and sweeps this project, and `sayCutOff` tells each Ask run the sweep closed why in its discussion. The boot of the data folder also deletes the QA copies a crash left (`sweepQaCopies`, in the data folder and in each project's `qaCopyRoot`). The loop that makes the shared scheduler (none in `schedulers` yet) is the boot of the data folder, so it sweeps every other registered project the same way. A later loop sweeps only its own project, never one whose runs are going. A project whose database cannot open is skipped, and its own loop says why when it opens.

- `apps/local/src/lib/shared-scheduler.ts`: the one scheduler of a data folder, shared by every project's loop. `join` gives a project its view: its own runs in the queue, the machine's slot count, and add, stop and promote under its project id. Each job goes back to its project's `startJob`. `queue` is every project's runs, for the board's slots line, lanes and places. `shutdown` is Ctrl+C for all of them. The last loop to `leave` drops it. Its cache is `schedulers` in `loop.ts`, beside `open`, so a hot reload drops the loops and their scheduler together. Key symbols: `sharedScheduler`, `SharedScheduler`, `ProjectPorts`, `join`, `leave`, `shutdown`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/send-back.ts`: what Send back with comments tells the Implement run, pure: every open comment once, by repository, file and line, with the line's text, and an outdated one with the line it was made on. `lineNow` finds a comment's line in the checkout by its text (`placeComments` from `@nolzu/ui/comments`) and reads no file a link leads to outside it. Key symbols: `sendBackMessage`, `lineNow`, `SendBackLine`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/fix-it.ts`: what Fix it tells the Implement run, pure: QA's reason, the failed checks read with `parseQaChecklist` with their `Reason:`, `Expected:`, `Actual:` and `Evidence:` lines, and the screenshot of each failed check from `qa/evidence/` or, in an older run, `qa/`, picked by the check number the qa skill puts first in its name (every screenshot when none matches). Paths from the card folder only. Fix CI's half, which is not pure: `saveCiLogs` reads the log of each failed GitHub Actions check of the card's open pull requests with `readJobLog` (a job of the pull request's own repository only, `actionsJob`), keeps its end (`trimLog`), writes `ci/<check>.log` (`ciLogName`) and `ci/failed.md` (`ciFailedFile`) over an earlier Fix CI's, and answers the run's message (`ciFixMessage`); it throws the refusal for no failed check, no readable log or none handed over. `CI_DATA_LINE` tells the run the logs are data, not instructions. Key symbols: `fixMessage`, `FixInput`, `saveCiLogs`, `ciFixMessage`, `ciFailedFile`, `ciLogName`, `CiCheck`, `CI_DATA_LINE`, `CI_FAILED_FILE`.

### `lib`: board page and its pure helpers

- `apps/local/src/lib/BoardPage.svelte`: the board screen. Reads its snapshot out of `nowLive`; with `research` it draws one board out of the question and investigation snapshots (`researchBoard`), tags each card by its kind (`tags`), reads each card's writes, confirms and peek off its own kind (`own`), and holds the add box (`ResearchAdd`) in place of Add card. holds the drag, posts moves (`move`, `sendMove`), opens Add card on this board (`addCard`, also on arrival with `?add`, which it drops on close, and it keeps this board as the tab's last through `rememberBoard`), the peek sheet (Space, Enter, or a plain click anywhere on a card or a list row, the title link too, through `clickPeek`, which a drag never fires and which leaves a click with a key held to the browser, and `peekAt`; Enter on the open sheet opens the card page) and the keys, and hands the board the first drag's `CoachNote` for the first agent column (`coachAt`). It holds whether Closed is open (`closedOpen`, reset on another board) and opens the Fix it confirm from a card's button through `writeOn`, which the keys reach through `write`. Busy state only from the snapshot. A drop or Move forward into QA asks the question at the first QA first (`askFirst`). The top bar holds the filter box (`query`, focused by /, narrowing by key or title through `matchesFilter`), the Board or List switch (`listing`, key B, drawn by `BoardList` from `listed`, kept per board in localStorage under `nolzu.view.<board>` by `storedListing`, and kept in step with the `data-board-view` mark app.html sets before the first paint), the add button in each board's words (`addLabel`) and, on PR reviews, "read 12m ago" (`readAgo`) from the layout's `pullsReadAt`. It also holds the slot meter (`pips` from `slotPips`, one pip per slot, a busy one naming its run on hover and focus, and `openSlot` peeking at a card on screen or opening its page). Key symbols: `clickPeek`, `peekAt`, `pips`, `openSlot`, `query`, `listing`, `storedListing`, `listed`, `addLabel`, `readAgo`, `move`, `addCard`, `allowedFor`, `sendMove`, `addOrHold`, `refusedDrop`, `startDrag`, `follow`, `toastStack`, `coachAt`, `closeCoach`, `askFirst`, `closedOpen`, `write`, `writeOn`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/BoardList.svelte`: the board's List view: the cards the page hands in as rows (key, title with the kind tag on Research, column, pill, model), each an `article[data-card]` so the board's keys reach it. Key symbols: `cards`, `tags`, `focusKey`, `empty`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/BoardOff.svelte`: what a board the project turned off shows, Research among them. Key symbols: `kind`, `choiceLabel`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/board-page.ts`: pure helpers of the board page. `allowedFor` asks core's `canMove`, so the board never offers a drop the server refuses. The move, Add card and toast helpers. R on a QA FAIL is Fix it: `keyWrite` asks or refuses for both runs, and `confirmFix` gives the confirm its two runs. `openCount` counts the cards not in an archive column for the board title and the rail, and `countLine` words it. `boardCount` adds the Delivery tasks of the open epics on Epics, and on PR reviews says how many are open and from which repositories, off the `repo#n` keys. `cardDeleted` is Delete card's toast, with what stayed under it. - `apps/local/src/lib/board-keys.ts`: the W key and Esc filter toggle, and the theme key. Key symbols: `toggleFilter`, `toggleTheme`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/snapshot-client.ts`: the browser side of the stream. Keeps the last whole snapshot, the activity and the limits, and reconnects with a doubling wait. A connection starts with `NO_LIMITS`, so a reader tells it from a limits event with no reading. Key symbols: `connectBoard`, `browserStream`, `BoardState`, `isBoard`, `LIMITS_EVENT`, `ACTIVITY_EVENT`, `NO_LIMITS`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/now-live.svelte.ts`: the one shared state the Now stream writes. The rail, Now and every board read it. Key symbols: `nowLive`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/follow.svelte.ts`: the card a key or the palette moved last, so the page selects it in its new column. Key symbols: `follow`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/peek.ts`: the rows the peek sheet shows, read fresh per ask, a result with its size, each with the time of day the runner noted for its line (`clockTime`), and for the Next step panel the open diff comments and the newest Implement session (`openComments`, `session`, the card page's own `openCommentCount` and `implementSession`). The live summary is read with `readCardText`. Key symbols: `peekFor`, `clockTime`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/PeekPanel.svelte`: the peek sheet on the board and on Now, around `PeekSheet`, from the card's board snapshot. It hands the sheet the card page's Next step panel (`decision`, from `nextStepOf` and `nextStepWrites`) once the first view arrives; a move forward from it sets `follow`, and lets go of it when nothing moved, as Move back does. The panel's heading is an h3 there (`level`), under the sheet's h2 title, and it leaves out its own Stop and Send back (`inPeek`), since the sheet's foot draws Stop with its key and the Move back that stops a running card first. Its Delete card row opens `DeleteCard` (`deleting`), and the card leaving the snapshot closes the sheet. Key symbols: `PeekSheet`, `stageBefore`, `postWrite`, `decision`, `nextStepOf`, `nextStepWrites`, `DeleteCard`, `deleting`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/next-step.ts`: the writes of the Next step panel's buttons for one card, shared by the card page and the peek: the move (the question at the first QA first, `askFirst`), retry, Run on, Fix it, Send back with comments, stop, minimize, and a refusal as the card's one toast. Key symbols: `nextStepWrites`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/rail.ts`: rail icons, board list, repository line, the selector's rows and usage foot. The selector's rows: `busyOf` counts the cards waiting on the person (as Now does) and the running cards off board snapshots, `selectorRows` puts the busiest first with the current project counted off the live Now stream and the others as the page load read them, and `projectLine` is the line under a name. The boards are `railKinds`: every board that is on, with one Research item where its two kinds stood, which the palette lists too; Projects folds the kinds with `choicesOf` itself, because the demo setup reaches `projects.ts` under plain Node, and this file imports the ui barrel through `board-page.ts`. The count after each board is `railCounts`, the open cards of each snapshot on the Now stream, Research counting both its kinds. - `apps/local/src/lib/Rail.svelte`: the frame of every screen but the first run: the brand with the version line (`version`, hidden in the folded strip), project selector (on every plan every project, the busiest first with its count, another one a submit button to `/api/current-project`, then the open project's Repositories and Add a repository, New project at `/new` and Settings of the project; with no list from the layout, the open project with its repositories and New project), the bell beside the selector in the header (`top`) with the unread count on its corner (`unread`, folded too, under the project's initial; `onbell` opens the panel and the bell takes focus first, so the panel gives it back; its tip names G then B, the layout's `go:notifications`), search, Now with its count, the boards each with its quiet count off the Now stream (`counts`, hidden in the folded strip and kept in the link's name), Workspace with the changed files of the Workspace page's last read in the same quiet way (`changed`, from the layout's `workspaceChanged`, none at 0 or before a read), Scout with its blue dot while a finding is new since the last visit (`scoutNew`, off the Now stream), settings, and in the foot Getting started in place of the usage while it shows, and under Settings the Help line, Docs and Report a bug, which open the site and the issue form in a new tab (`help`, hidden in the folded strip). - `apps/local/src/lib/app-version.ts`: the build's version line. `APP_BUILD` is the define, with a 0.0.0 fallback for tests, and `versionLine` gives the short text beside the logo and the full line with the date, and `readBuild` is the build rule vite.config.ts runs with its git (tagged HEAD or no git, no commit). Key symbols: `AppBuild`, `APP_BUILD`, `readBuild`, `versionLine`.

- `apps/local/src/lib/getting-started.ts`: the first run's guidance as pure rules, safe in the browser: the steps from their facts (the drag step names the first agent column of the first card's board), the coach note and the offer by name, where the note sits, and what the first phase does.
