- `apps/local/src/lib/app-header.ts`: the project name the rail shows. Key symbols: `projectName`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/selector.ts`: every project the selector lists, on every plan, each counting off its own boards (`boardsOn` over its loop); one that cannot be read is listed by its number with no counts and a `console.warn`, and one whose boards cannot be read keeps its name with no counts; a project with no folder yet is left out. Only the current project counts live (`selectorRows`); the others keep the page load's counts. Key symbols: `projectChoices`, `boardsOn`, `busyOf`.

### `lib`: Now

- `apps/local/src/lib/now-snapshot.ts`: everything Now draws, in one read (a wrap-up held for Knowledge with nothing pending is a queued run row on the Knowledge lane, with no place): every board that is on, the waiting extras (a finished epic waits from the newest move of its tasks, and a ready card before the archive says what it holds, `holds` from `cardHolds`), running and done rows, today's figures, profiles, the Scout teaser, the chain view, the first run's guidance the rail and the board read live, what the question at the first QA shows while it is open and the cards whose move into QA asks it (`qaAsks`), and the newest card runs that ended ready or failed and left the queue, for their toasts (`endedRuns`), and the bell's unread count and newest few (`notificationsView`), and Set up the project's steps and whether the first run waits (`setupView`). `boardsOn` is every board that is on, which the selector counts too. `knowledgePending` counts the knowledge changes waiting for review. Key symbols: `nowSnapshot`, `boardsOn`, `waitingOf`, `waitingExtra`, `runRows`, `doneRows`, `endedRuns`, `today`, `scoutTeaser`, `firstRun`, `qaSetupFor`, `stackAnswered`, `notificationsView`, `setupView`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/now.ts`: pure rules of Now. `waitingItems` uses the board's own `waitingOnMe`, so the list, its count and the rail count are one number. An epic whose every task is done is the `tasks-done` kind: Start Wrap-up and no Send back. `stackAsk` says whether a move, or a retry in the QA column, asks the question at the first QA (only for a card `qaAsks` names, when the stream sends it), and `learnAsk` whether a move opens the Learn dialog of Set up the project. `nearLines` is the near-limit warning in Usage limits, with the profile. `longSummary` says whether a run summary is cut to its first lines behind Show all. Now shows the current project only: `waitingCount` is its count for the rail and the section, and `projectPath` puts the project on a write's address, because card keys repeat across projects. `knowledgeRun` is the run holding the knowledge lane as the Knowledge screen names it, with its start and last tool action. Key symbols: `NowSnapshot`, `knowledgeRun`, `projectPath`, `RunEnd`, `nearLines`, `isNow`, `NowItem`, `ItemKind`, `waitingItems`, `waitingCount`, `whenLine`, `startStep`, `backStep`, `slotsLine`, `slotPips`, `SlotPip`, `rowPill`, `rowSays`, `doneLine`, `todayStats`, `usageRows`, `stackAsk`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/NowPage.svelte`: the Now screen: waiting on you, running, done today, usage, the chain card, no Add card (N goes to the last board's Add card, `addOnBoard`), the waiting cards closer together than the other sections (`.waiting`), and the Make this a workspace note the layout names. Approve into QA asks the question at the first QA first. It shows the project picked in the rail only, every write names that project, and the lane chips name another project's holder by the selector's names (`projectNames`). A running row names the engine, model and effort with `ranOn` (`runWith`). Key symbols: `addOnBoard`, `runRow`, `runWith`, `projectPath`, `projectNames`, `CoachNote`, `closeNote`, `askFirst`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/NowItem.svelte`: one waiting card, finished in place: answer, approve and start, Fix it on a QA failure, send back, and an epic whose tasks are all done with its Start Wrap-up. The header is one row: pill, key, the title cut to one line with an ellipsis (the whole title in its `title` attribute) and the time. A ready card's run summary is drawn as Markdown, cut to three lines behind Show all when `longSummary` says it is long (more than three lines, a long line counted as it wraps at 72 characters). The main action comes first with its cost line, then the link and Send back. On a phone the title takes its own row, and a cut summary fades out at the bottom. Key symbols: `startStep`, `backStep`, `whenLine`, `longSummary`, `OptionButtons`.

### `lib`: Research

- `apps/local/src/lib/ResearchAdd.svelte`: the add box on top of the Research board: a question or what to investigate with its kind switch, Add to Inbox (⌘↵) posts `/api/cards` with `researchCard`'s body (the words as the title, a GitHub issue link alone as input, which the server reads through `gh`) and starts nothing. The page binds the field (`box`), so N and the palette's Add card put the focus there. Key symbols: `add`, `box`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/research-board.ts`: the Research board's pure rules, safe in the browser. `researchBoard` draws the question and investigation snapshots as one, in the shared columns with the counts of both, and puts an investigation's own start under the question's phase name so the column a card is dragged over says `/investigate` for it. `boardOfCard` gives each write, confirm and peek the snapshot of the card's own kind, and `researchTags` the "Question" or "Investigation" tag. Key symbols: `researchBoard`, `boardOfCard`, `researchTags`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/research.ts`: the server half of a Research card's presses: each kind's phase and report file (`RESEARCH_RUNS`), the answer and the newest summary (`researchAnswer`), and `deliveryCardFrom` (a Delivery card linked back, the answer as extra context). `researchAction` is the route body. The report is read with `readCardText`. Key symbols: `RESEARCH_RUNS`, `researchAnswer`, `deliveryCardFrom`, `researchAction`.

### `lib`: Add card and `create-card`

- `apps/local/src/lib/create-card.ts`: the server half of Add card. `createCardFrom` reads the input with `parseTicketRef` and `ticketLink`: free text makes a local ticket, a GitHub ref reads the issue or pull request through `gh` (a bare `repo#n` under the owner `refOwner` finds among the project repositories' origins, handed in by `githubSource`), an external key or a Jira link on a Jira project is read through `jiraReader` (source `jira`), and a typed key elsewhere is kept with a note. A Jira link on a local project and a Linear link make a local card titled with the key, with the link in its text. A Jira link from another site than `jiraSite` is refused. One transaction for the card, its extra context and its links. `postCard` is the route body and starts the card with `startFirst` when asked; `JiraSeams` hands a test a fake keychain and fetch. `readTicket` reads one ticket of the project's ticket system by key or link through the same reader and the same refusals (`jiraLinkKey`, `readExternal`), or on a project of local tickets a GitHub issue by link, `owner/repo#N` or `repo#N` through `gh` (`readIssueRef`, with `ownerOf` and `readIssue`, which Add card's issue read uses too), writes nothing, and refuses a ticket that is a card already; `getTicket` is the body of `GET /api/tickets/read`. `postTasks` reads every linked ref before the write and refuses a key linked twice. `addTasks` writes an epic's tasks in one transaction: a linked row as its ticket's card (`LinkedTicket`, source and title from the ticket), any other row under the key its row sent (`createCard`) or the next local key (`createLocalTicket`), with the shareable description its row sent under a "Part of <epic>" line, and the plan step it came from on the link (`card_links.step`); after the commit each row's internal notes go to `from-epic.md` in its card folder (`cardFolder`), and a note it could not write comes back as `note`. `nextCardKey` reads the next key of one board, a Research kind under its own prefix (`keyPrefixFor`). An epic's notes for a task are written with `writeCardFile`. Key symbols: `CardRequest`, `createCardFrom`, `postCard`, `startFirst`, `StartCard`, `TicketSources`, `JiraSeams`, `addTasks`, `postTasks`, `readTicket`, `getTicket`, `LinkedTicket`, `remoteOwner`, `remoteRepo`, `refOwner`, `githubOrigins`, `githubSource`, `nextCardKey`, `contextSources`, `READ_TIMEOUT_MS`. `githubOrigins` is the GitHub repository of each project repository's origin, which `refOwner` and Your tickets read.
- `apps/local/src/lib/my-tickets.ts`: Your tickets in Add card, read when the dialog opens. One `gh` issue search (`githubIssues`) for open issues assigned to the gh login (`GITHUB_ASSIGNED`) in the project's repositories from `githubOrigins`, and on a Jira project one search with `ASSIGNED_JQL` through `jiraReader`. A ticket whose key or source ref a card holds, done cards too, is left out, and a Jira key with the local prefix as well; at most `MINE_SHOWN` rows. Each row carries the GitHub issue link or the Jira key as `input`, which a press posts to `/api/cards`. A source that fails adds its sentence to `problems` and the other still answers. Key symbols: `myTickets`, `MyTicket`, `MyTickets`, `MySources`, `MINE_SHOWN`, `GITHUB_ASSIGNED`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/ticket-system.ts`: local tickets or Jira, read only. The Jira login is the machine's (`jiraSite` and `jiraEmail` are machine keys). `connectJira` checks the site and the email the way a Jira Scout source does, puts the token in the keychain under `JIRA_TOKEN_NAME`, switches no project and makes no call; `disconnectJira` removes the token, then the site and the email; `switchTickets` goes both ways; `jiraReader` reads the keychain only for a Jira project, and every refusal names Settings, Integrations (`ON_INTEGRATIONS`); `postJira` is the body of Connect Jira in Add card, which also makes the project read Jira. `checkKeyClash` asks Jira once, read only, through `jiraCall` with `CLASH_TIMEOUT_MS`, whether a project has the local prefix or the prefixes `otherPrefixes` would offer, and keeps the answer in `jiraKeyClash`; called after a prefix save, a switch to Jira and a connect, never on a page load. The worklog export keeps its own connection. Key symbols: `connectJira`, `disconnectJira`, `switchTickets`, `jiraReader`, `postJira`, `JiraForm`, `checkKeyClash`, `otherPrefixes`, `CLASH_TIMEOUT_MS`.

### `lib`: the question at the first QA

- `apps/local/src/lib/qa-setup.ts`: the server half. The `qaApp` setting maps a repository path to its answer, and `*` answers every repository (a single answer stored before). `answerFor` (one repository's answer), `qaReposOf` (the repositories QA starts apps for: the card's list), `stackAnswered` (every repository of the card, or of the project with no card, has an answer), `startIn` (the start command a folder proposes, else its first child folder's, `START_FOLDERS` first, skipping `node_modules` and dot folders), `ownStarts` (a card's own commands in the order they start: `*` once in the checkout root, then each repository's own) and `ownFolder` (where they run), `qaSetupFor` (about one repository: the card's first with no answer, or the project's; the start command, the port, the scripts text of the folder it runs in, whether the wall can start, the test command and Playwright, read from the repository's project folder, and with a card from that card's copy when the project folder names no start command), `appFolder` (where the question reads the scripts: the card's copy, or the clone before it has one), `scriptsHash` and `scriptsChanged` (the hash of the package.json `scripts` saved with a start command, and the check before each start that clears that repository's answer on a change), `answerQa` (Start QA, and Edit on Settings, Project with `repo`: saves the answer for the repository asked about, own commands refused with no Start, with the hash of the scripts text the dialog sent, refused when it is not the text of the folder the app starts in, and with the browser switch on adds Nolzu's Playwright server to the project's MCP list on for QA through `setUpBrowser`, off turns QA off for every server through `turnOffBrowser`), `chromeFound` (where Playwright's `chrome` channel looks for Google Chrome, which the switch and the row need), `appRows` and `appWords` (the rows of Start the app on Settings, Project, each answer in a few words), `clearAnswer` (Ask again for one repository), and `answerFirst` and `answerAgain`, the refusals of a move or a QA retry nobody could ask on. Key symbols: `QaSetup`, `answerFor`, `qaReposOf`, `stackAnswered`, `startIn`, `START_FOLDERS`, `ownStarts`, `ownFolder`, `OwnApp`, `appRows`, `appWords`, `AppRow`, `clearAnswer`, `answerFirst`, `answerAgain`, `appFolder`, `scriptsHash`, `scriptsChanged`, `qaSetupFor`, `answerQa`, `PLAYWRIGHT_FILE` (the file an older Nolzu wrote, which `migrateQaBrowser` recognises), `chromeFound`, `setUpBrowser`, `turnOffBrowser`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/mcp-list.ts`: a project's MCP servers as the shared file `.nolzu/mcp-servers.json` holds them (`MCP_FILE`), pure. `parseMcpList` reads it as untrusted, names each entry it leaves out and keeps it with the unknown keys (`McpRest`, `McpEntry.extra`) for the next write, says when the file cannot be read at all (`broken`, a git conflict among them) and names a secret value a hand edit left, `mcpListText` writes it back with all of that and no secret value ever, `entryProblem` refuses a bad server (name, command, `https://` address with no login in it, env and header names, line breaks, a secret named after an engine's own variable, `reservedName`, tool names, phases), `urlWarning` names a query key that looks like a login, `secretKeysOf` lists a server's secrets, `gridPhases` the columns of the grid from the boards that are on, `unlimitedReadOnly` the read-only phases a server with no tool list is on for, and `serversFor` the servers of one phase. - `apps/local/src/lib/mcp-store.ts`: the list on disk and its secrets. `readMcpList` and `writeMcpList` through `writablePath` (a write reads the file again, keeps what it holds besides the servers and refuses a file it cannot read), `commitMcpList` (the file alone committed in the project folder's own repository, in `inTurn`, the turn the loop's knowledge commit takes too), `commitMcpListSoon` (one commit for a burst of grid clicks, flushed by `flushMcpCommit` when a loop closes, and the boot commits what is left), `listUncommitted`, `commitProblem` (no commit of a file Nolzu cannot read checked, an address with a login or a gone file refused, a refused secret left to "Needs secret", the list read again before the write, the setting cleared last), `sweepMcpRuns` (Cursor merges put back, then the data folder's `mcp-runs` emptied and made again with mode 700, at boot before the QA copies go), `promptMcp` (what a prompt names before the run), `cursorMergesHere` (whether a Cursor does not track the file) and `runMcp`: the servers a run of a phase on a vendor gets, secrets read from the keychain into memory (`Job.mcp`), with a sentence for each server left out (a missing or
