is not moved; a gone old file refuses the QA run. `secretName` takes the server and hashes its address, or command, arguments and folder, and its plain values, so a pull that points a server elsewhere leaves its secret behind. Key symbols: `readMcpList`, `writeMcpList`, `commitMcpList`, `commitProblem`, `listShared`, `secretName`, `appKeychain`, `missingSecrets`, `removeSecrets`, `browserEntry`, `BROWSER_NAME`, `TMP_TOKEN`, `qaServers`, `qaHasBrowser`, `migrateQaBrowser`, `sweepMcpRuns`, `commitMcpListSoon`, `RunMcp`, `runMcp`, `mcpNames`, `promptMcp`, `cursorMergesHere`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/mcp-import.ts`: Import from my engines, read only. `claudeServers` (`~/.claude.json`: its global `mcpServers`, then the project folder's, which win on a name), `codexServers` (`~/.codex/config.toml`: `[mcp_servers.<name>]` tables with their `env` and `http_headers`, inline or as sub-tables, through a small line reader, not all of TOML), `cursorServers` (`~/.cursor/mcp.json`), each a `Found` with every value a secret and the values kept apart from the entry; `findImports` reads the three under a home folder, and `importServers` reads them again, refuses a server whose address holds a login, adds the picked servers off everywhere, checks every one before it writes any secret, and keeps a value plain only where the person marked it. Key symbols: `Found`, `ImportSource`, `claudeServers`, `codexServers`, `cursorServers`, `findImports`, `importServers`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/mcp-page.ts`: Settings, MCP servers, the server side. `mcpPage` (the rows with what this machine lacks (a typed secret the keychain refuses is named, and a save reads the list again after the keychain), the read-only and address warnings, `cursorSecret` on a server with a secret while a profile runs on Cursor, the grid's phases, a file it cannot read, whether the list reaches the team, and why the old QA browser is not moved yet), every write refused while the file cannot be read, `saveServer` (add or edit from the form, a typed secret to the keychain, an empty one kept, a renamed server's secrets moved, a dropped one removed), `deleteServer`, `setPhase` (one cell of the grid), `saveSecret` (the "Needs secret" field) and `testServer` (Test: a saved server by name, or the form as typed with its typed secrets, through `probeEntry`, with the tools the server or form allows now). `testServer` fills an empty secret box of the form with the saved secret, read under the server as saved. Key symbols: `McpRow`, `McpPageData`, `mcpPage`, `saveServer`, `deleteServer`, `setPhase`, `saveSecret`, `TestAnswer`, `testServer`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/browser-test.ts`: Test the browser on the QA browser row. Starts the config's first MCP server in a wall that opens the temp folder only (`serverWall`, which the MCP probe shares) (unwalled with the reason where the wall cannot start), speaks JSON-RPC over stdio (`initialize`, then `browser_navigate` to a page in a temp folder), and answers with one sentence: the time it took, or the server's error or npm's failure line. Kills the server's process group after, and gives up after 120 s. Key symbols: `serverWall`, `testBrowser`, `BrowserTestOptions`, `BrowserTest`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/mcp-probe.ts`: the probe behind Test and the tool list on Settings, MCP servers. `probeEntry` reads the entry's secrets (typed in the form first, the keychain for the rest; a missing one starts nothing), then a local server starts only inside `serverWall` (a wall that cannot start means no start, never unwalled; a command not on PATH says so) and gets `initialize`, `notifications/initialized` and `tools/list` over stdio with its pages, and a remote one gets the same over streamable HTTP (a JSON or stream answer, the session id, a DELETE at the end) or the older SSE transport. 15 s at most, then the process group is killed. One sentence back: "Started, N tools: a, b." with the names, or the reason with the server's last words cut short, every secret value replaced. The fake stdio server of its tests is `apps/local/fixtures/mcp-server.mjs`. Key symbols: `probeEntry`, `Probe`, `ProbeOptions`, `probeLocal`, `probeRemote`, `sseEvents`, `redact`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/qa-ask.svelte.ts`: the question open over any screen, and `askFirst`, which a screen calls in place of a move: it opens the Learn dialog first while the project gates its first run (`gateFirst`), whose "I will write it myself" goes on to the question, then the question in place of a move into QA (`askQa`), and `askBeforeRetry`, the same before a retry of a card in QA (the board, Now and the Next step panel). Key symbols: `qaAsk`, `askFirst`, `askQa`, `askBeforeRetry`, `QaAskOpen`, `gateFirst`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/setup-gate.svelte.ts`: the Learn dialog of Set up the project open over any screen (`setupGate`), and `gateFirst`, which opens it in place of a move that starts a phase while the Now stream says the project gates its first run (`learnAsk`). Key symbols: `setupGate`, `gateFirst`, `SetupGateOpen`, `learnAsk`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/SetupGateDialog.svelte`: the Learn dialog. It reads `GET /api/setup` for the repositories not learned yet and the Starts line with its estimate through `learnEstimateText` (a skeleton meanwhile), says in one line what runs lack without the knowledge (`WRITE_IT_MYSELF`), and offers Learn and then start (go; posts the held move to the server, which starts it after the Learn runs) and "I will write it myself" (saves the mark, then sends the move the screen held back). Any close but the one after "I will write it myself" lets the screen's button go (`stay`). Key symbols: `press`, `sent`, `postWrite`, `startsCommand`, `WRITE_IT_MYSELF`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/SetupSteps.svelte`: Set up the project drawn two ways: `banner`, one quiet box the layout draws on the boards and on Now with each open required and recommended step until it is done or put away (`bannerSteps`), and `list`, every step with what it is for, its link and its state, on the first task screen. The list links a step from its action only, its title plain, so each place is one Tab stop. A recommended or optional step has Put away (`putAway`, posts the `dismissed` mark), the required one has none. Key symbols: `WHERE`, `bannerSteps`, `putAway`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/setup.ts`: Set up the project's server side. `setupFor` reads the steps off the project: the stored marks (`setupSteps`), Learn done when no repository file is a stub (`repoStubs`, read only while Learn has no mark), the QA browser from its config, the tickets from the ticket system. `setupView` adds whether the next run a person starts opens the Learn dialog (`learnGates`, only on a project New project made, whose marks are not null, and before `personStarted`), which Now's snapshot carries. `startLearnRepos` is Knowledge's Learn your repositories, which marks the step done on a start. `learnFirst` is the sentence `moveTo` refuses the first run with while that holds, for a sender nobody could ask. `learnThenStart` queues a Learn run per repository not learned yet, marks the step done, and stores the move in the `afterLearn` setting; `followLearn` is the check the loop calls on every change it tells and once when it starts, which makes each stored move through `moveTo` once no Learn run is left in the queue (with a warning for the card when the newest Learn run of a repository ended after the press failed or was cut off, `saySkippedLearn`), so a restart still makes it; it is no listener, so a shut down loop holds none. `withAfterLearn` puts `afterLearn` (the column, the repository a Learn run reads, the press time) on a held card in the loop's board snapshot. `postSetup` is the one write: a mark (refusing `dismissed` for Learn) or Learn and then start. `learnGateOffer` is `learnReposOffer` with the stubs only. Key symbols: `setupFor`, `setupView`, `markSetup`, `startLearnRepos`, `learnFirst`, `learnThenStart`, `followLearn`, `withAfterLearn`, `postSetup`, `learnGateOffer`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/QaAskDialog.svelte`: the dialog of the question at the first QA: the four choices of `QaChoices`, the browser switch only where Google Chrome or Playwright is found, Start QA (go) with the Starts line, Cancel the move. It always asks `GET /api/qa-setup` for the card's own copy, which can name the start command and gives the scripts text of the card's branch that the start option shows, with a line where the start runs with no wall. In a workspace that answer can be about another repository than the stream's, and then it replaces the question. Start QA sends the scripts it shows, and a refusal reads the card's copy again (`readCard`); a choice made before the answer stays (`picked`). Key symbols: `start`, `stay`, `picked`, `postWrite`.

### `lib`: the chain

- `apps/local/src/lib/chain.ts`: the chain suggestion on Now as pure rules over stored moves. Key symbols: `CHAIN_SUGGEST_AFTER`, `planDecisions`, `suggestAfter`, `suggestionText`, `ChainView`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/chain-store.ts`: the database read and the write behind it (`chainBoards`, `chainNotNow`), and the card's tick "Go on to Implement after Plan" on its Settings tab, stored as the size small (`cardChain`, null where the chain is off for the card's board, and `saveCardChain`). Key symbols: `chainView`, `writeChain`, `cardChain`, `saveCardChain`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/last-board.ts`: the board the tab showed last, in sessionStorage, which Add card away from a board opens on, Delivery when none or it is off. Key symbols: `rememberBoard`, `lastBoard`.

The chain itself runs in `chainOn` in `loop.ts`, through `smallChain` in `packages/core/src/board.ts`.

### `lib`: palette and keys

- `apps/local/src/lib/palette.ts`: the command palette rows. A key's row runs the key's own action, and a move row sends what a drop sends. "Check every Scout source now" runs the layout's `scout:check`, which presses the Scout page's Check all sources. A card query still shows the rows marked `beside`: Now, the card's own board, Add card and the Scout check. Key symbols: `paletteRows`, `rankRows`, `PaletteRow`, `Hrefs`, `beside`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/Palette.svelte`: the palette dialog over any screen with the rail. Its Add card row, where N adds none, opens the last board with `?add` (`lastBoard`). A move row into QA asks the question at the first QA first. A click on the backdrop closes it, as Esc does. Key symbols: `paletteRows`, `rankRows`, `sendMove`, `liveKeys`, `askFirst`, `closeOnBackdrop`, `lastBoard`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/page-keys.svelte.ts`: the keys of the current screen, which the layout merges with the global keys. Key symbols: `pageKeys`, `ScreenKeys`.

The key list and the matcher are in `packages/ui/src/keymap.ts`, the one handler is `packages/ui/src/Keyboard.svelte`.

### `lib`: toasts

- `apps/local/src/lib/toast-stack.svelte.ts`: the app's one toast stack. Key symbols: `toastStack`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/ToastCorner.svelte`: mounts `ToastHost` once in the layout, and drops a toast with `dismissed`. Key symbols: `ToastHost`, `dismissed`, `toastStack`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/now-toasts.ts`: the toasts the Now stream raises, pure: a card's run that ended ready, failed or with no QA verdict, with its run line; "Scout: 2 new findings" when the count rises; the near-limit warning once per window crossing 90%. `Heard` is what one tab has heard: the first snapshot and the first limits event replay state and raise nothing, and a run that ended before the page load is never news. Key symbols: `nowToasts`, `Heard`, `ToastHrefs`.

- `apps/local/src/lib/notifications.ts`: what the bell keeps of a toast, on the server. `noteRunEnd` keeps a card run's end in its toast's words, plus a question it asked, once per run (the loop calls it after the card lands and when a start fails). `noteMaintenanceFailure` keeps a failed knowledge run of no card, Learn or Optimize, linked to Knowledge. `notificationsView` is the unread count and the newest few the Now stream carries. The near-limit item is kept by `saveLimit` and Scout's by `saveScoutFindings`. Key symbols: `noteRunEnd`, `notificationsView`, `NotificationsView`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/NotificationsPanel.svelte`: the bell's sheet on the right, a `Dialog` with the `side` class: the list from `GET /api/notifications` newest first, with the stream's newer few on top, each with its kind in words, New, its card and how long ago, and a link to its card or its own address. It posts the read after the list lands and again when a new one comes in while it is open. A click outside the sheet closes it as Esc does, through `Dialog`'s own backdrop click: the page behind is inert, the bell included. Key symbols: `listed`, `items`, `hrefOf`, `read`, `Dialog`.
- `apps/local/src/routes/api/notifications/+server.ts`: `GET`, the project's kept list; `POST` reads them all, and only when one was unread moves the version and tells the stream. Key symbols: `GET`, `POST`, `markNotificationsRead`.

The rules (limit, one toast per card) are in `packages/ui/src/toasts.ts`. A toast about no card, such as Scout's, has a `title` in place of the key and a `note` for its mono line. Board toasts are built by `pressed`, `answered`, `refusedDrop`, `cardAdded` and `dismissed` in `board-page.ts`, and the answer to a Settings, Project save by `settingToast`, under the placeholder key `SETTING_TOAST` and with no href, so it has no Open (a card with no href draws none).

### `lib`: the card page readers

- `apps/local/src/lib/ticket.ts`: the card and its tabs, the tab redirect (a tab of the card's board its stage has not reached goes to the overview, not a 404), Move forward and Retry targets (a failed card, or an idle one whose newest run was stopped or interrupted), and `postWrite` (POST, answer with the refusal sentence or null). Key symbols: `loadTicket`, `ticketLayout`, `ticketTab`, `TAB_REDIRECT`, `stageLabel`, `forwardMove`, `gateMove`, `retryStage`, `clusterNote`, `postWrite`. `gateMove` is the column the gate offers once its questions are settled, while the card rests and that column starts a run.
- `apps/local/src/lib/overview.ts`: the overview tab: the answer or the report of a Research card with its sources split off (`researchReport`, `research`), the summary with which phase wrote it and when (`summaryFrom`, `summaryAt`), `plan/plan.md` on a card past Plan, closed too, and whether it opens (`planOpen`, a stage of Plan or Implement), the question of a blocked card with the phase that asked and the engine, model and effort it ran on, every move from the card events with who made it and its stamp (`said` and `at`, which the page draws as a time ago), a move into Review as the person's move on out of it ("approved by you"), and, on a move that has one, its reason and the phase it left (`back` on a move back, a send back note), linked cards, epic tasks with the plan step their link keeps and the key the next one gets (`nextKey`, from `peekLocalKey`), extra context limit, unlink, the facts under the trail (branch, with `Fact.edit` on an open card: locked once any checkout pushed it, and before a checkout the name the first run takes; on a closed card the repositories from `cardRepos`, checkout, sessions, the engine of the newest run or of the next step, the Discoveries count with `Fact.file` that opens it, source with the ticket key and its page from `sourceTicket` as `Fact.ticket`), a closed card's knowledge change with when its commit was made (`cardKnowledge`), and the tab counts of the head (Review counts the newest round's findings, through `reviewFor`, and Log the card's runs, "5 runs"), the ticket meta (`ticketSource`, `refinedAt`), the phase that wrote the summary (`summaryPhase`), the newest summary of a run other than QA for a QA FAIL (`built`), and on an epic its document chips and the excerpt of `architecture.md` (`epicDocs`, `architectureExcerpt`: the first bullet of `## Before you approve`, else the first paragraph of `## The shape`), and `seed`, the brief's criteria and the `D` lines of the architecture and plan, which the task table's bodies copy (`epicSeed`), and on an epic `sharePlan`, the text of `share/PLAN.md`, and `linkSystem`, "Jira" on a project that reads Jira and "GitHub" on one of local tickets. `planSteps` reads the step titles of a card's plan for the board's epic count. `discoveriesIn` counts the entries of `discoveries.md` (lines starting with "- ") for the Discoveries fact, left out at none. Key symbols: `overviewFor`, `epicDocs`, `architectureExcerpt`, `EpicDoc`, `planSteps`, `peekLocalKey`, `CardMoveLine`, `cardKnowledge`, `KnowledgeLine`, `cardFacts`, `Fact`, `sourceTicket`, `tabCounts`, `reviewFor`, `CONTEXT_LIMIT`, `saveContext`, `unlink`. `readOnlyBreak` is the newest run's read-only mark (`readOnlyBreakOf`), its phase and engine in words. `commitWarning` is what the newest Implement run committed or pushed (`commitWarningOf`), null once the next one is made.
- `apps/local/src/lib/changes.ts`: the Changes tab, one group per card repository and then one per other workspace repository that says the card has no changes there (no git for those), with the push facts read only for a repository the card worked in (`hasWork`), each with the `name` the tab shows (the root by its folder's name, never "project folder"), and the patch, message and commit writers. `commitAndPush` is the push dialog's one press: the commit, then `pushFor`. `commitAsk` reads `only`, the ticked paths the composer sends, which `commitFor` commits exactly. `changesCount` is the tab's count in the head (what is not committed, every card repository, "none yet" before a checkout). `changeGroups` takes the tab's `?view` and reads the commit list (`cardCommits`) only for Not committed, per repository with commits and nothing to commit, and `commitChanges` is one commit as a diff group, and `commentTarget` checks a comment's repository and file against the change and reads the commit it is made on. Key symbols: `changesFor`, `changeGroups`, `changesCount`, `commitChanges`, `commentTarget`, `repoAsk`, `patchFor`, `messageFor`, `commitFor`, `commitAndPush`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/diff-comments.ts`: the `diff_comments` rows: every comment of a card, add (checked by `commentTarget`), edit and delete, each answering a status and the comment or a sentence. The open count and the newest Implement session (`openCommentCount`, `implementSession`) feed the Next step panel on the card page and in the peek. Key symbols: `commentsOf`, `addComment`, `editComment`, `deleteComment`, `COMMENT_LIMIT`, `openCommentCount`, `implementSession`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/checkout.ts`: the card repository a request names, and the card branch. `templateBranch` is the name the template gives a card now, from its title and the `Kind:` and `Branch:` lines of its `refine/refined.md`, or the name the person typed (`chosenBranch`, `cards.branch`) when there is one; cleanup and Evidence read it too. `cardCheckouts` lists the checkouts on disk with their bases. `unsentWork` counts what they hold that no remote has: files not committed, and commits on no remote branch and not in the base, where the repository has a remote. `renameCardBranch` is the card page's rename: refused on a closed card and while a run is queued or running, checked by `branchNameProblem`, `renameFreshBranch` over every checkout with `byPerson`, then stored, so a card with no checkout yet only stores it. `templateBranch` reads the brief with `readCardText`. Key symbols: `cardRepo`, `checkoutOf`, `cardBranch`, `templateBranch`, `chosenBranch`, `cardCheckouts`, `unsentWork`, `renameCardBranch`, `noCheckout`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/checkouts.ts`: removing a closed card's checkouts. Key symbols: `isClosed`, `removeCardCheckouts`, `removeClosedCheckouts`, `checkoutsSaid`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/card-delete.ts`: what Delete card says, pure: the refusal while a run is queued or going, while this process holds the app, or when a checkout cannot be removed (`stuck`); what goes; a warning when the stored app state says it may still run; one warning per checkout (changed files, commits not pushed, the files git ignores by name, `ignoredPaths`, with dependency and build folders left out), and one per card branch (`DeleteBranch`, the card's own name and never the base; goes only when merged into the base, `branchGoes`), for a ticket card, an epic's tasks and a task's epic, each marked when it `loses` something. `changedSince` compares a fresh read with what the confirm showed, and `pickedUp` is the toast's line about what a card added later on the key picks up. `seenOf` is what the confirm shows and sends back. Key symbols: `deletePlan`, `DeleteFacts`, `DeleteRepo`, `DeleteBranch`, `DeletePlan`, `DeleteWarning`, `branchGoes`, `changedSince`, `pickedUp`, `seenOf`, `ignoredPaths`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/delete-card.ts`: Delete card's reads and removals. `deleteCheck` reads the facts (the scheduler, the run rows, the app's state and `appHeld`, each repository's checkout through `listWorktrees`, what would stop its removal (`stuckOf`), the commits not in the base and those on no remote, files git ignores, a branch left after the checkout went) for the confirm, with the counts it shows (`seen`); `deleteCard` reads them again, refuses with the same sentence or when the checkout holds more than `seen`, deletes the rows with `deleteCardRows`, then removes each checkout with force, the root folder, each branch `branchGoes` allows, the run logs and, when asked, the card folder, and answers "Deleted KEY." with what stayed and what failed (`Deleted`), a failure also kept as a bell row. Key symbols: `deleteCheck`, `deleteCard`, `DeleteCheck`, `Deleted`, `stuckOf`, `countsOf`, `ignoredIn`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/DeleteCard.svelte`: Delete card's confirm for the card page and the peek: asks for the check as it opens, on Check again and after a refused delete (`read`, `again`), posts the delete with what it showed, and raises its toast (`cardDeleted`). Key symbols: `DeleteCardDialog`, `cardDeleted`, `confirm`, `read`, `again`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/publish.ts`: push, pull and the compare link, whose body is the plain summary followed by the card's `implement/pr.md` without its title line, with any line that names a tool as the writer taken out (`pullRequestBody`). A push that went marks Getting started's last step. `remoteEnv` (ssh in batch mode) and `stopAfter` serve the Workspace push too. `implement/pr.md` is read with `readCardText`. Key symbols: `publishFor`, `pushFor`, `pullFor`, `pullRequestBody`, `markStep`, `remoteEnv`, `stopAfter`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/repo-param.ts`: the `repo` query of a Changes group. Key symbols: `at`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/discussion.ts`: the Discussion tab: threads from runs with their message count and run start (`ThreadRow`), the board's phases that have not run yet (`later`, greyed in the list), messages from the table, the model and effort a message runs on, the estimate of a run of that phase per model only where runs measured it (`startEstimate`), and `recordPhaseSummary`, which posts what a phase said into its thread. `withTools` gives each agent message the tool calls of the run that wrote it, read from that run's log file with its engine's parser (`toolCalls`: name, short subject from `toolSubject`, input, result size and error mark), or null when the log is gone. An ask discussion takes its session from its own Ask runs (`runs.thread`), the ask row's model, effort and estimate, and `asking` while its newest run goes. A phase thread is `asking` the same way while its newest run is queued or running (`GOING`). An ask discussion's row carries its `subject` (table `thread_subjects`) and the first line of its first message (`opening`, from `oneLine`); `titling` says a `thread-title` pass of the card still runs. `cleanSubject` reads the pass's answer, `saveSubject` writes it only where none is (a person's name replaces and marks it), `wantsSubject` asks for one while the discussion has no subject and no subject pass ran for it, and `renameThread` is the rename route's handler. A PR comment thread (`T3`, listed once it holds a message) is read-only the same way. `answerOf` is the text an Ask run ends with. The board's phases in run order (`order`) let the tab list threads in phase order. Each thread and message carries its time in the trail's words (`when`, from `whenSaid`), and the first message of each run carries the line drawn above it (`withDividers`, `RunDivider`): the phase, its start, length and cost where the row has them, and the files of the phase's folder (`listCardFiles`, per phase, not per run). A person's message in the words of a gate choice (`isGateAnswer`) carries `forNext`, the label of the column after the thread's phase. Key symbols: `discussionFor`, `isGateAnswer`, `withTools`, `toolCalls`, `ToolCall`, `ThreadRow`, `messagesIn`, `addMessage`, `recordPhaseSummary`, `sendMessage`, `answerOf`, `asking`, `withDividers`, `RunDivider`, `THREAD_TITLE`, `SUBJECT_LIMIT`, `oneLine`, `cleanSubject`, `saveSubject`, `wantsSubject`, `renameThread`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/gate.ts`: the gate of a card (`gateFor`): the newest run once it ended, its main file's review block and `blocks: yes` questions (`gateOf`), each with the person's choice, and the output check stored on that run; null while a run goes or with nothing to show, which a phase with no review block is unless its check found a problem. A choice is a person's message on the phase's thread tied to that run that starts with the question's id, so one typed in the Discussion counts, and a new run of the phase asks again. `settleQuestion` stores the default or a typed answer up to `CHOICE_LIMIT` that way, so the Discussion shows it and `personsWords` carries it to the next prompt. `waitsOn` is the sentence `moveTo` refuses with. Key symbols: `gateFor`, `CardGate`, `GateQuestion`, `waitsOn`, `settleQuestion`, `CHOICE_LIMIT`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/persons-words.ts`: what the person said on a card, for the prompt of a fresh run: each question a run asked (`runs.question`) with the answer stored after it in the phase's thread, the reason on a move (`card_events.reason`: Accept anyway forward, a Send back note back), and the person's other Discussion messages, leaving out PR comment threads, which open with the reviewer's words. `cardHistory` gives the wrap-up the card's runs before it: how often each phase ran (no Ask and no pass, `NOT_A_PASS`) and each QA verdict that was not PASS, with its reason. Key symbols: `personsWords`, `cardHistory`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/files.ts`: the card folder and the Files tab, each file with when it last changed in the trail's words. Every file goes out with `nosniff` and `FILE_POLICY`: a sandbox, nothing from the network, no script, stylesheets from this app only (a design screen's `tokens.css`), and framed by this app only. The page shows and frames a file through the path-shaped route `files/[...path]`, so a relative link lands beside the file; Download keeps `file?path=`. Key symbols: `cardFolder`, `filesFor`, `fileResponse`, `FILE_POLICY`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/when-said.ts`: a time as the trail and the Files tab say it: the time today, the weekday and time this week, the date and time before. `overview.ts` exports it too. Key symbols: `whenSaid`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/run-log.ts`: the Log tab and the log routes. `logFor` lists the card's runs with what each row knows (engine, model, effort, times, cost, tokens), Ask runs included. `logResponse` sends each event with the time noted in front of its line (`at`, read with `runLogTime`), and each tool event with what it acted on (`subject`, from `toolSubject` and the run's folder). `rawLog` is Download raw: the log file the run row names, refused for a run of another card and for any field beside the run id. Key symbols: `logFor`, `logResponse`, `rawLog`, `runLogTime`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/log-stream.ts`: reads JSON lines off a fetch body. Key symbols: `readLines`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/review.ts`: the Review tab of a PR reviews card: rounds read from the card folder, every one that can be read (`read`), the head the shown round read, and why a re-review makes sense (`why`: the commits pushed since the saved round, `newCommitsOf`, and the findings it left open). The loop reads the newest round with `roundsIn` too, to save a review's round on the card. Key symbols: `reviewFor`, `roundsIn`, `ReviewView`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/qa-panel.ts`: the two QA panels. `qaInputFor` reads the database and the card folder, and `stackStatusFor` runs the Status of each own command of the card at once, one block under each name, which the card page streams; `qaPanelFor` is both in one wait. It reads `qa/report.md` for its checklist and `qa/addresses.md` for the screenshot bar, with the start command and the list of own commands for the App line. `cardOwn` is the card's own commands with their folders and names, and `ownProblem` why they have nowhere to run. `cardStackEnv` is also what the loop hands own commands. Key symbols: `qaPanelFor`, `qaInputFor`, `stackStatusFor`, `StackStatus`, `stackLane`, `cardOwn`, `CardOwn`, `ownProblem`, `cardStackEnv`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/QaChoices.svelte`: the four ways QA gets the app, drawn by the QA question and by Edit on Start the app: the start command with its scripts, an address already running, own commands with Start, Stop and Status boxes shown once picked, and the code only. The caller owns the values (`$bindable`) and the save. Key symbols: `pickRunning`, `ownWhere`, `ownPlace`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/QaAppEdit.svelte`: Edit on one row of Start the app: `QaChoices` in a form that posts `?/qaRepo` with hidden fields, starting from the saved answer. Key symbols: `QaChoices`, `ownPlace`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/held-answer.svelte.ts`: the answer of a part a server load streams. A reload of the same card keeps the drawn answer until its own lands, a new card drops it, and a failure says SvelteKit's error page words (`failedWords`). Key symbols: `streamed`, `Settled`, `failedWords`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/stream-merge.ts`: a streamed answer merged into what the card page drew off the database: the status command's words into the QA panels' input, `statusPending` until then, which the stack line draws as a skeleton (`withStatus`), and slow fields into rows by id, so a stale answer never adds or drops a row (`mergeById`). Key symbols: `withStatus`, `mergeById`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/BranchFact.svelte`: the value side of the Branch row in the card facts: a pencil that turns the name into a box with Save and Cancel, its own pending state and the refusal beside the box, or with `locked` the plain name with why in its title. Key symbols: `locked`, `onsave`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/Streamed.svelte`: draws a streamed part: a `Skeleton` in the answer's room (`height`, a block unless `shape` says otherwise, `count` rows for the rows shape, with `waiting` read to screen readers, or the page's own `wait` snippet), the error words in place, then the answer's snippet. Key symbols: `streamed`, `waiting`, `height`, `shape`, `count`, `wait`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/stack-action.ts`: Start, Stop and Force on the App line (`load`, `unload` and `force` on the wire), over the card's own commands in order. A hand Start that keeps running is held in a map in the server process (`held`) until Stop, Force, `releaseHeld` before and after the card's QA, or `releaseAll` in the loop's shutdown. A second press of a card while one runs is refused (`pressing`). `appHeld` says whether this process holds the card's app, is pressing it, or started it this session with a start that exited (`started`, cleared by a Stop or Force that takes it down), for Delete card. `press` catches a throw from `startAll` or `stopAll`, such as a key `stackCommand` will not take, so the card never stays loading or unloading. Key symbols: `pressStack`, `isStackAction`, `releaseHeld`, `releaseAll`, `startAll`, `stopAll`, `pressing`, `appHeld`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/card-models.ts`: the Settings tab model rows and their save. Each row is an action with its step name on the card's board (`label`, the name the board's refused drop says), whether it is a step of that board, what it runs, what Reset gives back (`follows`, the card's preset under the project's own rows, or the saved preset it follows, whose rows read as the preset's, `fromSaved`), and the estimate of both only where runs measured one (`measured` from `project-models.ts`, over `startEstimate`). Each profile comes with its models, for the combined picker, and one with none keeps its place with a reason (`pickerProfiles`). The tab's per card figures come from `cardEstimate`: each step alone (`stepEstimate`, none on a row that will not start) and each preset over the board's steps, a built-in one on the project's rows and a saved one on its own (`presets`), only where this project's runs measured them. It names the project's saved presets (`savedPresets`), the one the card follows (`saved`: its own, or for a card that follows the project the one the project is on, `projectSavedPreset`, whose rows then read as that preset's except a phase the project changed on top of it) and the engine the built-in three run on (`presetEngine`, the first of `projectProfiles`). The save takes an engine only with its model, a reset of one action, or a preset, built in or saved by name, which clears every value the card set (`followPreset`, `followSavedPreset`). Key symbols: `settingsFor`, `saveCardModels`, `measured`, `followPreset`, `followSavedPreset`, `fromSaved`, `stepEstimate`, `presets`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/card-repos.ts`: the Settings tab repository picker. Key symbols: `cardReposFor`, `saveCardRepos`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/run-on.ts`: what "Run on" offers on a failed card. After a stop on a usage limit (`limitedVendor`: the run failed and the stored reading of its login has a full window) it leaves out the other logins of that vendor and says so when nothing is left. It, `settingsFor` and `modelsFor` take the wall state as a last argument that defaults to the runner's `wallHere`, and tests pass it. Key symbols: `runOnOffer`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/summary.ts`: the plain summary of a run: the phase's `summary.md`, or an extraction pass, which starts in the checkout and not in a QA copy that is gone by then, and gets none of the run's MCP servers. Key symbols: `recordSummary`, `readText`, `said`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/feedback.ts`: the files of a triage run. `writeFeedbackFile` writes `<card folder>/feedback/discussions.md` (the plan or brief, then every open discussion with its facts, earlier verdict and fix note, and its messages, the person's own marked; `keptMessages` cuts the oldest of a long thread past `THREAD_BUDGET` and says how many) and drops an old `triage.md`. `applyTriageFile` parses `triage.md` with `parseTriage` and applies it whole (`applyTriage`) or answers the refusal. `triageProblem` is the refusal the PRs tab shows once no run can still be writing the file, and `triageOfferFor` what the Run triage button runs on (`starts`, which the tab draws with `startsCommand` as every start is drawn), costs (measured runs, or the preset's measured range where there is one) and would be refused with. `fixFeedbackMessage` is what a Fix tells the Implement session (the discussion with every message whole, path:line, the triage summary and reason, and no commit, no push, no reply), `discussMessage` the first message of a Discuss thread, and `openDiscussion` writes it once under the ref. Its card folder reads and writes go through `readCardText`, `writeCardFile` and `removeCardFile`. Key symbols: `writeFeedbackFile`, `discussionsFile`, `keptMessages`, `applyTriageFile`, `triageProblem`, `triageOfferFor`, `TriageOffer`, `isOpen`, `fixFeedbackMessage`, `discussMessage`, `openDiscussion`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/fix-check.ts`: the commit and push check around every Implement run, a Fix run among them. `readHeads` reads HEAD and the upstream tracking ref's commit of each checkout with read-only git (no fetch; a folder whose toplevel is not itself is not a checkout), and `runMoved` words a moved HEAD as a commit, a moved upstream as a push, and an unreadable checkout as its own reason, naming the fix run or the implement run, or answers null. Nothing is undone. Key symbols: `readHeads`, `runMoved`, `Heads`, `Checkout`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/pr-diff.ts`: writes `<card folder>/pr-review/diff.patch` before a review run, right after `readPrHead` reads the pull request's head, which the round saves as the head it reviewed. The patch is written with `writeCardFile`. Key symbols: `writePrDiff`, `readPrHead`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/pull-list.ts`: the PR reviews board's pull request read (`postPullRefresh` reads every project repository with a GitHub origin, one `refreshPulls` each), which writes each head and commit count it saw, sends a card on Awaiting author whose head moved since its last round back to Incoming (`auto`, named in the sentence, never a closed or merged one), or on to Approved when the person's own opinionated review approved the current head, after the card last moved out of Approved when it ever did (`onward`, from `ownReview` and the card's newest `card_events` row from there) and names the cards pushed to elsewhere without moving them, PR watch on Delivery, and a closed card's pull request state (`cardPulls`), which asks what PR watch asks (`branchAsks`), adds the merger ("you" for the gh login), the open and merge times, the size and the base when gh answers with them, and keeps the link with no state when gh fails. `postPullRefresh` writes the `pullsReadAt` setting once a read finishes. `readCardDiscussions` finds a Delivery card's pull requests by branch, keeps them as found by branch, reads the discussions of every one the card holds and stores them (`storeDiscussions`); `postDeliveryPulls` runs it for each card the board press found a pull request for, one failing card named in the sentence. `attachPull` keeps a pull request a person names (`pullLink`, which also takes a link copied from a tab such as /files) after `getPull` read it, and `detachPull` drops an attached one and refuses one the branch found (`postPullLink`, `deletePullLink`). `postDiscussionsSeen` and `patchDiscussion` answer the PRs tab's seen and Mark done or Reopen writes, local only. Those two, `postCardDiscussions`, `postDeliveryPulls` and a detach bump the board version and tell the loop (`boardTold`), because the board card counts the rows. A pull request whose card was deleted (`deleted_tickets`) is left off. `unlinkedCards` lists the Delivery cards from Review to Wrap-up with no pull request linked, and `linkOpenPulls` asks, in one gh document, for the open pull request of each one whose branch has an upstream (`branchPushed`) and links it as found by the branch with the `pr_` columns. Every branch read stores why the pull request cannot merge on its row. `rereadOpenPulls` reads the open pull requests linked to cards from Review to Wrap-up again by number, one document per twenty, stores the answer, never asks a merged or closed one, keeps the last answer for one GitHub did not answer for, and answers the cards whose answer changed. Key symbols: `refreshPulls`, `onward`, `postPullRefresh`, `cardPull`, `unlinkedCards`, `linkOpenPulls`, `refreshDeliveryPulls`, `postDeliveryPulls`, `branchAsks`, `cardPulls`, `CardPullLine`, `readCardDiscussions`, `postCardDiscussions`, `DiscussionsRead`, `attachPull`, `detachPull`, `postPullLink`, `deletePullLink`, `postDiscussionsSeen`, `patchDiscussion`, `rereadOpenPulls`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/card-pulls.ts`: the browser's read of a closed card's pull request state, `GET /api/card/<key>/pr`, held a few seconds so the card head's "Changes merged" and the Overview share one gh read. `merged` reads every pull request merged, and `pullTrail` is the record's Pull request row, its words each with the time they name ("#42 opened" and ", merged by you", `TimedWords`). Key symbols: `readCardPulls`, `PullsRead`, `merged`, `pullTrail`, `TimedWords`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/clipboard.ts`: copy to the clipboard and say whether it took. Key symbols: `copyText`.

### `lib`: settings screens

- `apps/local/src/lib/settings-sections.ts`: the sections, each at `/settings/<id>` (Project, Engines, Models, Phase instructions, MCP servers, Runs, Integrations, Wall, Cloud), the boxes of Settings, Project in page order with their anchors (`PROJECT_BOXES`), where each old section went, the ids a feature block cannot take (a box's among them), and where a `/settings` address goes. Key symbols: `SECTIONS`, `PROJECT_BOXES`, `sectionOf`, `savesOnChange`, `MOVED`, `movedTo`, `RESERVED_IDS`, `addressFromHash`, `settingsTitle`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/settings-page.ts`: the Runs section and its Knowledge switch, which the Knowledge screen posts too. `storeSetting` checks with `settingProblem` and writes with `writeSetting`. The retry switch posts the default's id when on and nothing when off, which stores no retry. The three retry boxes post `wait1` to `wait3`, each writing its place in the same wait list, which `threeWaits` fills from the defaults (`boxes` is what they show). Key symbols: `agentsFor`, `saveAgentField`, `knowledgeFor`, `saveKnowledgeField`, `storeSetting`, `postSetting`, `postAgents`, `postKnowledge`, `RETRY_CHOICES`, `threeWaits`, `RETRY_BOX`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/SettingsMenu.svelte`: the menu on every settings page, with the quiet "2 found" after Engines (`engines`). On Project it lists the page's boxes under it as anchors, hidden on a phone, and marks the one crossing the top third of the window with `aria-current="location"` (`inView`, from an IntersectionObserver; the first in page order wins, and at the foot of a scrolled page the last box, which a short one never reaches the band). A box picked in the menu stays marked until it reaches the band or the person scrolls by hand (`picked`), so one too near the foot to reach the top is still the one marked. The settings layout sets smooth scrolling on the document while a settings page is open, and not under reduced motion. Tested in `SettingsMenu.mount.test.ts`. Key symbols: `SECTIONS`, `PROJECT_BOXES`, `engines`, `inView`, `picked`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/project-models.ts`: the Models section. `stateOf` reads the rows as they run, the built-in preset on the project's engine (`projectProfiles`), and the preset the project chose: the saved one `savedPreset` names, or, until the screen writes anything (`presetChosen`), a saved one its table matches exactly while it differs from the built-in one, so a built-in picked off a saved preset stays picked. Each row carries what the chosen preset gives it (`base`, in words as `baseText`, the effort left out for an engine that reads it off the id), whether it is the person's own on a built-in preset or differs from a saved one (`changed`), the line under a phase whose preset model the account lacks (`picked`), its picker models, the preset's model and effort on every profile for a move in its engine select (`presetPicks`), and its one warning (a gone model in the card Settings tab's words, `goneProblem`). The picker profiles keep the same engine order (`byEngine`) and mark the vendors that read the effort off the id (`effortInName`). The section adds the engine and preset table (`grid`): a row per engine found here (`EngineRow`: those on PATH, which the menu counts, and any with a login that answered models, in the order Claude Code, Codex, Cursor, `ENGINE_ORDER`, each closed with `why` when it has no login or no models; `foundEngines` is the ones that can take a preset), each with a cell per built-in preset on that engine (`PresetCell`, costed with that engine's main login first, `presetProfiles`, and on an engine that answered its models naming what Implement runs, `uses`), each with the cost of a Delivery card only where runs measured one (`cardCost`) and the one in use marked. The saved presets are apart from the table, in saved order (`mine`, `SavedLine`), each with every engine its phases run on (`runsOn`, in the Engines page order), its card cost and whether it is in use, since a preset that mixes engines belongs to no engine row. It also adds the engine the built-in preset runs on and the count of phases yours or changed. The save takes a built-in preset with or without its engine in one write (a cell of the table sends both), `presetEngine` alone (refused on a saved preset and for an engine not found), `discard` (Back to Nolzu's default on a built-in preset), a saved preset to apply, save (which then is the one in use), rename, `replace` (Update), remove, a reset of one phase to its `base`, an engine with its model and, on a move, the preset's effort (the picker), or one field. On a built-in preset a changed phase is stored whole as the person's choice, and the same rows under `presetChoices` by preset and engine (`writeChoices`, `writePhase`, the state's `key`), so a new built-in list never rewrites it; a pick equal to the preset drops it. The first write after the update takes the stored rows as the person's (`adoptChoices`). A move onto a built-in preset brings back its own choices, and a saved preset's unsaved changes go along (`moveTo`, `differing`); Save as new preset empties the built-in's choices, which went into it. A saved preset holds the engine, model and effort of every phase (`tableNow`). Every write leaves the effort out on an engine that reads it off the id (`fullRow`), so it never counts as a change. `projectSavedPreset` is the saved preset the project is on by the same rule, for the card tab. Key symbols: `modelsFor`, `saveProjectModels`, `projectSavedPreset`, `stateOf`, `moveTo`, `writeChoices`, `writePhase`, `adoptChoices`, `tableNow`, `foundEngines`, `refuseProfile`, `measured`, `PresetCell`, `EngineRow`, `SavedLine`, `sameTable`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/ModelsSection.svelte`: Settings, Models. The Engine and preset table: one `radiogroup` with a row per engine of `grid` (the reason beside an engine that cannot take a preset, whose three cells are then the only closed ones, drawn with a dashed edge and muted text) under the heads Engine, Light, Balanced (the stored `default`, through `presetLabel`) and Heavy, the three heads with fixed dots for cost and strength (`BUILT_IN`, no figure). Each cell is a `role="radio"` submit button in its own small form, posting `preset` with `presetEngine`, with the preset's name, "In use" on the chosen one, the cost of a card where measured (`face`, `costWords` for its name) and what Implement runs (`uses`), so no cell is blank before a cost is known, the line under the table saying once when none is (`anyCost`), with the page's `said` status under it so a pick's answer is in view. The arrows walk the cells and skip closed ones, Home and End go to the ends of a row, and Space or Enter picks (`move`); Tab has one stop (`stop`). Under the table, Your presets lists the saved presets (`mine`), one line each in its own `radiogroup`: a `role="radio"` button posting `apply` with the name (cut with an ellipsis, whole on hover), every engine it runs on, "In use" and the cost (`face`), each line its own Tab stop, so fifteen of them stay a plain list. Each line has Rename (an inline form, `editing`) and Remove in a `details` menu beside it, which Escape shuts (`closeMenu`). Below 760px each engine is a block with its three presets as a segmented control. Then every phase in one table in three groups (`GROUPS`), Assessment left out while `SCOUT_AND_EVIDENCE` is off (`shown`), under the heads Phase, Engine, Model and Effort, each with `ModelPicker` in `columns` (no per-row saved line and no no-models line, `emptyHelp`, and no engine with no models among its options unless the row is on it, so the selects stay narrow), a `HelpTip` with its `PHASE_HELP` line, a changed one tinted, with the Yours mark on a built-in preset (`builtIn`) or Changed on a saved one, Reset on the right (`.act`, which puts the focus on the row's model select after the answer, `focusAfter`, as Back to Nolzu's default and Discard put it on the Phases head) and Nolzu's default or what the saved preset runs under it, a phase whose preset model the account lacks with its `picked` line, and one that will not start red with its warning (the red select is the fix), keeping Reset and its default line when it is yours. While phases are yours, a sticky bar says "Heavy on Cursor, 7 phases yours" (`title`, `changedWord`) with "Back to Nolzu's default" behind a confirm (`confirmDefault`); on a saved preset it says "2 changes from Evenings" with Discard and Update; both have Save as new preset, which shows the name field (`naming`, closed again when the bar goes), a refused name on its own line under it, gone once the name is edited (`nameRefused`). Remove asks first (`confirmRemove`). A pick posts to `?/models` with fetch and reads the answer with `deserialize`. Nothing amber. Key symbols: `ModelPicker`, `HelpTip`, `PHASE_HELP`, `presetLabel`, `BUILT_IN`, `GROUPS`, `title`, `deserialize`, `naming`, `nameRefused`, `editing`, `move`, `stop`, `costWords`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/model-chips.ts`: a step's short name on a preset card's chip (`stepWord`). No screen draws the chips: Settings, Models shows one table of engines and presets. Key symbols: `stepWord`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/engine-profiles.ts`: the Engines section: logins per vendor, each row with its folder, variable, status command and sign-in line; the look at the tools (`engineTools`, version, place and install line); and Check login (`checkProfileLogin`), which runs the tool's status command with the environment a run of that profile gets (`engineEnv`). Each tool row says which phases this machine can run on it and why (`phasesLine`, from `vendorRefusal` and the wall probe; `engineTools` takes the wall and platform for a test), and says nothing when the engine runs every phase inside the wall. The wall itself is named once for the section (`wallLine`, null where it cannot start). Key symbols: `profilesFor`, `saveProfile`, `planNote`, `vendorChoice`, `engineTools`, `ToolRow`, `phasesLine`, `wallLine`, `checkProfileLogin`, `LoginCheck`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/EngineProfiles.svelte`: the Profiles list of Settings, Engines: per row the id, the folder and variable, the limit block with its bars, and Check login beside it, which posts `?/login` through `enhance` and keeps each row's answer while the page is open. Add profile opens the add form with the sign-in line for the folder typed (`signInLine`) and Copy, and a refused add (`refusedAdd`) opens it again. The `HelpTip` beside it (`PROFILE_HELP`) says that a second login of one tool needs a config folder of its own. Key symbols: `profiles`, `check`, `answers`, `refusedAdd`, `signInLine`, `FolderBrowser`, `PROFILE_HELP`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/EngineTools.svelte`: the Tools list of Settings, Engines: found count, the time of the look, Look again, and per tool its version and place, or its install line with Copy, or its install page. Under each tool, its phases line (`data-phases`). While the look runs, the shared `Skeleton` rows in the same grid with the words for screen readers only; the failed line spans the row. Key symbols: `rows`, `onlook`, `copyText`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/sign-in.ts`: the line a person types to sign a tool in to one folder, quoted for a POSIX shell. Key symbols: `signInLine`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/engine-limits.ts`: what each profile last said about its usage windows, with the card whose run read it (`card`, for the near-limit toast). A window at 90% or more keeps one notification per profile, window and reset time, and one with no reset time one per crossing. Key symbols: `limitBlock`, `saveLimit`, `keepLimitsOf`, `LimitState`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/cloud-status.ts`: the words for the hub connection, and the offline banner. `cloudState` is the Cloud section's line: Local only for a Free machine with no account (`LOCAL_ONLY`), Connect again only on `closed` with the cloud board, and Paused with Turn sync back on after Delete my cloud data (`SYNC_PAUSED`). Key symbols: `cloudConnection`, `cloudState`, `LOCAL_ONLY`, `SYNC_PAUSED`, `offlineBanner`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/CloudStatus.svelte`: the Cloud section's state as a pill with its sentence and fix, never amber, and Connect again (a form posting `?/redial`) or Turn sync back on (`?/resume`, when `paused`) where `cloudState` offers it. Key symbols: `status`, `free`, `paused`, `cloudState`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/CloudLinks.svelte`: account and team links. Key symbols: `account`, `team`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/DataSync.svelte`: What goes to a hub, one box at the foot of Settings, Cloud: Sent to your hub and Sent to the team hub from `column`, each row a message type in mono with its sentence, and the frames that carry no data. On a plan without the cloud board it is one line, "No hub: nothing is sent." Never sent from `neverSent` is folded in a `details` on every plan. Key symbols: `canSync`, `team`, `column`, `NO_DATA`, `neverSent`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/data-leaves.ts`: which hub each runner message goes to, in its groups, as a Record so a new message fails the typecheck until it has a column; the team's `card.state` line, which has no runs and no next start. While `SCOUT_AND_EVIDENCE` is off the Scout group and the Scout lines of `NEVER_SENT` are left out (`neverSent`). Key symbols: `column`, `neverSent`, `LeavesGroup`, `TEAM_CARD_STATE`, `NO_DATA`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/confetti.ts`: the ready screen's confetti, seeded: about 120 strips and squares in four colours, each with its sideways travel, the height of its throw, its fall, its drift, its turn and its flips. Key symbols: `confettiPieces`, `ConfettiPiece`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/coach.ts`: closing a coach note into `seenNotes`, the first run's guidance read fresh (the first card and the first drag off their own rows, the rest off settings), and the mark of a step that leaves no row. `personStarted` says whether a person ever started a phase here, which the first drag and the Learn gate read. Key symbols: `closeNote`, `firstRun`, `markStep`, `personStarted`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/start-at-login.ts`: the login item behind a port, written under the home folder by pure writers per system: a LaunchAgent (macOS), a systemd user unit with the `default.target.wants` link `systemctl enable` would make (Linux). Each runs `node <script> --no-open` by absolute paths, or for a copy in npm's `_npx` cache `node <npx> --yes nolzu@latest --no-open` with node's folder on PATH (`loginCommand`), and no system command runs. The item on disk is the whole setting: `loginSwitch` reads it for the Settings switch, On goes through `turnOnAtLogin` and Off through `turnOffAtLogin`, both only from the login-item route. Key symbols: `LoginItem`, `loginCommand`, `launchAgent`, `systemdUnit`, `loginItemPath`, `systemLoginItem`, `turnOnAtLogin`, `turnOffAtLogin`, `loginSwitch`, `LoginSwitch`, `LAUNCH_AGENT`.

### `lib`: projects

- `apps/local/src/lib/projects.ts`: Settings, Project: the one project row, the repositories table apart from the row so the page streams it (`repoTable`, `RepoRow`: remote, base, on disk or Missing with its own Clone; four git calls at a time through `fewAtATime`, so the page's first byte does not wait on them), origin's branches for each base branch box from one `for-each-ref` per repository in `remoteBranches`, apart from the row so the page streams them (`projectBranches`), the found repositories it offers to add (`foundToAdd`), the Jira key clash while its prefix and site are the ones asked (`keyClash`), the confirm of a Remove built from its check pass (`removeConfirm` with what failed in `gone` and what holds in `checked`), run only when the dialog asks for it (`removeConfirmNow`), never on a page load, field saves (a base branch only origin has gets its local tracking branch first, `trackBaseBranch`, and a saved base is written and committed into its manifest entry through `recordInRoot`; one Research switch turns both its kinds, `switchBoard`; the command chips one at a time; the Walls list chips one at a time through `saveWallEntry`, checked by the runner's `wallEntryProblem` against `wallPlaces`), the list's own refusals (`listRefusal`), the folder in the file manager (`revealProject`, worded by `openerWords`), rescan, clone, and the account's project list. Key symbols: `switchBoard`, `projectRow`, `repoTable`, `fewAtATime`, `projectBranches`, `RepoRow`, `remoteBranches`, `foundToAdd`, `saveProjectField`, `listedRepos`, `removeConfirm`, `removeConfirmNow`, `listRefusal`, `removeRefusal`, `removeProjectRepo`, `cardsWithCheckout`, `revealProject`, `openerWords`, `rescanRepos`, `addToList`, `cloneableOrigin`, `projectList`, `addCommands`, `REPO_BASE`, `ADD_WALL`, `REMOVE_WALL`, `wallPlaces`, `saveWallEntry`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/ProjectsList.svelte`: the body of Settings, Project, set in boxes (`PROJECT_BOXES`, each a `section.box` with its name on top and rows inside, name left and value right through `head`, the name on top in a box narrower than 600px by a container query). Each label that needs more carries a `HelpTip` with a few sentences and an example (`HELP`), and a link to the docs page where one exists (Start the app, the wall lists), right after the label's last word; the line under the field is one short sentence. Name and folder: name and location read only, in the same mono field, with the file manager button. Repositories: Scan again in the head, one `InfoBox` of three lines (what Add does, the base branch, that Remove checks first; the details stay in the Remove dialog), the table right of its label (`st table`, the label on top in a box narrower than 900px) with fixed columns (`table-layout: fixed`, a `colgroup`) so it never scrolls sideways, the remote column taking what is left (owner and name without the host through `remotePath`, ellipsis, the full remote as its title), each repository a stacked row in a box narrower than 600px, a row's refusal in a full-width row under it with the git command set as code (`withCommand`), each base branch a select of origin's branches with Save (`baseInput`, a typed box when origin lists none, a skeleton line while `branches` streams), Add a repository with "Or pick one found on this machine" and an Add per remote (`found`, awaited in the markup, a skeleton line until then), the Add answer with "Learn <repo>" for the repository it cloned (posting `learn`), and "Remove the checkouts of closed cards" with their count. Cards and branches: the `setting` rows for key prefix with the next key and branch name with the next branch. Agent: the command chips with Propose again, a note that runs cannot reach Docker under a container command (`needsContainerDaemon`), and "The list is right" while the Confirm the run commands step is open (`commandsConfirmed`), Start the app with one line per repository (and `*` in a project of several) through `QaAppEdit`, each with Ask again (`qaClear`), and Ask again for all, the QA browser row (Set up a browser, off without Chrome; the servers on for QA with Test the browser, busy while it runs through `testingBrowser`, and Turn off; a link to Settings, MCP servers), and the project files with the status line of the notice, the count of files changed by hand and `HandEditedFiles` under the button, which alone is the `files` form (`filesStatus`, `filesLine`, `handLine`, `handEdits`). Boards: the switches. Knowledge: the location. Walls: one line with a link to Settings, Wall, then the three lists as chips through `wallList`. Tickets: one line, the system in words ("Read from Jira at" the host, "Only local cards", or Jira not connected) with Change under it folding open the other choice worded as what a press does, or a link to Settings, Integrations with no login, and the line that local cards work either way. The key prefix row draws the Jira clash warning (`clashLine`) through the `warning` of `setting`, with Use and the free prefix under it. No plan line. The table streams (`table`, a grey row per listed repository until it lands, the count in the head after). Remove asks `/api/remove-check` for the check pass, says it checks meanwhile, and opens `ConfirmTyped` with the findings when they land (`openRemove`, `fresh`), staying off while one failed. The name typed into it is kept per repository (`typedFor`) when it closes without a send. Every form is a full page post without JavaScript and posts in place with it (`inPlace`: no reset, the Add boxes marked `data-clear` empty on success), and the answer is a `settingToast` with no Open link, Saved going by itself after a few seconds, in place of the Saved line at the foot, which only a page without JavaScript draws. Each form's refusal and typed value stay (`kept`, read through `refusal`) when another form saves, until that form's own next answer. Every form posts through `inPlace`, which refuses a second submit of a form on its way and marks its pressed button busy until the answer. The Closed cards hint names what Remove its checkouts keeps: uncommitted changes, unpushed work and ignored files no tool makes again. Key symbols: `ConfirmTyped`, `typedFor`, `openRemove`, `fresh`, `withCommand`, `Switch`, `HelpTip`, `InfoBox`, `HELP`, `head`, `inPlace`, `testingBrowser`, `settingToast`, `kept`, `refusal`, `setting`, `baseInput`, `found`, `onJira`, `jiraConnected`, `clashLine`, `boardForms`, `wallList`, `filesStatus`, `QaAppEdit`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/ProjectFilesNotice.svelte`: the quiet line above every board when Nolzu has newer project files: "Nolzu has newer project files for this project." with Update, which posts to Settings, Project's `files` action (`update`), then says what it did (`done`: how many files to commit, what knowledge moved, and the hand edits with a link to Settings, Project). Knowledge files to move alone also offer Update (`filesOffer`). A folder a newer Nolzu wrote gets its line and no button. Key symbols: `update`, `done`, `offer`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/project-files-notice.ts`: the words of the project files notice, shared with Settings, Project, and the ask before Take the new one, shared with the server. Key symbols: `filesStatusLine`, `filesOffer`, `filesUpdatedLine`, `handEditedLine`, `unsavedLine`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/HandEditedFiles.svelte`: the project files changed by hand on Settings, Project, under one line pointing to phase instructions: each with its diff (`numberedLines`, drawn as the Changes tab draws one), Take the new one and Keep mine. Take on a file git does not hold opens the ask in the row instead of posting (`asking`), and so does the server's refusal with `ask`. Key symbols: `answer`, `asking`, `problem`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/add-repo.ts`: clones a repository from its URL into the project folder and adds it to the list, for Settings, Project and New project. `cloneMissing` clones what the manifest names and the disk has not got, or one row's folder. Clones of one project take turns (`inTurn` from `manifest.ts`), because each commits the manifest. A clone that settles answers with the folder it went into (`added`), so Settings, Project offers Learn this project for it. `startClone` runs one in the background as a `CloneJob` (waiting, cloning with git's progress, added or failed), `cloneEnded` waits for its end, `stopClone` stops it, and `cloneStream` sends the list on every change. `stopAllClones` stops every clone on Ctrl+C and waits for each to remove its folder. `stoppable` wraps the clones Settings, Project starts with no job, so `stopAllClones` reaches them too. Key symbols: `addRepository`, `cloneMissing`, `Clone`, `inTurn`, `projectFolder`, `CloneJob`, `startClone`, `cloneEnded`, `stopClone`, `cloneJobs`, `onCloneChange`, `cloneStream`, `CLONE_STOPPED`, `stopAllClones`, `stoppable`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/folders.ts`: the folder browser. Refuses a path outside the home folder as typed and again once links are resolved, and reads names only. Key symbols: `listFolders`, `FolderList`, `inside`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/remove-repo.ts`: remove a repository from a project in two passes. The check pass (`removeChecks`) finds a card's checkout, a run that can use the repository, work not committed, a stash and commits on no remote, and changes nothing; a commit only a tag reaches counts as pushed when origin has a tag at the same commit, which it asks with `git ls-remote --tags origin`; the page shows it before the typed name. The delete pass (`removeRepository`) runs the checks again in its turn behind a running clone, then wants the typed name, then deletes. The work check also refuses a clone holding files git ignores, named through `ignoredPaths`, folders tools make again left out. The ignored files refusal names them with `somePaths`. Key symbols: `removeChecks`, `RemoveChecks`, `REMOVE_CHECKS`, `removeRepository`, `inTurn`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/to-workspace.ts`: turn a project of one repository into a workspace with one rename, and the coach note on Now that offers it once, until Got it or the move. The copied run recipe's project folder section is renamed for the repository's folder (`recipeMoved`), and the project folder's `repository.md` and its notes at the top of the knowledge folder move into that folder (`repoMoved` for the repository file). Key symbols: `makeThisAWorkspace`, `workspaceNote`, `WORKSPACE_NOTE`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/manifest.ts`: the tracked list of a workspace's clones, read as untrusted, and `inTurn`, which makes every manifest commit of one project (clone, Remove, Scan again) wait for the one before. Key symbols: `MANIFEST`, `parseManifest`, `readManifest`, `manifestWith`, `manifestWithout`, `recordInRoot`, `forgetInRoot`, `inTurn`.

### `lib`: onboarding

- `apps/local/src/lib/terms.ts`: the terms a person accepts on the first start: `TERMS_VERSION` (a date), the words (`TERMS`), what the server hook does before the accept (`termsGate`, `TERMS_REFUSAL`), and the work held until then (`startWhenAccepted`, `acceptTerms`, `workStarted`). The accept lives in the `termsAccepted` machine setting. Key symbols: `TERMS_VERSION`, `TERMS`, `ACCEPT_PATH`, `termsState`, `termsGate`, `afterAccept`, `startWhenAccepted`, `acceptTerms`, `workStarted`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/onboard.ts`: the first run's server side: redirect rule, engine check, opening a folder as the project (no screen calls it since the wizard went), the workspace (at <parent>/<name>, or with `folder` at the project's own folder (a typed link to an empty folder is used and stored as the folder it points to), made with the folders above it when missing and refused when a file or not empty, `folderTarget`; a refused `git init` takes back only the folders made, `removeMade`), the key prefix, the scripts of a `package.json`, a base branch picked from origin's that gets its local tracking branch with no checkout moved (`trackBaseBranch`), allowed commands (`proposedCommands` merges what every repository of `projectRepos` proposes, each command once), and `applySetup`, which writes the project files (the run guard among them) and syncs the phase skills into the three skills folders, and first moves a knowledge folder of the older layout into the folders of its repositories (`moveOldKnowledge`, from `planKnowledgeMoves` in core: renames and one commit, refused while the folder fails `checkKnowledgeDir` (ignored repository folders too), has changes git has not committed, a knowledge change waits or a knowledge run is queued or running (read last, right before the renames), or a link is on the way; never onto a file that is there, which it names; a rename or the commit that fails puts every file back; a refusal also skips `writeRepoKnowledge`, whose new files would block the move). `projectFilesPlan` is what a write would do, for `applySetup`. `projectFilesStatus` is what a write would update and which files are the person's, with no write (the plan, and a dry run of the sync), null for a project Nolzu never wrote skills into; `projectFilesNotice` keeps it per project until `applySetup` clears it, with the count of knowledge files to move and of those that stay because their new place holds a file (`knowledgeMovesPending`). A project file kept with Keep mine for the text this Nolzu makes is not the person's. `handEditedFiles` lists the person's files with git's patch from the shipped text, and whether git holds the person's text (`unsaved`); `takeShipped` writes the shipped text for one of them, refusing with `ask` when git does not hold it and `sure` is not set, and `keepMine` records the keep. Both take only a path the status lists. `applySetup` writes nothing when a skills record names a newer Nolzu. `NOLZU_FILES` are the files Nolzu writes in the project folder, which the Workspace page ticks by default. `NOLZU_VERSION` is this Nolzu's version, read from the app's `package.json`, which the skills record keeps. `writeRepoKnowledge` writes each repository's knowledge file and its run recipe section, for every project shape (one repository too), and commits them; a knowledge folder in no git repository gets the files with no commit. Key symbols: `onboardRedirect`, `engineCheck`, `checkProject`, `saveProject`, `createWorkspace`, `workspaceTarget`, `folderTarget`, `FolderAnswer`, `removeMade`, `storeTickets`, `prefixFree`, `packageScripts`, `fillAllowedCommands`, `proposedCommands`, `trackBaseBranch`, `projectFilesPlan`, `projectFilesStatus`, `projectFilesNotice`, `handEditedFiles`, `takeShipped`, `keepMine`, `NOLZU_VERSION`, `applySetup`, `setupSaid`, `writeRepoKnowledge`, `moveOldKnowledge`, `knowledgeMovesPending`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/new-project.ts`: New project's server side: the project being made (only the newest registered project can be, `newest`), the add that makes the workspace first (`openNewProject`: on a first run in the project with no folder, and for a later project in a new database from `createProject` on every plan, made current once its folder is there and then synced to the hub with `hubProjectsChanged`, and taken back with `dropProject` when refused before that) (with the project files in its first commit, with Research and PR reviews off, Getting started on, Set up the project started empty so Learn holds the first run back, and `NEW_PROJECT_BRANCH_TEMPLATE` unless a template is stored), Make the project, the first task screen's data (`firstTask`: the keys with their prefix and the engine) and its card (`makeFirstCard`), and `endFirstRun`, which the first card, wherever it is made, and Skip call to end the first run, the machine scan with one URL per repository, and the `gh` login. Put on GitHub and a new private repository both go through `toGitHub`: the name check, a repository in a temp folder (a copy of the person's, or a first commit), the workspace, `gh repo create --private`, then a background clone whose failure names the new repository (`madeNotCloned`). `startFromIdea` checks the idea and the owner, makes the private repository that way, puts the idea in the Epics inbox with what is decided as its extra context, and waits for the clone (`cloneEnded`) before it finishes the project. Key symbols: `projectInProgress`, `newest`, `openNewProject`, `NewProject`, `addToNewProject`, `finishNewProject`, `FirstTask`, `firstTask`, `RESEARCH_TASK`, `makeFirstCard`, `endFirstRun`, `machineRepos`, `MachineRepo`, `ghLogin`, `GhLogin`, `putOnGitHub`, `newPrivateRepository`, `toGitHub`, `madeNotCloned`, `GitHubSeams`, `startFromIdea`, `IdeaAnswer`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/first-task.ts`: the next keys the first task screen shows. Key symbols: `nextKeys`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/project-folder.ts`: the Folder field of New project and the idea page, pure for the browser: the folder that follows the name, the folder as the server reads it, and the link that carries both between the two pages. Key symbols: `folderOf`, `shownFolder`, `carried`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/FolderBrowser.svelte`: Browse on New project and the idea page: lists the folders `/api/folders` reads (skeleton rows until the first list lands), and hands back the one picked with "Use this folder". Tested in `apps/local/src/lib/FolderBrowser.mount.test.ts`. Key symbols: `open`, `onuse`, `browse`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/gh-wait.ts`: a page's wait for something done in a terminal: reads every two seconds and stops once an answer is done. `waitForGh` waits for `gh auth login`, and the no-tool block waits for a tool with `waitFor`. Key symbols: `waitFor`, `waitForGh`, `GH_WAIT_MS`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/NoAgentTool.svelte`: New project's block while no engine that runs every phase here is on the PATH (any engine where the wall starts, Claude Code or Codex where it cannot; the server hands `here` from `everyPhaseEngines` and `wallHere`): "Nolzu needs one agent tool to run cards", Claude Code and Codex CLI with their npm install line and Copy, Cursor CLI with its install page, "runs every phase here" on the rows that qualify where the wall cannot start, a neutral box from `missingEngine`, and the Watching line with the last look. Looks on `/api/tools` every two seconds and calls `onfound` once a qualifying engine is there; the look and the clock stop when it unmounts. Key symbols: `SHOWN`, `waitFor`, `onfound`, `copyText`, `ago`.

The pure rules are `packages/core/src/onboarding.ts`: whether a machine with no project yet goes to New project, and the key prefix. The files written into a project come from `packages/runner/src/project`.

### `lib`: the hub link to the cloud

- `apps/local/src/lib/hub-link.ts`: the one place the client dials its hub, from `hooks.server.ts`. One personal socket always, and one team socket per project whose `teamProjectId` is set (a project on no team opens none). The personal socket carries every project the link serves (`servedIds`: every registered one when the display document holds `projects` and `hub-projects`, the first one alone without `hub-projects`, the open one on Free); each has its own `Served` entry with its registration, its board memory per socket, its team socket and its listener, sends its own `project.sync` (`registerProject`) on every connect, and pushes its own board on its loop's change. `serveProjects` serves projects not held yet and dials team sockets, after every job, at start and on `hubProjectsChanged` (New project, once the new project has a folder). A team socket's job runs on its own project, and one naming another project is refused. Runs hub jobs one at a time. Every frame it posts names its project (`Wire.post`, the link's own loop by default); a job runs on the loop of the project it names (`loopFor`: none means the first project, an id the registry lacks is refused, another project opens through `HubLinkDeps.projectLoop` or a late `loop(env, id)`), and answers under that project. After Delete my cloud data (the hub closes with `CLOUD_DATA_DELETED` or today's sentence) the link writes `syncPaused`, closes every socket and holds an idle link (`idleLink`, as in fake mode); a start with the setting on dials nothing, and `resumeSync` (Turn sync back on) clears it and dials again. `redialHub` is Connect again: one dial on a press, only from `closed` and never while a keychain read is still going, waiting for the read so the next load shows it. `hubLastSent` is when a frame last went out. Key symbols: `startHubLink`, `dialHubAgain`, `redialHub`, `hubLastSent`, `closeHubLink`, `hubStatus`, `sendToHub`, `sendToTeamHub`, `registerProject`, `pushBoard`, `pushTeamBoard`, `openTeamHub`, `loopFor`, `HubLinkDeps`, `Served`, `servedIds`, `serveProjects`, `hubProjectsChanged`, `idleLink`, `resumeSync`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/hub-jobs.ts`: the nine server jobs mapped onto the writers a click reaches: `run.start` to `Loop.start`, `question.answer` to `Loop.answer`, `card.move` to `moveTo` (with a note, `moveBackWithNote`: Send back from the web or the phone), `scout.assess` to `Loop.assess`, `report.write`, `knowledge.commit` to `approveKnowledge`, `project.join`, `project.list`, `claim.answer`. Drops a repeat by the per hub job mark (`hubJobDone` on the machine, `teamHubJobDone` in the project the team socket belongs to). Key symbols: `takeHubJob`, `HubJobMark`, `run`, `projectKey`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/hub-sync.ts`: the client half of board sync, evidence days, Scout findings and run costs. Each push sends only rows that changed since the last push on that socket, in chunks. On the personal socket a run carries its plain summary cut to 600 characters and the answers its question offers, and a card carries its next start (`startOf`, read off the loop's snapshot: the next stage for an idle or ready card, its own for a failed one, with the vendor and never the profile id); a triage run (`feedback`) and a Fix (an implement run with a ref in `runs.thread`) go with no summary, question or answers, because they quote pull request comments; the team socket passes no snapshot and no runs, so it gets none of them. Key symbols: `createHubSync`, `HubSync`, `CardStatePayload`, `startOf`, `runCostOf`, `recentDays`, `EVIDENCE_DAYS_ON_OPEN`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/weekly-report.ts`: the week the hub asks for with `report.write`. Key symbols: `weeklyReportFor`, `NO_WEEKLY_WORK`.

### `lib`: knowledge

- `apps/local/src/lib/knowledge-page.ts`: what the Knowledge screen reads: the folder, the change rows, the patch of each change of the open file (`commitPatch`), one `git log` of the folder for the day of every commit and the day each file last changed (`folderDates`), and a waiting change's file against its kept copy, every file's text for the search box, and the folder short, from the project or with `~` (`shortFolder`). The path check asks each repository's base branch for the paths the notes name, one `git ls-tree -t` per repository (`pathsOnBase`); a path in no listed repository or a base git cannot read stays unchecked. It also reads the folder's markdown files for the knowledge index in a run's prompt, each with its description or first line and when it changed (`knowledgeIndexFiles`); `buildJob` gives the index to every phase but the ones that write knowledge and Ask, and names the folder in the job's `knowledgeReadDir`, which Claude Code turns into one read-only rule for the folder.
