- `apps/local/src/lib/knowledge-review.ts`: Approve and Return a held change, Apply all over the runs a post names, oldest first and stopping at the first refusal (`answerApproveAll`), and the kept copy in the data folder. `readHeldNow` is a kept change as Apply writes it now: for a Learn your repositories run, the run recipe is the recipe on disk with that repository's section put in (`withRecipeSection`), so applying one repository never drops the section another one applied. Key symbols: `keepHeld`, `readHeld`, `readHeldNow`, `dropHeld`, `approveKnowledge`, `returnKnowledge`, `answerApprove`, `answerReturn`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/knowledge-revert.ts`: Revert an applied change with a revert commit. Key symbols: `revertKnowledge`, `answerRevert`, `revertable`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/knowledge-optimize.ts`: the Optimize button, `Loop.optimize`. Key symbols: `answerOptimize`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/knowledge-learn.ts`: Learn this project's posts (`startLearnPost` for Knowledge, the first task and the Add answer, `answerLearnPost` for its question) and the question it waits on: the newest run under the reserved key, when it is `orient` and ended with a question (`learnAsking`, with the answers it offered). Learn your repositories' offer (`learnReposOffer`: each repository with whether its file is missing or holds only Nolzu's first lines, whether a result for it waits in Pending review (`waiting`), `repoStubs` over `isRepoStub`, which Set up the project reads too, and the engine, model, effort and estimate of the `learn-repo` row, a Refine run on the same model and effort as `like` while no Learn run is measured, or that row's warning, read off `modelsFor` and `measured`; with the scheduler's queue (`LearnQueue`) each repository a Learn run runs or waits for carries `queued`) and its post (`startLearnReposPost`, one `repo` field per ticked box). ### `lib`: the Workspace page

- `apps/local/src/lib/workspace.ts`: the project folder and each repository of the project (`projectRepos`), by the id the server gives out: `.` for the folder, else the repository's path. The folder's files are marked with `NOLZU_FILES` and leave out the inner repositories, `worktrees` and `.tasks`. A missing folder or one that is not a repository says so and the rest still read. Every request's path is checked against a fresh status and `outsideRepo`. `workspaceCount` is the rail's changed-file count from the last read, kept in memory; the layout reads it without git, and before the first read it starts one without waiting. The push builds its env with `remoteEnv` and stops after `stopAfter`, as the card push does. Key symbols: `workspaceStatus`, `workspaceCount`, `workspacePatch`, `workspaceCommit`, `workspacePush`, `workspaceCommitAsk`, `WorkspaceRepo`, `WorkspaceStatus`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/workspace-page.ts`: the Workspace page's words, pure and with types only from the server side: the sync column (`syncLine`: Up to date, N to push, N behind, No upstream, Detached, Missing), the changed column, each file's letter, the default ticks (Nolzu's files only), the message box's default for a commit of Nolzu files alone, which folders fold into the "N clean" line (`isClean`), and the file list by folder, two path parts deep (`folderTree`). Key symbols: `syncLine`, `changedLine`, `fileMark`, `defaultTicks`, `defaultMessage`, `allClean`, `isClean`, `folderTree`, `NOLZU_MESSAGE`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/WorkspacePage.svelte`: the Workspace page as a git client: the top bar with Check again, the folders as a short list on the left (the workspace folder, then the ones that need a hand, the picked one, and the clean ones folded into "N clean", `listed` and `folded`), the first folder with changes picked on the first read, a refused commit or push as a plain alert, the commit box above the picked folder's "Nolzu files" (with All) and "Your files", each by folder and folded with a tick per folder (`isOpen`, `unfolded`), a `Skeleton` while status or a diff loads, the diff of the open file drawn as the Changes tab draws one (`numberedLines`), the message box, Commit, Run hooks, Push with the confirmation sentence in a `Dialog` first, and "Nothing to commit" when every folder is clean with nothing to push. Reads on mount, on Check again and from the answer of a commit or a push, never on a timer; a rejected push keeps Push off until the next read (`refused`), and a push or a refused commit keeps the message and the ticks (`keep`). Check again clears the last action's line. After a commit or a push that swapped or turned off the pressed button, focus goes to the alert or the status line (`keepFocus`). The "Checked 1 minute ago" words follow a 30 second clock that reads no git. Run hooks shows only when there is something to commit or push. Key symbols: `apply`, `read`, `show`, `commit`, `push`, `keepFocus`, `refused`, `hooks`, `onchecked`, `listed`, `folded`, `isOpen`, `unfolded`.

### `lib`: Scout

- `apps/local/src/lib/scout-sources.ts`: the sources on the Scout page: source forms (each with what it needs and how its keychain entry is named, `SourceEntry`), save, check now, the first visit's paste (`checkFirstPaste`, which makes the pasted text source when there is none), schedule sent to the hub, and the short times of the strip and the inbox rows (`shortWhen`). A source's secret sits under a name by kind, project and id (`sourceEntry`, such as `sentry-recipes-web-7`); `sourceSecret` reads it and moves an older `scout-<id>` entry there on the first read, a save with a new project moves it, and a remove takes both names. Key symbols: `SOURCE_FORMS`, `SourceEntry`, `sourceEntry`, `sourceSecret`, `sourceSettings`, `scoutEntry`, `scoutFor`, `saveScoutSource`, `checkScoutSource`, `checkFirstPaste`, `saveScoutSchedule`, `checkedSentence`, `shortWhen`, `dayKey`, `HubDeps`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/ScoutSources.svelte`: the Scout source dialog on `Dialog`: Add a source with the kinds on the left, opened on a `kind`, or one source from the strip (`row`) with Check now, Save and Remove. The fields, the token with its keychain name as `nolzu/<kind>/<project>` while it is typed, Test the connection (`test`, which posts the unsaved form to `?/test`), and the two "without asking" switches marked not active yet. No cloud schedule. Every press posts with fetch (`send`), so a refusal or a test answer stays in the dialog; a success closes it, reads the page again and hands the answer on (`applyAction`). A remove goes to `/scout` instead, because the finding on show may have gone with its source. A changed field takes a test answer away (`edited`). A close without a save hands the fields and the paste back through the bindable `draft` (never the token), which the Scout page and its first visit keep for the next open of the same source, or of a new one on the same kind (`closed`). Key symbols: `Dialog`, `kind`, `row`, `draft`, `closed`, `send`, `write`, `test`, `edited`, `applyAction`, `scoutEntrySlug`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/scout-connection.ts`: Test the connection: one read with the dialog's form and no database, the typed token or the one entry the form names, with a keychain that refuses every write, timed. Key symbols: `testConnection`, `Probes`, `ConnectionAnswer`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/scout-schedule.ts`: schedule options, pure. Key symbols: `SCHEDULE_FEATURE`, `SCHEDULE_OPTIONS`, `scheduleChoice`, `readScheduleForm`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/scout-findings.ts`: the findings list and its writers: mute, start a card. A row carries its pill (`pillOf`: New, Assessing while its run goes, To decide, or the status word) and "seen 12 times in 2 days" (`seenOften`). The seen marker is the `scoutSeen` setting: `markScoutSeen` moves it on each Scout page load, and `fresh` counts the findings first seen after it. Key symbols: `scoutFindingsFor`, `scoutRow`, `seenOften`, `pillOf`, `assessing`, `markScoutSeen`, `muteScoutFinding`, `answerStart`, `cardsOnBoard`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/scout-detail.ts`: the finding on show in the inbox's right pane, with the read-only run that wrote its assessment (`runLine`). Key symbols: `scoutFindingDetail`, `runLine`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/scout-assess.ts`: reads an assessment back and starts one (`Loop.assess`). Key symbols: `readAssessment`, `ASSESSMENT_HEADINGS`, `answerAssess`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/scout-actions.ts`: the four finding writers the Scout page posts to. Key symbols: `scoutActions`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/scout-page.ts`: what both Scout addresses load, and the seen marker moved on the way, with the loop told so the rail's dot clears. While there is no source it also hands the first visit the owner of the project's GitHub remote as a promise the page does not wait for (`githubOwner`, `remoteOwner`). Key symbols: `scoutPage`, `markScoutSeen`, `remoteOwner`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/scout-check-all.ts`: Check all sources: every source that is on and not pasted text, one after the other through its own check (`checkSource`), with one line for the press and one per source that failed. Also the source save behind the dialog's "Add and check now" (`saveAndCheck`), which reads a new source once only when the add posts `check=now`. Key symbols: `checkAllSources`, `checkSource`, `saveAndCheck`, `Checks`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/scout-tickets.ts`: the ticket query check over the network, and the same read for Test the connection (`ticketsReadable`). Key symbols: `checkTicketQuery`, `ticketsReadable`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/scout-sentry.ts`: the Sentry check, and its read for Test the connection. Key symbols: `checkSentryIssues`, `sentryReadable`, `SCOUT_SENTRY_LIMIT`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/scout-search.ts`: the log query check (Elasticsearch, OpenSearch), and its read for Test the connection. Key symbols: `checkLogQuery`, `logsReadable`, `clusterOrigin`, `clusterAuth`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/ScoutPage.svelte`: the Scout page: the top bar with Check all sources (`data-check-all`), the sources strip at `#sources` with each source's state and the source answers under it, and the inbox (`ScoutFindings`, `ScoutFindingDetail`). A strip entry, its Fix and Add source open the source dialog (`ScoutSources`, on `openRow` or a new source). With no source it draws `ScoutFirstVisit`. Key symbols: `ScoutFindings`, `ScoutFindingDetail`, `ScoutFirstVisit`, `ScoutSources`, `SOURCE_SAID`, `openRow`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/ScoutFirstVisit.svelte`: Scout with no source yet: three source buttons at `#sources`, the GitHub one with "Uses your gh login · <owner>" (`owner`), Paste a log picked, and the paste box posting `?/firstPaste` with ⌘↵; GitHub, Sentry and More kinds open the source dialog (`ScoutSources`) on their kind. Key symbols: `ScoutSources`, `FORM_OF`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/ScoutFindings.svelte`: the inbox's left pane: the findings seen last first, each row a link to `/scout/f/<id>` with its pill, source, how often and when, then `ScoutMemory`. Key symbols: `item`, `ScoutMemory`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/ScoutMemory.svelte`: "Muted (n)" folded at the end of the list, open while a muted finding is on show, each row with Unmute posting to its own page. Key symbols: `item`, `selected`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/ScoutFindingDetail.svelte`: the inbox's right pane: pill, source and title, What Scout saw, the Assessment with its run, and the buttons, which post to the finding's own page (`self`). Key symbols: `self`, `StatePill`.

### `lib`: evidence and timesheets

- `apps/local/src/lib/evidence.ts`: the Evidence screen's day and the standup, from the database and git, and the phase word of each run block, kept off the block because the hub stores the block (`runPhases`). A commit links to a started card by the branch its checkout is on, and to any other card by the template's name. Key symbols: `evidenceFor`, `standupFor`, `runPhases`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/evidence-timeline.ts`: the Evidence day, pure: an axis of whole hours with one lane per card in key order and the work on no card last, moves, commits and runs as shares of the axis, close runs side by side in one group (`GROUP_SHARE`), the now line on today; the Week view's seven days summed (`weekEvidence`) on an axis of seven days (`weekTimelineOf`); the four stat tiles, each saying so where it has no figure, for a day or a week; the day and week labels; each lane's state off the board snapshot. Key symbols: `timelineOf`, `weekEvidence`, `weekTimelineOf`, `dayStats`, `laneStates`, `dayLabel`, `weekLabel`, `dayShift`, `GROUP_SHARE`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/timesheet.ts`: the week grid's rows, and the client, the rate and the currency Evidence asks for (saved together or not at all). `billingPrompt` is true on the first visit of a project with no client and no rate, and stores the visit in `billingAsked`. A deleted card's runs keep their `KEY (deleted)` key as the label. Key symbols: `timesheetFor`, `weekShift`, `billingFor`, `billingPrompt`, `saveBilling`, `Billing`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/timesheet-export.ts`: the exporters: destinations, keychain token, sent rows, one week export. Key symbols: `EXPORT_DESTINATIONS`, `EXPORT_ADAPTERS`, `exporters`, `saveExporter`, `removeExporter`, `postExporter`, `exportEntries`, `exportWeek`, `timesheetSync`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/timesheet-call.ts`: what the four exporters share: one API call, refusal sentences, id checks. Key symbols: `callDestination`, `idSetting`, `requiredId`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/timesheet-toggl.ts`: Toggl. Key symbols: `exportToggl`, `findTogglEntry`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/timesheet-harvest.ts`: Harvest. Key symbols: `exportHarvest`, `findHarvestEntry`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/timesheet-clockify.ts`: Clockify. Key symbols: `exportClockify`, `findClockifyEntry`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/timesheet-jira.ts`: Jira worklogs. Key symbols: `exportJira`, `findJiraWorklog`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/EvidenceDay.svelte`: the day timeline of Evidence: the Timeline head with its card count, "Hours in UTC" and its legend, the week on seven days when `week` is set, the ruler, and one lane per card whose key and title link to its log, with its state dot beside the link, the moves, the commits and the run blocks with their phase word. A block is amber agent work, so it is a mark and no link (`amber.test.ts`), and its name says what the old list printed: the sentence, the time, the duration and the cost (`runSays`). Key symbols: `timelineOf`, `weekTimelineOf`, `week`, `runSays`, `NO_EVIDENCE`, `formatCost`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/TimesheetWeek.svelte`: the week grid in its own sideways scroll, with its Timesheet head, the route's week links (`steps`) and Export CSV, today's column marked, each card linked, and on Free the note that names the trackers Solo sends weeks to. Key symbols: `week`, `csvHref`, `today`, `cardHref`, `steps`, `weekLabel`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/TimesheetExport.svelte`: the export panel. Key symbols: `Exporter`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/StandupComposer.svelte`: the standup as three labelled boxes, Yesterday with its day, Today and Blocked (`standupLists`), edits kept in this browser under the day, and "Nothing is posted from here.". Its `copy` joins the boxes (`standupTextOf`) and is exported for the heading's Copy and the top bar's Copy standup. Key symbols: `standup`, `copy`, `standupLists`, `standupTextOf`.
- `apps/local/src/lib/DatePick.svelte`: a date field, the Go to day in Evidence's top bar on Solo. Its free line shows nowhere since the stepper holds the one Solo line. Key symbols: `canPick`.

## `packages/core`

Pure rules in `src`, with no Node built-ins. Node code only in `src/node`, imported as
`@nolzu/core/node`. Private, like every workspace package.

### Boards, stages, models, settings

- `packages/core/src/index.ts`: the barrel of the pure part. Key symbols: `board`, `scheduler`, `settings`, `models`.
- `packages/core/src/board.ts`: the five boards as data. `deliveryBoard` and `OTHER_BOARDS` list the stages; `boardFor(kind, flow)` is what every caller asks. `canMove` (one forward or any number back, never while queued or running), `phaseOnEnter` (the phase a forward move starts), `advanceTo` (a stage's `advanceOn`), `chainTo` (where a finished run takes its card by itself: one stage on, onto a person's column or the archive, for the run of the stage's own phase that ended ready, QA on a PASS alone, never onto the archive while the run left something waiting), `smallChain` (Plan to Implement on a small card). Key symbols: `deliveryBoard`, `OTHER_BOARDS`, `boardFor`, `canMove`, `phaseOnEnter`, `nextStage`, `startsLabel`, `advanceTo`, `chainTo`, `SMALL_CHAINS`, `smallChain`, `stageSays`, `relatedByParent`.
- `packages/core/src/models.ts`: presets `light`, `default`, `heavy`; the actions that make an AI call, `ask` among them (the vendor's small model at low effort in every preset), and `learn-repo` on the same row; `refusedPhases` (the phases a vendor cannot run here, from `vendorRefusal` over every action) and `everyPhaseEngines` (the engines onboarding accepts: all three where the wall starts, Claude Code and Codex where it cannot); a preset table per vendor; `presetStandIn` (the model a preset model gives way to when the vendor's list here lacks it, by the ranking `STAND_INS`: the same family, then the same tier, then the preset's next step down); `resolveModels` (card over project over preset, against what each vendor offers, a preset model the list lacks replaced by its stand-in and named in `insteadOf`; a card's preset is its own pick or the project's; on an engine that reads the effort off the model id, `effortInId`, a stored effort is read past and the preset's stands); `presetProfiles` (the main login of the project's preset engine first, the profile every row with no engine of its own resolves to). `renamedActions` reads a stored table's old `mockups` row as `design`, for the project's table, the saved presets and a card's own rows. Key symbols: `PRESET_NAMES`, `ACTIONS`, `ModelAction`, `refusedPhases`, `everyPhaseEngines`, `renamedActions`, `presetRows`, `presetModels`, `presetStandIn`, `presetProfiles`, `resolveModels`, `ResolvedTable`, `modelsForPick`, `profileChoices`, `pickerProfiles`, `NO_MODELS_REASON`, `validateModelTable`, `goneText`, `presetNameProblem`.
- `packages/core/src/settings.ts`: every setting key with its type, default and validator. `currentProject` is the project opened last, a machine key. `termsAccepted` is the version and the time of the accepted terms, a machine key. `presetEngine` is the engine a built-in preset runs on, `savedPreset` the saved preset chosen and `presetChosen` whether Settings, Models wrote anything yet, all per project. `presetChoices` holds the phases a person changed on each built-in preset, by preset and engine ("heavy:cursor"), null until the first write after the update. `QaApp` is the answer to how QA gets the app: `dev`, `running`, `code` or `own` (your own Start, Stop and Status). `ticketSystem` is local or jira, with `jiraSite` and `jiraEmail` beside it, machine keys since Settings, Integrations, and `jiraKeyClash`, what Jira last said of the key prefix. `offBoards` keeps the two Research kinds on or off together. `MACHINE_KEYS` are the keys that follow the machine and live in `machine.db`; `teamHubJobDone` is not one, because each team project has its own team socket. `syncPaused` is set after Delete my cloud data and keeps the hub link down until Turn sync back on. `branchTemplate` defaults to `NEW_PROJECT_BRANCH_TEMPLATE` (`{type}/{key}/{desc}`), which New project also stores; a started card keeps the branch its checkout is on. `wallLists` holds the project's three wall lists as typed; only the shape is checked here. `setupSteps` holds the mark of each step of Set up the project, per project, and is null on a project made before the steps, which New project starts empty. `afterLearn` holds the moves Learn and then start waits to make (`AfterLearn`). Key symbols: `Settings`, `SettingKey`, `AfterLearn`, `DEFAULTS`, `NEW_PROJECT_BRANCH_TEMPLATE`, `MACHINE_KEYS`, `isMachineKey`, `validateSetting`, `readSetting`, `isSettingKey`, `projectRepos`, `validateRepos`, `NO_REPOSITORY`, `NOLZU_FOLDERS`, `GETTING_STARTED_STEPS`, `QaApp`, `TICKET_SYSTEMS`, `TicketSystem`, `AGENT_SLOTS`.
- `packages/core/src/phase-policy.ts`: which tools each phase gets, read only or not, knowledge or not, and which vendor cannot run which phase. Every phase that holds Write holds Edit, which the guard holds to the same folders, and `CHANGE_TOOLS` is `Bash`, which a read-only phase holds only with `commands`. `ASK_POLICY` is an Ask run's: Read, Grep and Glob, outside the skills table like the passes. QA reads git (`readGit`, `READ_GIT_COMMANDS`). The four knowledge runs (`knowledge`) are the wrap-up, Optimize, Learn this project (`orient`) and Learn your repositories (`learn-repo`), the last two with writing that always waits for review (`alwaysHeld`). Key symbols: `PHASE_POLICIES`, `policyFor`, `PhasePolicy`, `CHANGE_TOOLS`, `READ_GIT_COMMANDS`, `EXTRACT_SUMMARY_POLICY`, `ASK_POLICY`, `vendorRefusal`, `startsInCardFolder`, `WallState`, `RunPlace`. `vendorRefusal` takes a `RunPlace` (not a git repository, and whether the wall starts, read once on the server from `wallHere`): where the wall starts it refuses nothing but the outside-git rule; where it cannot, every read-only phase of Cursor is refused with the failed check in the sentence, and Codex runs every phase under its own sandbox (QA writes its throwaway copy). `startsInCardFolder` takes the runner's `ownSandbox` answer, true for Codex only where the wall cannot start.
- `packages/core/src/tabs.ts`: the card page tabs per board, and with a stage the ones the card has reached: Delivery's PRs tab from Pull request on (`PR_STAGES`), or earlier on a card that holds a pull request (`pulled`, which `ticketLayout` reads off `card_pulls`). Key symbols: `tabsFor`, `tabAnywhere`, `Tab`, `PR_STAGES`.
- `packages/core/src/threads.ts`: discussion threads per card. An Ask run makes no thread of its own, and neither does a triage (`feedback`), which is a side run of the PRs tab. Each ask discussion takes the session of its own Ask run, which the caller hands in, and so does a PR comment thread (kind `feedback`, `isFeedbackRef`), which takes a message with no session too. Key symbols: `threadsFor`, `ASK_THREAD`, `askThreads`, `canSend`, `sendLabel`, `isDeliveryPhase`, `isFeedbackRef`, `resumeCommand`.
- `packages/core/src/strip.ts`: the phase strip segments. Key symbols: `phaseStrip`.
- `packages/core/src/summary.ts`: cleans a phase summary. Key symbols: `cleanSummary`, `needsExtraction`.
- `packages/core/src/attribution.ts`: the files for other people never say a tool wrote them, pure. `isAttributionLine` is a whole line that names a tool (Claude, Codex, Cursor, Nolzu, AI and the like) and opens with a making verb and with, by or using, or is a co-author trailer or a robot line; `withoutAttribution` takes such lines out with the blank line each leaves and gives any other text back unchanged. Key symbols: `isAttributionLine`, `withoutAttribution`.
- `packages/core/src/output-check.ts`: the output check, pure. Reads a phase's files as text, handed over by path in the card folder, and lists what breaks its skill's promises: a missing file or heading, an empty section, a broken or repeated id, a brief, plan or implement record with no review block, a criterion with no level or `Check:` line, an open question past the third or with no default or `blocks:`, no `Read at:` line, an architecture decision with no `Not:`, an architecture or design question whose `Q` id an earlier phase already gave or that repeats, a plan decision whose `D` id the architecture already gave, an `AC` id the brief does not have (one after another card's key, as `APP-5 AC2`, is that card's), a design screen with no `Address:`, `States:` (with a `- <state> (<ids>):` line) or `Data:` line or a question with no `Q` id, a plan step with no `Check:` line, an implement record with no `Base:` line, no `Deviations:` or `Tests:` line in its review block, a finished step with no `Check:` or `Green:` line or a `Red:` with no `Green:`, or a line `stepsDone` would read as a step, a deviation with no `Why:` or `If wrong:`, a `pr.md` with no `## What changed` or `## How to test`, an Implement run with no `share/SUMMARY.md` or `share/TESTING.md` (`SHARE_FILES`, which QA and the wrap-up correct but are not held to), an epic's Plan with no `share/PLAN.md` or no `## Tasks` in it (a file rule with `board`, which `checkOutputs`, `outputCheck` and `checkedPaths` take; the loop passes the card's board), a criterion no step or check covers, a check that drops its criterion's level, a QA row in no known form, a pass or fail row with no `How:` or `Evidence:` line, a fail with no `Reason:`, `Expected:` or `Actual:`, a not reached row with no `Reason:`, an evidence file with capital letters, a PASS beside a `must` criterion that did not pass or that no row checks (a criterion with no level counts as must) or beside a failed project check, section 1 of the QA report held to the review block rules, a review round `parseRound` refuses, a Research or Investigate answer that does not open with its sentence and a `Confidence:` line or lacks Why, Searched and not found or (Investigate) Hypotheses tried with a `Result:` on each, a card draft (`## If this becomes a card`) with no `Problem:` or `Change:` line or with criteria and questions out of the brief's shape, an assessment cause with no `Confidence:` or `What would change it:` line or a heading the screen would split at, a review round's `summary.md` whose `Verdict:` or `Findings:` do not follow the newest round as `parseRound` reads it, with no `Not reviewed:` line, or with no `Earlier issues:` line on a re-review, placeholder text, a review block over 15 lines, a main file over 300 lines, and, for a wrap-up or Optimize (`knowledge` on the phase's rules), a note the run wrote with no frontmatter `description:` or with a path in `paths:` that no base branch can hold (`.tasks/`, absolute, `~` or `..`), or, for a note the run made (`created`), outside the folder its paths belong in by the project's repositories (`misplaced`, with `noteFolder` from knowledge.ts), and for Learn this project (a phase's own `notes` rule) more than three notes, more than about 60 lines in its own notes, or a line in them that does not end with `(from <file>)` or `(told by the person)`; the repository files and the run recipe keep their own shape. `OUTPUT_RULES` is the table per phase that the format tasks extend. `gateFile` names the main file of a phase that opens with a review block (Refine, Architecture, Design, Plan, Implement, and QA's section 1), and `gateOf` reads its review block lines and its questions marked `blocks: yes` with a `Q` id (the brief's Open questions, the architecture's What is still open, a design screen's `Question:` line); a file from before the block gives none. Key symbols: `OUTPUT_RULES`, `SHARE_FILES`, `checkOutputs`, `outputCheck`, `checkedPaths`, `OutputCheck`, `OutputProblem`, `gateFile`, `gateOf`, `Gate`.
- `packages/core/src/merge.ts`: why a pull request cannot merge, pure. `mergeBlockers` reads the merge fields of a GitHub pull request node (`MergeFields`: `mergeable`, `mergeStateStatus`, `reviewDecision`, `isDraft`, the check rollup with the failed checks by name and link) into a `MergeRead`, in the chip's order; running checks are a state, and a BLOCKED nothing explains says GitHub does not name the reason. `WATCHED_STAGES` are the Delivery columns where a card's pull request is watched (Review, Pull request, Wrap-up). Key symbols: `mergeBlockers`, `MergeFields`, `WATCHED_STAGES`.
- `packages/core/src/estimate.ts`: cost and time estimate from past first runs. Key symbols: `historyEstimate`, `estimateOf`, `quantile`.
- `packages/core/src/onboarding.ts`: the first run: the redirect of a machine with no project yet, whether the first run ended with its first card (an old wizard row reads as its flag), key prefix (never a Research kind's, `researchKindOf`), the start command proposal (`proposeStart`, from `START_SCRIPTS`, which the run recipe reads too), and what the question at the first QA reads: an app address, Playwright in a repository, the port a start script names. Key symbols: `START`, `START_SCRIPTS`, `Progress`, `needsOnboarding`, `validateProgress`, `proposePrefix`, `nextKey`, `prefixProblem`, `proposeStart`, `appAddress`, `usesPlaywright`, `scriptPort`.
- `packages/core/src/allowed-commands.ts`: the shell commands a project lets the agent run. `allowedCommandProblem` refuses a wildcard in a variable set in front (`PORT=* npm *`) and a variable with no command after it. `lockfileOf` names the lockfile and its tool, and `scriptCommand` how that tool runs a script (`bun run test`, never `bun test`), for the list and the run recipe. `stackCommands` is one pass over `STACKS`, one row per stack or framework detected by its files at the top (`DEEP_FILES` are the few below it, such as `bin/console`); rows that share a group take the first match, and `{run}` is the Python row's runner. Each command carries its recipe step (`StackStep`) and the file that matched, because the run recipe reads the same table. `proposeAllowedCommands` reads one repository: the Node commands from its `package.json` scripts, then every `stackCommands` command but Start. `needsContainerDaemon` says whether a command starts docker, docker-compose, podman or nerdctl, whose socket the wall closes. Key symbols: `proposeAllowedCommands`, `stackCommands`, `StackCommand`, `StackStep`, `STACKS`, `DEEP_FILES`, `lockfileOf`, `scriptCommand`, `parseAllowedCommands`, `allowedCommandProblem`, `needsContainerDaemon`.
- `packages/core/src/knowledge.ts`: where knowledge lives (`.nolzu/knowledge` by default), grouped by repository: a repository's folder in it (`repoKnowledgeFolder`, empty for the project folder's own repository) holds its `REPO_FILE` and its notes, and notes about several repositories go to `SHARED_FOLDER`, as `noteFolder` reads off a note's paths (`repoOfPath` for each); the first file of each repository (its folder, base branch and an empty purpose line, no stack and no folders) and the run recipe `RUN_RECIPE_FILE`: a section per repository with the install, test, lint, type-check, build and start command read from its `package.json` and lockfile, each with its source, then every other stack's commands from `stackCommands` (the allowed commands' table), each a first guess with the file it came from, "Not read" for a step nothing gives, and a Catches line. `runRecipe` adds only the sections of repositories the file has no heading for, and `recipeMoved` renames the project folder's section when Make this a workspace moves it, as `repoMoved` does the lines of its repository file. `isRepoStub` tells a repository file with only those first lines (`REPO_STUB_LINE`). `recipeSectionOf` reads one repository's section, from its heading (`recipeHeading`) to the next, and `withRecipeSection` puts one in place of the old one or adds it at the end, for Learn your repositories. Key symbols: `resolveKnowledgeDir`, `REPO_FILE`, `repoKnowledgeFolder`, `repoKnowledgePath`, `SHARED_FOLDER`, `repoOfPath`, `noteFolder`, `planKnowledgeMoves`, `repoKnowledge`, `REPO_STUB_LINE`, `isRepoStub`, `repoMoved`, `RUN_RECIPE_FILE`, `RecipeFacts`, `runRecipe`, `recipeMoved`, `recipeHeading`, `recipeSectionOf`, `withRecipeSection`.
- `packages/core/src/repositories-line.ts`: reads the repositories line refine and plan write. Key symbols: `repositoriesLine`.

### Queue, scheduler and run fold

- `packages/core/src/queue.ts`: the queue as a plain value. Slots, one job per card of a project, and the lanes `stack`, `knowledge`, `scout` with one job each, shared by every project. A run is named by its run id and its project, since both repeat across projects; a caller with one project leaves the project out. Key symbols: `LANES`, `createQueue`, `enqueue`, `pick`, `promote`, `remove`, `waitFor`, `QueueState`.
- `packages/core/src/scheduler.ts`: the only stateful piece. Holds the queue and the jobs, calls `startJob` and `onChange` ports, frees the slot only when a run has ended. A second stop of a run already ending does nothing. `resize` takes a slot count saved in Settings at once and stops no run. `stop` and `promote` name the project too. Key symbols: `createScheduler`, `Scheduler`, `SchedulerPorts`, `resize`.
- `packages/core/src/run.ts`: folds a run's events into a `RunRecord` and the card state. A stop makes the card idle, unless a check after the run halted it, which stays failed. Key symbols: `foldRun`, `stateAfterRun`.

### Scout (pure)

- `packages/core/src/scout.ts`: normalize, fingerprint, dedup. Key symbols: `normalize`, `fingerprint`, `dedup`, `ScoutGroup`, `ScoutItem`.
- `packages/core/src/scout-paste.ts`: the pasted text source. Key symbols: `fetchPaste`.
- `packages/core/src/scout-tickets.ts`: the ticket query plan and its answer. Key symbols: `ticketPlan`, `fetchTickets`, `ticketKeyOf`.
- `packages/core/src/scout-search.ts`: Elasticsearch and OpenSearch request and answer. Key symbols: `searchRequest`, `fetchSearch`, `SEARCH_LIMIT`.
- `packages/core/src/scout-sentry.ts`: Sentry issues as findings. Key symbols: `fetchSentry`, `SentryIssue`.
- `packages/core/src/jira-site.ts`: a Jira site address and its auth header. Key symbols: `siteOrigin`, `basicAuth`.
- `packages/core/src/jira-tickets.ts`: what a Jira site is asked and how its answer reads. `ASSIGNED_JQL` is the query for Add card's Your tickets. Key symbols: `ASSIGNED_JQL`, `searchPath`, `ticketFrom`, `readIssue`, `tokenRefused`, `refusalSays`, `JIRA_TOKEN_REFUSED`, `accountRefused`.

### The database layer (`src/node`)

- `packages/core/src/node/index.ts`: the barrel of `@nolzu/core/node`. Key symbols: `cards`, `db`, `migrations`, `runs`, `snapshot`.
- `packages/core/src/node/data-dir.ts`: `~/.nolzu`, or `NOLZU_DATA_DIR`, or `~/.nolzu-fake` in fake mode. Key symbols: `resolveDataDir`.
- `packages/core/src/node/db.ts`: opens `nolzu.db` (WAL, foreign keys, busy timeout), runs migrations by `PRAGMA user_version`, the `seq` counter, the board version and backups. `openDatabase` migrates `machine.db` first, on a connection of its own (`openMachine`), so one newer than the code refuses before `nolzu.db` is touched, then attaches it as `machine`; `machineAttached` says a connection has it, so one made by hand keeps every setting in its own table. The project registry is `registeredProjects` and `registerProject`, on either connection; `openProject` opens a registered project by its id, and `projectFiles` is where its run files go (the data folder for `FIRST_PROJECT`, `projects/<id>` for every other). A project's file always resolves inside the data folder (`insideDataDir`), and any other name is refused before it opens. `createProject` makes a later project's database at `projects/<id>.db` under the next id, writing the registry row through `openMachine` (it opens the first project only to adopt it into an empty registry), so a damaged first project does not stop New project, `dropProject` takes back one New project made nothing in, and `projectOf` names the data folder and the project of a connection. While it is empty, the boot adopts `nolzu.db` as the first project where it lies and copies its machine keys, in one write (`adoptFirstProject`). A key that left `MACHINE_KEYS` (`teamHubJobDone`) moves from `machine.db` back into the first project once (`takeBackProjectKeys`). `migrate` copies an existing database with `backup` into `backups/<name>/` beside it before the first step. Key symbols: `openDatabase`, `openMachine`, `machineAttached`, `adoptFirstProject`, `takeBackProjectKeys`, `FIRST_PROJECT`, `openProject`, `projectFiles`, `insideDataDir`, `createProject`, `dropProject`, `projectOf`, `migrate`, `nextSeq`, `bumpVersion`, `boardVersion`, `backup`, `RegisteredProject`, `registeredProjects`, `registerProject`.
- `packages/core/src/node/migrations.ts`: the schema as a list of SQL strings, run once each, in order, and `machineMigrations`, the list of `machine.db` with its own version. Append only. The newest adds `deleted_tickets`, the tickets a person deleted, which Read pull requests leaves off. Key symbols: `migrations`, `machineMigrations`.

The migrations in order:

1. Tables `projects`, `cards` (key, board, stage, state, title, summary, extra context, models, minimized), `card_events` (stage moves), `runs`, `settings`, `seq`.
2. Run columns for state, lane, start and end, question, attempt, plain summary and four token counts (drops `tokens`); `cards.source` and `source_ref`; table `card_links`.
3. Table `writer`, one row, the writer lock per data folder. Dropped by 32.
4. Table `messages`, the discussion.
5. Table `knowledge_changes`, one row per file a run touched.
6. Run columns `verdict`, `verdict_reason`, `stack_state`, `stack_reason`.
7. Rebuilds `knowledge_changes` with a `note` and the `returned` status.
8. Card columns `stack_state`, `stack_reason`.
9. Table `scout_sources`.
10. Table `scout_findings`.
11. Card columns `pr_author`, `pr_decision`, `pr_head_at`, `pr_draft`.
12. `scout_findings.external_key`.
13. `scout_sources.schedule_minutes` and `notify`.
14. `cards.pr_state`.
15. Moves Delivery cards off the removed merge column to `pr-watch`.
16. Card columns `repos`, `repos_by`.
17. `runs.cwd`, where a session was made.
18. `knowledge_changes.moved_from`.
19. Tables `exporters` and `export_entries`, the timesheet exports.
20. Rekeys GitHub issue cards from `owner/repo#n` to `repo#n` in every table.
21. The same for GitLab issue cards Scout started.
22. Run columns `resumed`, `asked_model`.
23. `runs.question_options`.
24. `cards.size`.
25. `runs.chain_refused`.
26. Run columns `retry_reason`, `retry_max`, `retry_until`.
27. `card_events.reason`, the line Accept anyway sends.
28. Table `diff_comments`, a person's comments on lines of a card's diff, with the line's text and the commit they were made on, and `sent_at` for the send back.
29. `runs.thread`, the ask discussion an Ask run answers in.
30. `cards.preset`, the preset a card follows when it is not the project's.
31. Card columns `pr_seen_sha`, `pr_seen_commits` (the head and commit count the last read saw), `pr_head_sha`, `pr_head_commits` and `pr_round` (the head and the round of the last review).
32. Drops `writer`: the lock moved to `machine.db`, and the name on a project connection now reads that one.
33. `cards.title_by`, who set the title after the card was made: `refine` or `person`.
34. `card_links.step`, the plan step an epic's task was made from.
35. Moves an epic on `mockups` to `design`, with its moves, runs (`phase`) and discussion thread.
36. `runs.in_card_folder`, whether a run's process started in the card folder.
37. `runs.read_only_break`, what the git compare after a read-only run found (JSON, `ReadOnlyBreak`).
38. `runs.wall`, the wall that held the run (`WallKind`).
39. `cards.branch`, the branch name a person typed on the card page, which wins over the template.
40. Table `notifications`, what the bell keeps: the event's own key as the id, its time, kind, card, text, address and when it was read.
41. `runs.output_check`, what the output check found in the files a finished run wrote (JSON, `OutputCheck`).
42. `cards.saved_preset`, the project's saved preset a card follows, by name.
43. Tables `card_pulls` (a Delivery card's pull requests, found by branch or attached, with the title and state the last read saw and when its comments were read), `pr_discussions` (one per review thread, review body or issue comment, with its ref, status, verdict, fix note, fix time and why the last fix failed) and `pr_messages` (each message once per discussion by its GitHub id, with its link, mine and unread).
46. Table `thread_subjects`, an Ask discussion's subject and whether a person gave it (`by_person`).
50. `card_pulls.merge` and `merge_at`: why a card's pull request cannot merge as the last read saw it (JSON, `MergeRead`), and when.

The machine migrations in order:

1. Tables `settings` (the machine keys) and `project_registry` (one row per project database).
2. Table `writer`, the writer lock of the machine, one row.

- `packages/core/src/node/cards.ts`: every card write. `cardPreset` is the built-in preset a card's rows follow and `cardBase` the table they sit on (the saved preset the card follows, `cardSavedPreset`, else the project's rows), both read by every resolver of a card's models; `followPreset` and `followSavedPreset` move a card onto one and clear its own rows in the same write (a pick of the project's built-in preset is stored as following the project, unless the project is on a saved preset), `cardFollowsProject` is a card that picked neither, and `renameCardPresets` carries a rename or a removal on Settings, Models to the cards on that preset. `epicOfTask` is the epic a Delivery card is linked to, which a task's run reads. `createLocalTicket` numbers a Research kind under its own prefix (`keyPrefixFor`), and cuts typed text to its first line with the whole text kept as the description. `setCardTitle` writes a title from Refine (local cards only, never over a person's, the old title into an empty description) or from a person, with `title_by`. `moveCard` checks under `BEGIN IMMEDIATE`, writes the move (`recordMove`, the one writer of `card_events`, with the line of reason Accept anyway sends), sets the card queued or idle, bumps the version, commits, then calls the injected `startRun`. A pull request card's head as the last read saw it is `seenHead` (`PullHead`), and `savePullRound` keeps a review's round with the head it read. `deleteCardRows` is Delete card's database part in one transaction: it refuses while a run is queued or going (the caller asks `appHeld` right before), records a ticket card in `deleted_tickets` (which `createCard` clears for a ticket added again), moves the runs and knowledge changes to `deletedKey`, drops the bell rows and the card row with its cascades, and answers the run logs to remove. Key symbols: `deleteCardRows`, `deletedKey`, `moveCard`, `recordMove`, `allCardMoves`, `setMinimized`, `boardOfCard`, `createCard`, `peekLocalKey`, `createLocalTicket`, `linkCards`, `relatedCards`, `unlinkCards`, `setExtraContext`, `cardModels`, `setCardModels`, `cardPreset`, `cardSavedPreset`, `cardBase`, `cardFollowsProject`, `followPreset`, `followSavedPreset`, `renameCardPresets`, `cardRepos`, `setCardRepos`, `reposOfCard`, `cardStack`, `setCardStack`, `setCardTitle`, `StartRun`, `seenHead`, `savePullRound`, `PullHead`.
- `packages/core/src/node/runs.ts`: every run row write and read. `NOT_A_PASS` leaves the passes and Ask runs out of a card's newest run. `cardHolds` is what keeps a finished wrap-up short of the archive, as the sentence Now shows: a pending knowledge change of any run of the card, then output problems on its newest run. `saveRun` keeps the engine's own run time, or counts it from the start to the end where the engine reported none, as Codex does. `readOnlyBreakOf` also says whether the run was walled, from the row's `wall`. `otherRunOfCard` is another queued or running run of the card, or, with `since`, one that ended at or after that time; `clearReadOnlyBreaks` drops the marks of a card's earlier runs. Key symbols: `clearReadOnlyBreaks`, `otherRunOfCard`, `cardHolds`, `startRun`, `markRunning`, `saveRun`, `saveVerdict`, `saveQuestionOptions`, `saveStackStatus`, `savePlainSummary`, `retryWait`, `readRun`, `cardRuns`, `boardRuns`, `allRuns`, `sessionFolder`, `sessionInCardFolder`, `saveReadOnlyBreak`, `readOnlyBreakOf`, `saveCommitWarning`, `commitWarningOf`, `saveOutputCheck`, `outputCheckOf`, `parseOutputCheck`, `NOT_A_PASS`, `ENDED`. The `output_check` column holds the stored `OutputCheck` as JSON, null on a run that was not checked. The `wall` field of `StoredRun` is the `wall` column (`WallKind`), null on a row made before it. `sessionInCardFolder` says whether a session's process started in the card folder (the `in_card_folder` column), and answers a row made before the column with the old Codex rule, so an old run resumes where it started.
- `packages/core/src/node/snapshot.ts`: builds the `BoardSnapshot` from the database, the queue and the offered models. The queue is every project's: the slots, the lanes and a run's place count them all, and only the runs of `project` mark this board's cards. A lane held by another project's card carries that project (`LaneView.project`). An epic's tasks are its linked Delivery cards (`epicTasks`), with their state and the plan step the link keeps; in In delivery the steps of its plan not added yet count too (`planned` on the input, matched with `matchPlan`), and an epic there with every task done is `finished` (`deliveredOf`). A pull request card carries its round and the commits pushed since that round read the head (`newCommitsOf`). A card's `totalUsd` is `spendOf` over all its runs, passes and Ask included, the same sum as the card page. `outputProblems` holds the problems the output check stored on the newest run, left out at none and on a closed card (`outputProblemsOf`). A Delivery card's `feedback` is `feedbackCounts`, counts only, left out at none and on a closed card; the hub frame is built apart and never carries it. `SnapshotInput.wall` is the wall probe the loop hands in, so a drop's refusal matches the spawn. A wrap-up held in `waiting_review` reads `waiting_lane` on the knowledge lane while no knowledge change is pending, so it is the person's only while one is (`shownRunState`, which the card page's trail, the peek and Now's run rows read too). A Delivery card from Review to Wrap-up carries why its open pull requests cannot merge (`blockers`, from `mergeBlockersByCard`). Key symbols: `boardSnapshot`, `shownRunState`, `SnapshotInput`, `epicTasks`, `deliveredOf`, `newCommitsOf`.
- `packages/core/src/node/settings-store.ts`: reads and writes settings rows through the validators, a machine key in `machine.settings` and every other key in the project's table. `machineRow` is the upgrade rule: a machine key `machine.db` lacks is copied once from the project's row, which is never written again. A write bumps the board version. `projectProfiles` is the profile list every resolve of the project's rows takes, the preset engine's main login first, read by the board, the card, the run, Run on, a discussion, Settings, Models, the rail's usage foot and the first task. Key symbols: `getSetting`, `projectProfiles`, `writeSetting`, `readSettings`, `machineRow`, `settingProblem`, `projectOutsideGit`.
- `packages/core/src/node/recovery.ts`: the writer lock and the sweep of runs a crash left open. The lock row is in `machine.db`, which a project connection reads as `writer`, so a second server is refused whatever project it opens. The sweep is per project database. It sets a PR comment row still fixing back to new with the reason, and a card whose only cut off run was a Fix goes back to idle, not failed. A holder whose process started after the lock row was written is a reused number and is taken over (`processStart`, from `ps`). Key symbols: `startWriting`, `takeWriter`, `releaseWriter`, `recoverRuns`, `processRunning`, `processStart`.
- `packages/core/src/node/estimates.ts`: estimate figures per project database, over all first runs of every card (no card has a size to pick), keyed by the vendor of the run's engine profile, the phase, the model and the effort. The preset fallback applies only to the vendor it was measured on (`presetRange`). `cardEstimate` adds up one figure per step of a card, or gives none when one step has too few runs. Key symbols: `startEstimate`, `cardEstimate`.
- `packages/core/src/node/spend.ts`: the cost roll-ups. Key symbols: `spendReport`.
- `packages/core/src/node/knowledge-changes.ts`: knowledge change rows: record, pending, apply, return, revert. Key symbols: `recordKnowledgeChanges`, `pendingKnowledgeChanges`, `allKnowledgeChanges`, `applyKnowledgeChanges`, `returnKnowledgeChanges`, `returnedKnowledgeNote`, `revertKnowledgeChange`, `knowledgeChangesOfRun`.
- `packages/core/src/node/scout-sources.ts`: source rows. The secret is never here. Key symbols: `scoutSources`, `scoutSource`, `addScoutSource`, `setScoutSource`, `removeScoutSource`, `finishScoutCheck`, `clearScoutCursor`.
- `packages/core/src/node/pr-discussions.ts`: a Delivery card's pull requests and the discussions on them. `storeDiscussions` keeps one read: refs in order of first message, each message once, an edited one takes its new text and is unread again unless it is the viewer's, the viewer's never unread, a newly resolved thread done (after the fix when one runs), a reviewer message after `fixed_at` reopens a fixed one, and nothing else touches a status. `detachCardPull` drops an attached one with its discussions and messages, keeps its highest ref in `seq` so no later discussion takes it, and leaves one the branch found or one a fix runs on. A read stamps the pull request's `read_at` and keeps each message's GitHub link. `markDiscussionsSeen` clears a card's unread marks, and `markDiscussion` is the person's done (status done, override cleared) or reopen (done back to new, override set), refused while a fix runs. `applyTriage` writes a triage's verdict, summary, reason and reply onto the open rows only and never a status. `beginFix` sets a row fixing and clears its last failure, refused for a done row, a ref the card does not have and while any row of the card is fixing; `endFix` sets a fixing row fixed with its note and time, or new with `fix_failed`. `cardDiscussions` gives a row still fixing on a blocked card `fixAsks`, the question of the newest implement run that names its ref. `feedbackCounts` answers, per card and in one query, the rows with verdict fix that are new or fixing and the rows with an unread message, for the board snapshot; it carries no text. `saveCardPull` also keeps a merge read with its time (`merge`, `merge_at`), and a save without one keeps the last; `mergeBlockersByCard` answers the reasons of every card's open pull requests in one query, in the chip's order. Key symbols: `feedbackCounts`, `applyTriage`, `beginFix`, `endFix`, `saveCardPull`, `cardPullRows`, `detachCardPull`, `storeDiscussions`, `markDiscussionsSeen`, `markDiscussion`, `setDiscussionStatus`, `cardDiscussions`, `DiscussionInput`, `mergeBlockersByCard`.
- `packages/core/src/node/notifications.ts`: the bell's rows. `addNotification` keeps one row per event key and drops all but the newest `NOTIFICATIONS_KEPT` (200). Key symbols: `addNotification`, `notifications`, `unreadNotifications`, `markNotificationsRead`, `NOTIFICATIONS_KEPT`, `NotificationItem`.
- `packages/core/src/node/scout-findings.ts`: finding rows, mute, assess, start a card. A save that opened a new finding keeps one notification, "Scout: 2 new findings from" the source. Start writes source `jira` for a ticket of a Jira source, so the Jira worklog export logs it, and `external` for the others. Key symbols: `saveScoutFindings`, `scoutFindings`, `scoutFinding`, `dismissScoutFinding`, `unmuteScoutFinding`, `assessScoutFinding`, `startScoutFinding`, `canStartFinding`.
- `packages/core/src/node/scout-check.ts`: one check per source kind, with the fetch injected. Key symbols: `checkPasteSource`, `checkTicketSource`, `checkSentrySource`, `checkSearchSource`.

## `packages/protocol`

Types and pure functions both sides read. Imports nothing.

- `packages/protocol/src/index.ts`: the barrel. Key symbols: `board`, `hub`, `snapshot`, `display`, `package`.
- `packages/protocol/src/board.ts`: board kinds and labels, the two kinds the Research board holds with their labels, report files and key prefixes, a board as a person picks it (`BoardChoice`, Research once for its two kinds), card states and sizes, delivery phases, a line of help per model row for Settings, Models (`PHASE_HELP`), and the rest of each phase's help on Settings, Phase instructions (`PHASE_INSTRUCTION_HELP`), the preset names people read (`presetLabel`, and `presetTitle` for "Heavy on Cursor", which Settings, Models and a card's Settings tab share), passes, the Ask run's phase, `Stage`, `Board`. Key symbols: `BOARD_KINDS`, `BoardKind`, `isBoardKind`, `BOARD_LABELS`, `RESEARCH_KINDS`, `ResearchKind`, `isResearchKind`, `RESEARCH_KIND_LABELS`, `RESEARCH_PREFIXES`, `keyPrefixFor`, `BoardChoice`, `choiceOf`, `choiceLabel`, `choicesOf`, `CARD_STATES`, `CardState`, `CARD_SIZES`, `isCardSize`, `DELIVERY_PHASES`, `DeliveryPhase`, `PHASE_LABELS`, `PHASE_HELP`, `PHASE_INSTRUCTION_HELP`, `PASSES`, `ASK_PHASE`, `phaseLabel`, `presetLabel`, `presetTitle`, `Stage`, `Board`.
- `packages/protocol/src/snapshot.ts`: what the stream sends: `BoardSnapshot` (version, kind, flow, stages, cards, slots, lanes) and `CardView`, `StageView` with what a drop starts. An epic's `CardView` carries its tasks (`EpicTask`) and `finished`, and a card Learn and then start holds carries `afterLearn`; `matchPlan` pairs each step of an epic's plan with the task it became (by the step the link kept, else by title), for the board's count and the epic page's table alike. `MergeBlocker` is one reason a pull request cannot merge and `MergeRead` one read of them with whether checks are running; `CardView.blockers` carries a Delivery card's. `actionsJob` reads the run and job ids of a failed check's GitHub Actions job link in the pull request's own repository, and null for any other link. Key symbols: `BoardSnapshot`, `CardView`, `StageView`, `StartsDescriptor`, `cardStarts`, `LaneView`, `SlotMeter`, `RunActivity`, `PullView`, `EpicTask`, `matchPlan`, `checkWords` (the output check problems in a few words for the board card's chip, and in full), `MergeBlocker`, `MergeRead`, `actionsJob`, `ActionsJob`.
- `packages/protocol/src/run.ts`: the job and the engine neutral run events. `Job.qaCopy` is the throwaway copy a QA run starts in. `Job.mcp` (`McpRun`) holds a run's MCP servers with their secret values in memory, filled when the run starts, with their `McpRule`s and the run's folder; `Job.mcpConfigPath` is Claude Code's config for one attempt. `Job.walls` carries the project's `WallLists`, put on every job by the loop's `adapter`. `ReadOnlyBreak` is what the git compare after a read-only run found, kept on the run row. A tool result carries its size and, for a text answer, its lines (`lineCount`). `NEAR_LIMIT` is the 90% line of the board's warning and the server's notification, and `shortReason` the first line of a reason that a toast and a notification show. Key symbols: `Job`, `WallLists`, `RunEvent`, `RunRecord`, `RunState`, `Effort`, `RunTokens`, `atLimit`, `tokensSoFar`, `lineCount`, `NEAR_LIMIT`, `shortReason`.
- `packages/protocol/src/hub.ts`: the wire to the hub: protocol version, paths, close codes (`TOKEN_REFUSED`, `PROTOCOL_REFUSED`, `PLAN_REFUSED`), heartbeat words, the job names the server sends and the frame names the runner sends, the envelope (with the optional `project`, a registry id; left out, the first project), and a reader per frame. `SYNCED_DATA` and `NEVER_SENT` are what What goes to a hub on Settings, Cloud prints, and the site's security page copies. `capSummary` cuts a plain summary to `SUMMARY_CAP` (600) on a word, and `CardStart` is a card's next start on `card.state`. `CardMoveJob` is a `card.move` payload, whose optional `note` is a send back. `CLOUD_DATA_DELETED` (4410) is the close after Delete my cloud data, and `isCloudDataDeleted` reads it or today's 1000 with `CLOUD_DATA_DELETED_REASON`. Key symbols: `HUB_PROTOCOL`, `HUB_PATH`, `TEAM_HUB_PATH`, `handshakeProtocols`, `TOKEN_REFUSED`, `PROTOCOL_REFUSED`, `PLAN_REFUSED`, `isHubRefusal`, `PING`, `PONG`, `SERVER_MESSAGES`, `RUNNER_MESSAGES`, `JOB_ACK`, `SYNCED_DATA`, `NEVER_SENT`, `hubEnvelope`, `serializeHubFrame`, `parseHubFrame`, `readProjectSync`, `readScoutSchedule`, `readProjectList`, `readFindingResults`, `readPullState`, `readKnowledgeProposal`, `readRunCosts`, `capSummary`, `SUMMARY_CAP`, `CardStart`, `CardMoveJob`, `CLOUD_DATA_DELETED`, `CLOUD_DATA_DELETED_REASON`, `isCloudDataDeleted`, `MIN_SCOUT_INTERVAL_MINUTES`, `MAX_SCOUT_INTERVAL_MINUTES`.
- `packages/protocol/src/display.ts`: the signed display entitlements document and its reader. Key symbols: `DisplayEntitlement`, `SignedDisplay`, `displayPayload`, `readDisplay`, `base64urlEncode`, `base64urlDecode`, `DISPLAY_SIGNATURE_ALGORITHM`.
- `packages/protocol/src/package.ts`: the signed paid package manifest and its reader. Key symbols: `PackageManifest`, `packagePayload`, `readPackage`, `isPackageEntry`, `sha256Base64url`.
- `packages/protocol/src/engine.ts`: engine names and labels, profiles, limit signals. `profileName` words a profile ("Claude Code (work)") for the UI and the server. `goneSentence` words a model its engine stopped offering, and `goneProblem` adds what to do, so the refused drop, the card's Settings tab and Settings, Models say it one way. `effortInId` marks Cursor, which reads the effort off the model id, and `effortInModel` asks it of a profile id, so `ranOn`, `startsCommand` and the trail name no effort there. - `packages/protocol/src/env.ts`: the one reading of `NOLZU_FAKE_AGENT`. Key symbols: `isFakeMode`.
- `packages/protocol/src/sentinels.ts`: the lines every run prints, the question file, the QA verdict words. Key symbols: `SENTINEL_COMPLETE`, `NEEDS_INPUT_PREFIX`, `QUESTION_FILE`, `VERDICT_PREFIX`, `VERDICTS`.
- `packages/protocol/src/soon.ts`: `CLOUD_OPEN`, false in this release: off, `startHubLink` dials nothing and reads no keychain without a test's socket, `currentDisplay` answers `FREE` with no keychain read and no call, a stored `teamProjectId` counts as no team (the wrap-up's hold in `loop.ts`, `cardHolds`), `/api/package/entry` serves the empty module, `/team` sends a person to Now, `parseCommand` takes an account command with any argument, `SECTIONS` drops Cloud and `MOVED` sends its old links to Project, `usage` leaves out the account commands, and `bin/nolzu.js` answers `login`, `logout` and `status` with `NO_ACCOUNT`. `apps/local/src/lib/cloud-closed.test.ts` pins the closed state, and the tests of the cloud code mock it on. Then the one switch for Scout and Evidence, false in the first release; off, `createHubSync`'s `evidence` sends nothing, a `report.write` job is taken and answered with nothing, and `data-leaves.ts` drops the Evidence and reports group. Off, the rail draws no row for either, Settings, Project has no Evidence box, Settings, Wall no timesheet line (it needs `CLOUD_OPEN` too, as a Solo feature), `KEYS` drops their go keys, the palette drops their rows and the Scout check, Now drops the Scout panel and toast, Settings, Project drops Client and rate, What leaves this machine drops the timesheet export, and `hooks.server.ts` sends `/scout` and `/evidence` to Now and answers `/api/scout` and `/api/evidence` with 404. `apps/local/src/lib/coming-soon.test.ts` loads the modules with it off and on. Key symbols: `SCOUT_AND_EVIDENCE`, `CLOUD_OPEN`.
- `packages/protocol/src/triage.ts`: the triage file a feedback run writes, four lines per ref (`T3: fix`, `Summary:`, `Reason:`, `Reply:`), parsed whole or refused whole with the line named. Here so the loop and the page share it. Key symbols: `parseTriage`, `TRIAGE_VERDICTS`, `TriageBlock`.
- `packages/protocol/src/question-options.ts`: the answers a question offers. Key symbols: `parseQuestionOptions`, `storedQuestionOptions`, `MAX_QUESTION_OPTIONS`.
- `packages/protocol/src/ticket.ts`: what a person typed in Add card, and card key rules. `pullRequestRef` names the pull request a pasted link (a tab tail dropped) or owner/repo#N names, and refuses an issue link before GitHub is asked. `ticketLink` recognises a pasted Jira, Linear or GitHub link: GitHub and Linear by their exact host, Jira by its path on any host, which comes back for the read to compare. `localTitle` is a local card's title from typed text (first line, cut to `TITLE_CAP` on a word by `cutOnWord`), and `refinedTitle` reads the `# <KEY>: <title>` heading Refine writes, `refinedBranch` the `Kind:` and `Branch:` lines under it. `refinedCriteria` reads the brief's acceptance checks with their level (`must` or `should`) and `Check:` line (or a `Check:` after the text on the same line), and `refinedQuestions` its open questions with the `Q` id, the default and `blocks:` (under another heading too, as the architecture's `## What is still open`); a brief from before them reads with those left null. `decisionLines` reads the `D1:` lines of an architecture's `## The decisions` and a plan's `## Decisions`, the first of an id winning. - `packages/protocol/src/spend.ts`: cost roll-up of runs. Key symbols: `spendOf`, `mergeSpend`, `reportedCost`, `Spend`.
- `packages/protocol/src/strip.ts`: number formats for the strip. Key symbols: `formatDuration`, `formatCost`, `formatTokens`, `PipSegment`.
- `packages/protocol/src/estimate.ts`: the estimate shape and its words, and `estimateSum` for two runs one press starts, nothing unless both are measured. `estimateBrief` is the column header's short "~$0.05". `learnEstimateText` is Learn your repositories' line, which names a Refine run on the same model and effort as the low end until a Learn run is measured. - `packages/protocol/src/timesheet.ts`: the week grid and its CSV. Key symbols: `timesheetOf`, `timesheetCsv`, `weekOf`, `minutesOf`, `timesheetEntries`.
- `packages/protocol/src/standup.ts`: Yesterday, Today, Blocked, as one text and as the three lists the screen draws, which join back into the same text. Key symbols: `standupText`, `standupLists`, `standupTextOf`, `previousWorkingDay`.
- `packages/protocol/src/git.ts`: shapes of the Changes tab and the project scan, `hasWork` (whether the card did anything in a repository, which the tab and the server both read), `hasUncommitted` (the same for the Not committed view), `changesView` (the view the Changes tab opens on: the address, then Whole card in Review with no commit, else Not committed), `BranchCommits` (the commit list), and `DiffComment`, one comment on a diff line. - `packages/protocol/src/setup.ts`: Set up the project as pure rules the server and the screens share: the four steps in order with their need, title, line and link words (`SETUP_STEPS`), the marks a project stores (`SETUP_MARKS`), each step's state from its fact or its mark, a required one never put away (`setupSteps`), what the banner lists (`bannerSteps`), whether the first run waits (`learnGates`), what Now's stream carries (`SetupView`) and what runs lack without the knowledge (`WRITE_IT_MYSELF`). Key symbols: `SETUP_STEPS`, `SETUP_MARKS`, `SetupStep`, `SetupFacts`, `SetupView`, `setupSteps`, `bannerSteps`, `learnGates`, `WRITE_IT_MYSELF`.
- `packages/protocol/src/knowledge.ts`: the key of a run with no card, change statuses, and the frontmatter of a knowledge file, which the Knowledge screen, the knowledge index and the output check read: its `description` and the `paths` it depends on (a comma list, a bracket list or `- ` lines, each path once, without quotes, a leading `./`, line numbers or a last slash). A Learn your repositories run has a key per repository (`learnRepoKey`, read back with `repoOfLearnKey`), and `isMaintenanceKey` is true for it and for the reserved key, which every screen and sentence that says "maintenance" reads. Key symbols: `KB_MAINTENANCE_KEY`, `learnRepoKey`, `repoOfLearnKey`, `isMaintenanceKey`, `KnowledgeStatus`, `frontmatterEnd`, `frontmatterDescription`, `frontmatterPaths`.
- `packages/protocol/src/scout.ts`: Scout source kinds, the keychain name of a source's secret by kind, project and id (`scoutEntryName`, with the old `scoutSecretName` kept for the move), and the assessment key. Key symbols: `SCOUT_SOURCE_KINDS`, `isScoutSourceKind`, `SCOUT_SOURCE_LABELS`, `scoutEntryName`, `scoutEntrySlug`, `scoutSecretName`, `assessKey`, `findingOfKey`.
- `packages/protocol/src/stack.ts`: the five stack states. Key symbols: `STACK_STATES`, `StackState`.

## `packages/runner`

Spawns the engine CLIs, parses their lines, runs git, the app QA starts and the ticket CLIs. Not
published. Imports protocol and core.

### Engines and their parsers

- `packages/runner/src/index.ts`: the barrel; `@nolzu/runner/detect` is a second entry for the launcher. Key symbols: `engine`, `fake`, `select`.
- `packages/runner/src/engine.ts`: the engine contract (`spawn`, `parse`, `listModels`), sentinel detection. Key symbols: `Engine`, `detectSentinel`, `withSentinels`.
- `packages/runner/src/select.ts`: picks the engine for a vendor, or the fake engine in fake mode (`NOLZU_FAKE_DELAY_SCALE`, and the switches `NOLZU_FAKE_QA_FAIL`, `NOLZU_FAKE_PLAN_ASKS`, `NOLZU_FAKE_TRIAGE_BROKEN` and `NOLZU_FAKE_FIX_ASKS`). Key symbols: `ENGINES`, `selectEngine`, `loginShellPath`, `parseRunLine`.
- `packages/runner/src/run.ts`: one run over an engine: spawn, parse, the quiet timeout, the missing result, an engine that stays up after its result stopped after `AFTER_RESULT_MS` of quiet (Claude Code waits on a server it started in the background), a time mark in front of a log line at most once a second (`MARK_EVERY_MS`), in the same write, and the npm login line (`npmScopeRefusal`) added to the reason or question of a run whose output held a 404 on a scoped package. `RunHalted` is a thrown error that fails the run even after its result line. `resumeJob` turns a job into a session resume. Key symbols: `runJob`, `resumeJob`, `RunEngine`, `RunHalted`, `MARK_EVERY_MS`.
- `packages/runner/src/npm-refusal.ts`: the sentence for an npm 404 on a scoped package in a run's output, naming the scope and `npm login --scope`. Reads no npmrc file. Key symbols: `npmScopeRefusal`.
- `packages/runner/src/retry.ts`: waits out API errors and usage limits, then resumes. Key symbols: `runWithRetries`, `retryDelay`, `retryable`, `limitReached`, `limitText`, `resetText`.
- `packages/runner/src/spawn.ts`: the process side of each engine: arguments, prompt on stdin, lines out, stop on abort. Every engine starts inside the wall for every phase (`walled`), Codex too while `ownSandbox` says false, with its own sandbox off. Where the probe says the wall cannot start (`wallStateOf`), Claude Code runs unwalled with a first line that says so, Codex runs under its own sandbox (a run that runs commands gets the package caches in the data folder, `packageCaches`, as a walled run does), Cursor runs Implement only with its own sandbox flags back (`walled` false on `cursorArgs`), and `vendorRefusal` refuses the rest before anything starts. `walled` builds the spec with `wallSpecFor`, refuses a profile config folder that holds the project or the home folder, fails the run before any process starts when the wall refuses, and writes a first stream line that names the wall and its writable folders. It checks `job.walls` again with `checkedWallLists`, maps the project's paths onto the card's checkout (`onCheckout`), and names each entry it left out on that first line. It makes the session folders first. On Linux it keeps what bwrap can hold (`specOnDisk`) and names a hook file or `.mcp.json` an Implement run created in the checkout. Every run, under any wall, gets `watchedCheck`, which reads the row's watched files before the run and fails it with `watchedChanged` when the part that starts something later changed. `runProcess` calls `afterRun` only once the process group is gone, so a tool the run left behind cannot write after the compare. It passes the repositories one folder down in the checkout (`childRepos`) to `wallSpecFor`. For a run that may write, under any wall or Codex's own sandbox (`checked`), also `linkCheck` fails a run that may write when `gitLinkIn` finds a link in the git folders of the checkout or one of `childRepos`. `pointerCheck` reads `gitPointers` before the run and fails it with `POINTER_CHANGED` (through `ProcessSpec.afterRun` and `RunHalted`) when they changed. `job.startsInCardFolder` says where a Codex process starts. A QA job starts in `job.qaCopy` (`onCopy`), which the wall opens while the real checkout stays read-only. `SpawnOptions.wall` is the test seam (`WallPort`), and `SpawnOptions.wallState` stands in for the probe; `packages/runner/test/wall-double.ts` holds the doubles. `prepareTaskDir` writes the job's method to `INSTRUCTIONS_FILE` under the phase and, on a run that resumes no session, its prompt to `PROMPT_FILE` beside it, so the Files tab shows what the run was told. `prepareTaskDir` writes and removes the method and prompt files through `writeCardFile` and `removeCardFile`. `restoreCursorMcp` and `cursorMcp` write, put back and remove `.cursor/mcp.json` and the exclude line through `checkoutWrite` (`realFolders`, then `writeOwnFile`): no link at the file, `.cursor`, the git folder or `info`; a refused write at the start puts the merge back and throws. Key symbols: `checkoutWrite`, `prepareTaskDir`, `spawnClaude`, `spawnCodex`, `spawnCursor`, `walled`, `wallStateOf`, `WallPort`, `onCopy`, `pointerCheck`, `linkCheck`, `POINTER_CHANGED`, `watchedCheck`, `watchedChanged`, `cursorRoot` (the folder whose `.cursor/mcp.json` Cursor reads: the nearest one at or above the start folder holding a `.git`, as the CLI finds it, else the start folder; the project folder for a Research card), `cursorMcp` (the run's servers merged into `.cursor/mcp.json` at `cursorRoot` through `writablePath`, never in the home folder, a `.cursor` it made given a `.gitignore` of its two files, else its one line in the repository's git exclude file, and the record in the run's folder, `CURSOR_RECORD`; each secret goes into the file as `${env:NOLZU_MCP_SECRET_<tag>_<n>}` with a random tag for each run and its value into the `env` `spawnCursor` adds to cursor-agent's environment), `restoreCursorMcp` (puts the file back step by step, best effort; a file changed since the merge, which the record's hash of what Nolzu wrote tells, keeps the change and loses only Nolzu's servers, with a same-named one of the file before given back; skips a folder that is gone, takes out only Nolzu's exclude line once no other record needs it, and always drops the record), `readOrNull` reads an engine's watched file following a link and `readOwnOrNull` a checkout file without, both as no file for a pipe or a device (`readPlain`); `restoreCursorMcp` and `cursorMcp` read with `readOwnOrNull` after `realFolders`. `claudeConfig` (Claude Code's config for one attempt in the run's folder, mode 600, removed on its init message through the spec's `onLine` and `claudeInit`), and `runConfigWall` (every walled run hides the data folder's `mcp-runs`, `MCP_RUNS` and `mcpRunsFolder`, and a Claude Code run with servers opens its own folder again read only, `WallSpec.reopened`). `spawnCodex` adds the secrets `codexMcp` keeps off the command line to Codex's environment. `spawnCursor` takes a turn at its root first (`cursorTurn`, `cursorTurns`, `within`): a second run with servers at the same root waits, with a log line and another every few minutes (`WAIT_NOTE_MS`, at most half the quiet timeout), until every run before it has put the file back; a run stopped while it waits gives up its place without letting the next one in early. `runProcess` starts nothing when a stop came while its first line was read. Its after-run check runs once, also when the caller stops reading early (`checkedAfter`), and joins every check's sentence (`joinedHalts`); a `RunHalted` becomes a failed event marked halted, which no retry resumes.
- `packages/runner/src/claude-engine.ts`: Claude Code. Key symbols: `claudeEngine`.
- `packages/runner/src/claude-args.ts`: its arguments, with the Read rule for the project's AGENTS.md (`projectFileReads`, from `Job.projectDir`) and for the files outside the checkout the prompt names (`outsideFileReads`, from `Job.epicFiles`, the epic documents of a task, and `Job.readFiles`, the project's Implement and QA instructions Plan reads), and one rule for the whole knowledge folder the index lists (`Job.knowledgeReadDir`), never for a run that writes knowledge. Key symbols: `claudeArgs`, `projectFileReads`, `outsideFileReads`.
- `packages/runner/src/claude.ts`: its `stream-json` parser. Key symbols: `parseClaudeLine`.
- `packages/runner/src/models.ts`: the Claude Code model list, kept by hand. Key symbols: `claudeModels`.
- `packages/runner/src/codex-engine.ts`: Codex CLI. Key symbols: `codexEngine`.
- `packages/runner/src/codex-args.ts`: its arguments. `ownSandbox: false` turns Codex's own sandbox off inside Nolzu's wall (`danger-full-access` and no approval, through `-c` on exec and resume); true, the default, is its own sandbox, with the knowledge folder and, for a run that runs commands, the package caches (`cachesDir`) as writable roots. The run's servers (`job.mcp`) go in as `-c mcp_servers.<name>` overrides (`codexMcp`): stdio with plain env, a secret env variable through `env_vars` and Codex's environment, `cwd`, a streamable HTTP server with `url`, plain `http_headers` and a secret header through `env_http_headers`, a tool list as `enabled_tools`, and every secret's variable taken out of the agent's shell with `shell_environment_policy.exclude`. An SSE server is left out. `--ignore-user-config` keeps the person's own `config.toml` and its servers out of every run. Key symbols: `codexArgs`, `codexMcp`.
- `packages/runner/src/codex.ts`: its `exec --json` parser. `turn.failed` is the run's failed result with Codex's sentence, and an `error` item is a start-up warning that only the Log tab shows (`codexWarning`, read by `parseRunLine`). Key symbols: `parseCodexLine`, `codexRefusals`, `codexWarning`.
- `packages/runner/src/codex-models.ts`: its model list with effort levels per model, read with a five second wait (`MODEL_LIST_TIMEOUT_MS`) that a test's freshly written stub passes longer. Key symbols: `codexModels`, `parseCodexModels`, `MODEL_LIST_TIMEOUT_MS`.
- `packages/runner/src/cursor-engine.ts`: Cursor CLI. Key symbols: `cursorEngine`.
- `packages/runner/src/cursor-args.ts`: its arguments, with `--approve-mcps` when the run has a server. Key symbols: `cursorArgs`.
- `packages/runner/src/cursor.ts`: its parser. Key symbols: `parseCursorLine`, `cursorFailure`.
- `packages/runner/src/cursor-models.ts`: its model list. Key symbols: `cursorModels`, `CURSOR_AUTO`.
- `packages/runner/src/detect.ts`: which CLIs are installed and their versions, each engine's install page and npm line (`ENGINE_INSTALLS`), and the PATH scan that finds a binary without running it (`enginesOnPath`). Key symbols: `detectEngines`, `runVersion`, `parseVersion`, `usedApiKey`, `ENGINE_INSTALLS`, `EngineInstall`, `enginesOnPath`.
- `packages/runner/src/login-status.ts`: each tool's own status and sign-in command, checked against its docs (the comment names the page), and the reading of a status answer as signed in, signed out, not installed or a failed check. Key symbols: `LOGIN_COMMANDS`, `checkLogin`, `readLoginStatus`, `statusCommand`, `signInCommand`, `STATUS_WAIT_MS`, `LoginAnswer`.
- `packages/runner/src/engine-env.ts`: the environment an engine process gets: the guard's folders and command rule, the profile's config folder, and shell variables that would override the model removed, along with Nolzu's own `ORIGIN`, `PORT` and `HOST` (the local API still has no token, see SECURITY.md) and the GitHub and npm tokens (`RUN_TOKENS`, also taken off the QA app in `app-server.ts`; Nolzu's own `gh` keeps them). `packageCaches` gives npm, yarn and pnpm their caches in `<data folder>/caches`, made on the spot, which `walled` in `spawn.ts` and the QA app's wall open and hand on, because the wall keeps the home folder read-only. Key symbols: `engineEnv`, `RUN_TOKENS`, `CONFIG_DIR_VAR`, `packageCaches`.
- `packages/runner/src/shell-env.ts`: the login shell's `PATH`, read once. `startPath` is the `PATH` the launcher (`apps/local/bin/start.js`) gives the whole process before anything starts a tool: its own entries, then the login shell's, absolute entries only, so git finds the person's gpg, credential helpers and git-lfs when Nolzu starts outside a terminal. Key symbols: `startPath`, `shellPath`, `readLoginShellEnv`, `findExecutable`.
- `packages/runner/src/wall.ts`: the wall around an engine process, pure: one `WallSpec` (writable, files replaced through a temp file beside them in `replaced` (macOS only), hidden, write-denied paths, exact write-denied paths in `writeDeniedLiteral`, the `.env` roots and their exceptions, folders inside a hidden one that stay readable in `reopened`, the git places where macOS refuses a new symbolic or hard link in `noLinks`, and on Linux the closed unix `sockets`) in, one command out. A reopened folder must sit inside a hidden one, or the wall refuses to start; it is allowed again after the denies on macOS, with a metadata read on the folder above, and bound read-only over the hiding tmpfs on Linux. A file rule does not stop a unix connect, so on macOS the profile denies a connect to and a write of any socket named like Docker's or Podman's (`DAEMON_SOCKET`, a name regex, so a socket in the temp folder cannot be renamed out of it), and on Linux each closed socket is covered with `/dev/null` or a tmpfs. A write-denied path with a `*` is a `(regex ...)` deny of one folder name on macOS (`deniedFilter`), and an exact path with a `*` denies only a rename or delete there (`literalDeny`), so the engine can still make a new project folder. An exact path is a `(literal ...)` deny on macOS, and on Linux a file bound read-only or a writable folder bound onto itself, which cannot then be renamed. A Seatbelt profile under `sandbox-exec` on macOS, where the last matching rule wins so the line order is the meaning, and which ends with `(deny lsopen)` and `(deny appleevent-send)` because an opened app or one an Apple event drives runs outside it, and a `bwrap` call on Linux with the network left open. Anything else is a refusal with a reason, never the bare engine. Key symbols: `wallCommand`, `seatbeltProfile`, `bwrapArgs`, `WallSpec`, `WallCommand`.
- `packages/runner/src/wall-detect.ts`: the node side of the wall: whether it can start here (`sandbox-exec` probe on macOS, `bwrap` present and its probe on Linux, cached per process), the project's `.env` files for `bwrap`, the folders a `*` write-denied path names for `bwrap` (`starredPaths`) and the spec with missing writable paths dropped and those stars turned into folders (`specOnDisk`), the git folders of a checkout (`gitDirs`, its own and the shared one, and `gitCommonDir`, read off the files, no git process), the repositories one folder down (`childRepos`), what points git at the repository there and in the checkout (`gitPointers`, compared before and after an Implement run), and the walled command with links resolved through the nearest parent that exists (`resolvedPath`), `noLinks` included. Key symbols: `checkWall`, `wallHere`, `envFilesUnder`, `starredPaths`, `specOnDisk`, `walledCommand`, `gitDirs`, `gitCommonDir`, `childRepos`, `gitPointers`, `resolvedPath`.
