- `packages/runner/src/engine-walls.ts`: the wall's fixed rules as data (each fixed hidden `~/` path is hidden under HOME and under the account's home, `systemHome`, which is `os.userInfo().homedir` or nothing when that throws): paths hidden in every run (`HIDDEN_FOR_EVERY_RUN`, made of `HIDDEN_LOGINS`, `HIDDEN_SOCKET_FOLDERS` and `HIDDEN_HISTORY`, so Settings, Wall groups them from the same rows), the browser profiles hidden only on their own system (`HIDDEN_ON`), the Linux daemon sockets, session bus and X socket a run may not connect to (`LINUX_SOCKETS`, into the spec's `sockets`; macOS closes them in the profile), one row per engine (session folders and files, logins, config and hook files, and the files compared after each run in `watched`), the hook paths in a checkout (`CHECKOUT_WRITE_DENIED`, the whole `.claude` folder and the `.mcp.json` file among them, held by name so a write, a rename or a new one fails on macOS), and `wallSpecFor`, `SHARED_GIT_WRITABLE`, which turns a phase policy into a `WallSpec`. `appWallSpec` is the wall of QA's app start: the checkout, the temp folder and the listed caches open, the checkout's git holds (and those of its `childRepos`) and engine config paths read-only, every engine's login hidden. Its `writeDeniedLiteral` holds the checkout folder, its `.git`, the git folder and the `commondir`, `gitdir` and `config.worktree` files of the shared and own git folder (`gitOwnDir`), and the same plus the config and hooks for each of `childRepos` (`gitHolds`), so a run cannot repoint git. A row opens only its session folders and files (`sessionFolders`, `sessionFiles`, made first through `sessionFoldersOf`), because in SBPL the last matching rule wins, so a whole open state folder left every name the row did not close writable, and bwrap cannot hold a path that is not there yet; Cursor on Linux is the one exception, `linuxOpen` and `linuxStateOf`: its whole state folder opens, because the CLI saves its config through a temp file and a rename that a bound file refuses, and `walled` holds the rest of that folder read-only and fails a run that added a name there or replaced an entry, by inode and link target (`addedToState`, `identityOf`), or left the folder unreadable. On macOS the Codex row still opens its whole state folder (`writable`) until its session files are known. A `*` in `writeDenied` is one folder name (`~/.claude/projects/*/memory`). Every writable folder between an open state folder and one of the row's write-denied paths joins `writeDeniedLiteral`, so a run cannot rename it away and move its own in. `watchedFilesOf` places a row's `watched` files, each with the part that is compared: the MCP servers and hooks in `~/.claude.json`, and the permissions in Cursor's `cli-config.json`. A profile's config folder takes the place of the row's `configRoot`. `lists` are the project's checked lists: hidden paths join the read deny list before the fixed ones, writable folders open for Implement and QA (`policy.commands`) only, and an exception is kept only for a `.env` file outside every fixed secret path. `copyOf` names the real checkout when `checkout` is a QA copy: the copy is writable, the real one read-only, the `.env` files of both hidden, and an exception is mapped onto the copy. `ownSandbox` per row says whether the engine's own sandbox holds the run instead of this wall: false for all three, Codex included, until the report says a Codex permission profile can deny the secret reads. The function `ownSandbox(vendor, wall)` also answers true for Codex where the wall cannot start. Rows wait for the report of `scripts/measure-walls.mjs`. For a worktree checkout an Implement run gets only `SHARED_GIT_WRITABLE` of the shared git folder (objects, refs, logs, lfs, rr-cache, packed-refs and gc's files and locks, `gc.pid.lock` among them) and the card's own admin folder; a plain checkout's git folder opens whole. Every writable git place but `objects` and `lfs`, of the checkout and each of `childRepos`, goes into the spec's `noLinks` (`gitNoLinks`). Key symbols: `HIDDEN_FOR_EVERY_RUN`, `HIDDEN_LOGINS`, `HIDDEN_SOCKET_FOLDERS`, `HIDDEN_HISTORY`, `HIDDEN_ON`, `LINUX_SOCKETS`, `ENGINE_WALLS`, `CHECKOUT_WRITE_DENIED`, `wallSpecFor`, `sessionFoldersOf`, `linuxStateOf`, `watchedFilesOf`, `ownSandbox`, `appWallSpec`, `wallKind` (the `WallKind` the loop stores on a run row, from `ownSandbox`, the probe and the platform).
- `packages/runner/src/wall-lists.ts`: the project's three wall lists from Settings, Project, Walls, checked by one pure function on save and again at spawn (at spawn a hidden `~/` entry is also placed under the account's home, `WallPlaces.systemHome`, and checked there; a writable one never is): an exception must be a `.env` file inside the project, a writable folder may not be or hold the project, its git folder, the home folder, Nolzu's data folder, a fixed secret path or an engine's config or hook file, and a hidden path may not cover a folder the run needs. `wallGroups` is what Settings, Wall shows: the fixed rules of this system in groups (keys and cloud logins, `.env` files, shell history, Docker and Podman, browser profiles, engine logins, engine config and hook files, the card checkout rules), each with one line and its paths, built from the rows of `engine-walls.ts`. Key symbols: `wallEntryProblem`, `checkedWallLists`, `wallGroups`, `WallGroup`, `WallPlaces`, `WallListName`.
- `packages/runner/src/qa-copy.ts`: the throwaway copy of a card's checkout that QA runs on: an APFS clone on macOS, a reflink on Linux, a plain `cp -R` when the clone is refused (`copyAttempts`), with uncommitted work and `node_modules`, in a Nolzu folder on the checkout's volume (`qaCopyRoot`: `<data folder>/qa-copies`, or `.nolzu-qa-copies` at the top of another disk), one path per card and checkout (`qaCopyPath`), deleted after the run and swept at start. Key symbols: `makeQaCopy`, `removeQaCopy`, `sweepQaCopies`, `qaCopyRoot`, `qaCopyPath`, `copyAttempts`.
- `packages/runner/src/args-guard.ts`: refuses a value that would read as a flag. Key symbols: `guardLeadingDash`, `guardName`.
- `packages/runner/src/prompt.ts`: the prompt of a run, the prompt of an Ask run (`askPrompt`, the whole method, no skill), and the progress file a phase keeps. The first line names `<card folder>/<phase>/INSTRUCTIONS_FILE` on every engine. A run that starts in neither the project folder nor the card folder, such as one in a card checkout or QA's copy, is told the code is in `./` and that the project folder its card folder sits in is not the card's code, unless the prompt lists the repositories and where each is read. Every path is relative to the folder the run starts in and an absolute one is refused, except with `fullPaths`, which `buildJob` sets for QA's copy: it names the card folder and every other path outside the copy in full (physical) and adds the line that says so, because a model miscounted the long `../` path from the data folder. `QA_CHECKS_FILE` is the one file a phase writes outside its folder (Plan's list of QA checks). `folderRepos` adds the paragraph that tells a run on several repositories to use each tool's folder option instead of `cd`. `epic` adds "The epic": a task's run reads the epic's documents that exist, read only: `epicDocPaths` lists `EPIC_DOCS` with the screens and `tokens.css` of `design/` (or the old `mockups/`) after its list, then the epic's `QA_CHECKS_FILE`, the epic's `DISCOVERIES_FILE` last. The read-only line also lets a run add to `DISCOVERIES_FILE` at the top of the card folder. `projectInstructions` adds "Project instructions for <phase>" after the extra context, in both prompts (`projectBlocks`), with the fixed last line: they may change the work, the language and add `##` sections, but the file names and places, the skill's headings and fields, the ids, the review block and the verdict line stay as the skill writes them, in English (for Ask, that the run still changes nothing). `readFirst` adds "Read first" after the epic: the card folder files the earlier phases wrote, from `READ_FIRST` (one entry per phase, the share files among QA's and the wrap-up's) through `readFirstPaths`, which keeps the ones that exist. `buildJob` gives a re-review its earlier rounds instead, from `roundsIn`. `laterInstructions` adds Plan's read of the project's `.nolzu/phases/implement.md` and `qa.md`, with the line that turns each rule into a step or a QA check. `personsWords` adds "What the person said" after the extra context (`personsBlocks`): each question asked with its answer, the line typed on a move, the person's Discussion messages (newest last), the first QA answer and the allowed commands, each item on one line and cut at 500 characters. Each of the first three lists keeps its newest lines that fit (`newest`: 5,000 characters of questions, 2,500 of moves and 2,500 of messages) and counts the ones left out. `history` adds "What this card went through" after it, for the wrap-up (`historyBlocks`): how often each phase ran and each QA that did not pass with its reason. `knowledgeIndex` adds "Project knowledge" after "Read first" (`knowledgeBlocks`): the project folder's knowledge folder and one line per file with what it holds, cut at 150 characters, and the rule to read at most three before the code. It asks for no line saying an area has no knowledge file, because the finish rules ask for the fact instead. `pickKnowledge` keeps at most `KNOWLEDGE_INDEX_CAP` (30) files and counts the rest. With a `KnowledgeScope` (the card's repositories and the project's, from `buildJob`) it lists `RUN_RECIPE_FILE` (from core), each card repository's `repository.md` and notes, newest first, then `shared/`, then the other notes, keeps the card's part (each repository's file and `CARD_NOTES` newest notes) past the cap, and counts the other repositories' files (`other`, a line of its own). With none, for a run of no card or a card on every repository, `RUN_RECIPE_FILE` and the repository files first, then the newest. `commit` adds "The code you read is at commit <sha> (<date>)." under the card folder lines, which `buildJob` reads with `commitAt`: the card's checkout when it is there, else the base branch in the repository folder, and nothing on several repositories unless the run works in one. `browser` adds, for QA only, the line that names the browser servers the engine is handed and says to use them for the checks that need a page. `servers` names the other servers with their descriptions. `browserLine` and `serversLine` build those two lines, and `swapPromptLine` swaps one for the servers a run got, with a replacer function. `PROMPT_FILE` is where spawn writes the prompt beside the method. `learnRepo` names the one repository a Learn your repositories run learns, where it is read, and the two files it writes (its repository file and its `recipeHeading` section of the run recipe), and makes the block "This run". Key symbols: `buildPrompt`, `projectBlocks`, `readFirstBlocks`, `personsBlocks`, `historyBlocks`, `knowledgeBlocks`, `pickKnowledge`, `KnowledgeScope`, `CARD_NOTES`, `KnowledgeEntry`, `KNOWLEDGE_INDEX_CAP`, `PersonsWords`, `PROMPT_FILE`, `READ_FIRST`, `readFirstPaths`, `EPIC_DOCS`, `epicDocPaths`, `DISCOVERIES_FILE`, `askPrompt`, `PROGRESS_FILE`, `INSTRUCTIONS_FILE`, `QA_CHECKS_FILE`, `SHARE_PLAN_FILE`, `FROM_EPIC_FILE` (the epic's notes in a task's card folder, which `epicNotes` names to every run of the task), `stepsDone`, `nextStep`, `resumeMessage` (a resumed run's message with the finish rules a message alone drops: write the summary again, which every run starts without, and on Implement update the share files).
- `packages/runner/src/phase-instructions.ts`: a project's own instructions for a phase, one file per phase at `.nolzu/phases/<phase>.md` in the project folder (`phaseInstructionsPath`). `INSTRUCTION_PHASES` is every action but the summary pass. `readPhaseInstructions` reads the file off the project folder for each run, the first 16 KB (`PHASE_INSTRUCTIONS_CAP`) with `cut` set when there was more; `savePhaseInstructions` writes it or deletes it on an empty text, refuses an unknown phase and a text over the cap, turns a failed write into a sentence, and both refuse a file or folder that resolves outside the project. Key symbols: `INSTRUCTION_PHASES`, `PHASE_INSTRUCTIONS_CAP`, `phaseInstructionsPath`, `phaseInstructionsFile`, `readPhaseInstructions`, `savePhaseInstructions`.
- `packages/runner/src/activity.ts`: one short line of what a run did, for the card, and what a tool acted on (`toolSubject`), which the Discussion tab's tool rows show too. Key symbols: `activityLine`, `toolSubject`.
- `packages/runner/src/log.ts`: the raw stream of a run, written and read back, with Nolzu's own lines: a note (`noteLine` for one the runner puts into the stream, such as the wall line), a retry wait, and the time the next line came (`nolzu.at`, which `parseRunLine` skips). Key symbols: `openRunLog`, `noteInRunLog`, `noteLine`, `retryInRunLog`, `timeInRunLog`, `runLogTime`, `tailLog`.
- `packages/runner/src/verdict.ts`: the QA verdict out of a run's text. Key symbols: `extractVerdict`.
- `packages/runner/src/mcp-config.ts`: writes Claude Code's config for one attempt with mode 600 (`writeMcpConfig`), reads the stdio servers (`mcpServers`) and the remote ones (`mcpRemotes`) of a config file, which the QA browser path not moved yet and Test the browser use, reads the server names a config holds, and says why a config file cannot be read. Key symbols: `writeMcpConfig`, `mcpConfigProblem`, `mcpServerNames`, `mcpServers`, `mcpRemotes`, `McpServer`, `McpRemote`.
- `packages/runner/src/awake.ts`: holds the machine awake during a run. Key symbols: `holdAwake`, `systemAwake`, `keepAwakeCommand`.
- `packages/runner/src/card-files.ts`: the Files tab reads. SVG is text, not an image. `.html` and `.htm` are the `html` kind, sent as `text/html`. `.css` is text, sent as `text/css` so a framed screen applies it under nosniff. `listCardFiles` groups by top folder, phase folders in the order the phases run, the top of the card folder first when it holds `DISCOVERIES_FILE` (listed first in it), gives each file the phase that wrote it (`writerOf`, off the card folder layout: `<phase>/`, and `QA_CHECKS_FILE` to Plan) and its modified time, and lists text over `CARD_FILE_CAP` as a download marked `tooBig`. A cache folder at any depth (`isCacheFolder`: `node_modules`, `npm-cache`, `.npm`, `.cache`, `.pnpm-store`, `.yarn/cache`) is counted off the folder listings with no look at each file and goes into its group's `caches`, never `files`, so the tab count leaves it out. Only `share/` is share. `sharedFiles` lists the files that leave the card (the Markdown and text files of `share/`, and `implement/pr.md`, regular files only and never through a link), and `stripSharedAttribution`, which the loop calls after every run, takes the tool lines out of them. `readCardFile` opens with `O_NONBLOCK`, so a named pipe answers at once. Every write, read and delete Nolzu makes in a card folder goes through `writeCardFile`, `readCardText`, `makeCardFolder` and `removeCardFile`: the nearest folder that is there and the real parent must be inside the real card folder, the card folder itself may not be a link, the file opens with `O_NOFOLLOW`, and a file that is no plain file or has a second name is refused. `readCardFile` refuses a second name and a linked card folder too, and `listCardFiles` lists nothing through a linked card folder. `writeOwnFile` is the open without following a link and the plain file check, then a check that the open file is the one in the real folder the caller checked (`cardParent` answers it), which `writeCardFile` and the Cursor merge in `spawn.ts` share. Key symbols: `writeOwnFile`, `listCardFiles`, `isCacheFolder`, `readCardFile`, `writeCardFile`, `readCardText`, `makeCardFolder`, `removeCardFile`, `cardFileKind`, `writerOf`, `CARD_FILE_CAP`, `sharedFiles`, `stripSharedAttribution`.

### The fake engine

- `packages/runner/src/fake.ts`: replays `packages/runner/fake/<phase>.ndjson` (Claude Code lines) with a delay, and writes what the phase skill would leave in the card folder. Refine writes `refined.md` with the card's key, a short title, `Kind:` and `Branch:` on top, the review block and the skill's sections under them, with levelled criteria and a `Q1` line (`FAKE_REFINED`). Architecture writes the review block and the eleven sections, with `Read at:`, Interfaces and a `D1` decision (`FAKE_ARCHITECTURE`). Plan writes `plan.md` in the plan skill's sections and step form, with the review block, `Read at:`, a `D1` decision (`D2` after an architecture, which also marks an epic and adds `share/PLAN.md`, `FAKE_SHARE_PLAN`), and `Check:` and `Covers:` on each step, `qa/checks.md` beside it with `AC` ids, levels and a `How:` line under each check (`FAKE_CHECKS_FILE`), and adds one line to `discoveries.md` once (`FAKE_DISCOVERY`). Design writes `design/main-page.html` with its states, linking `design/tokens.css`, and `design/design.md` with the review block and the screen's `Address:`, named `States:`, `Data:`, `Reuse:` and a `Q2` question (`FAKE_SCREEN`, `FAKE_TOKENS`, `FAKE_DESIGN`). Implement writes `implement/deviations.md`, `progress.md` (with `Base:`, the review block and `Red:`, `Green:` and `Check:` proof under each step) and `pr.md` (a title, `## What changed` and `## How to test`), and `share/SUMMARY.md` and `share/TESTING.md` beside them, in the words its replay writes (`FAKE_DEVIATIONS`, `FAKE_PROGRESS`, `FAKE_PR`), Research, Investigate, the wrap-up, Optimize and Learn this project write their main file (`FAKE_MAIN_FILES`); Learn this project's first round (`orient-asks`) asks one question with three answers (`FAKE_ORIENT_OPTIONS`), and the resumed round writes `project-rules.md` (`FAKE_RULES`), Learn your repositories (`learn-repo`, the repository read off its key) fills that repository's file in place of the stub line and puts its section in the run recipe (`fakeLearnRepo`), so the output check finds nothing missing in fake mode, the wrap-up's summary is of the whole card, and QA writes a `qa/report.md` with section 1 as bullets and one row per check in that order, each with `How:` and `Evidence:` lines (`FAKE_EVIDENCE`). A test reaches another scenario by replacing `loop.engine.spawn` with one that calls the fake with `{ ...job, phase: 'needs-input' }`. Two switches, which `selectEngine` reads from the environment in fake mode only, turn on a first round read off the card folder (`scenarioOf`): `NOLZU_FAKE_QA_FAIL` fails a card's first QA with a report and a screenshot in `qa/evidence/`, and `NOLZU_FAKE_PLAN_ASKS` makes its first plan ask one question with two answers. The feedback scenario writes one triage block per ref of the comments file (`fakeTriage`), and `NOLZU_FAKE_TRIAGE_BROKEN` makes the first verdict one the parser refuses. `NOLZU_FAKE_FIX_ASKS` makes an implement run whose message opens like a Fix on a pull request comment (`FIX_OPENS`) replay `needs-input`, so the answer, which is the person's text, goes on. Key symbols: `fakeEngine`, `fakeTriage`, `scenarioDelays`, `FakeEngine`, `FakeSwitches`, `scenarioOf`, `SUMMARIES`, `FAKE_CHECKS`, `FAKE_REFINED`, `FAKE_ARCHITECTURE`, `FAKE_SCREEN`, `FAKE_TOKENS`, `FAKE_DESIGN`, `FAKE_DISCOVERY`, `FAKE_DEVIATIONS`.
- `packages/runner/fake`: one scenario per phase (`refine`, `plan`, `implement`, `qa`, `wrapup`, `architecture`, `design`, `investigate`, `research`, `pr-review`, `feedback`, `optimize`, `orient`, `learn-repo`, `assess`, `commit-message`, `thread-title`), `orient-asks` for the first round of Learn this project, `ask` for an Ask message (reads one file and answers), three by name (`needs-input`, `api-error`, `rate-limit`), and the two first rounds the switches turn on: `qa-fail`, `plan-asks`.

### Git and checkouts

- `packages/runner/src/git/links.ts`: the places git writes by name, through a link (`COMMIT_EDITMSG`, `FETCH_HEAD`, the reflogs, `packed-refs`, `gc.log`): `gitLinkIn` finds the first symbolic link, file with a second name or entry that is neither a plain file, a folder nor a socket (a named pipe) in a card's own git folder (whole, when it is not the shared one), and in the shared folder at each entry at its top that is not a folder (a linked `hooks` aside) and in the folders git writes in by name (`WALKED`: `refs`, `logs`, `rr-cache`, `rebase-merge`, `rebase-apply`, `sequencer`, `worktrees`), each looked up by that name so a folder renamed to another case on macOS is still found, from the real folders, never following a link, leaving out `objects`, `lfs`, `hooks` and `modules` right inside a git folder. Other folders of the shared one, such as `info`, `nolzu-removing` and `nolzu-locks`, are not looked in. `gitLinkSentence` says it. `linkBeforeWrite` is what `git()` asks before every command `gitKind` in `commands.ts` does not call a read (an unlisted one included), in the folders `GIT_DIR` and `GIT_COMMON_DIR` name or those of the nearest `.git` up from where it runs. For the folders `init`, `clone` and `worktree add` make (`madeFolders`) it refuses a `..` part in them or in a `-C` folder, then walks each step from the owner folder (`GitOptions.owner`, else the folder git is started in, never a `-C` folder) down and refuses a link (`linkOnTheWay`); for `init` and `clone` the git folders checked are the new repository's, if it is there already. Key symbols: `gitLinkIn`, `gitLinkSentence`, `linkBeforeWrite`, `GitLink`.
- `packages/runner/src/git/exec.ts`: the one place that spawns git. Never throws on a non zero exit. A command that writes in a git folder holding a link is refused with code 128 and `linkBeforeWrite`'s sentence as stderr, before git starts. Every call passes `core.fsmonitor=false` (`GIT_FLAGS`), so a config a run planted cannot run a command through a status, and the submodule settings that keep status, diff, checkout and fetch out of a nested repository a run made; status and diff also take `--ignore-submodules=dirty` (`withFlags`, past a leading `-C` or `-c`), because a `.gitmodules` the run writes beats the setting. `add` leaves out every pointer to another repository in the index (`addWithoutGitlinks`), because it looks inside one whatever the config says. `commitAt` reads a repository top's short commit and date at a rev, sync, and null for anything else. Every call also passes `core.hooksPath=/dev/null` (`NO_HOOKS`) unless `hooks` is true, so a hook a run wrote in the checkout, such as `.husky/post-checkout`, does not run outside the wall. Only a commit or push the person ticked Run hooks on, and the first commit of a new workspace, pass `hooks`. Every call also passes `safe.bareRepository=explicit`, so git refuses a folder that is a repository with no `.git` name unless GIT_DIR names it. `diff`, `show` and `log` get `--no-textconv --no-ext-diff` (`withFlags`), `push.recurseSubmodules=no` is in `GIT_FLAGS`, and git starts by the full path `gitBinary` finds on the absolute PATH entries only, here, in `commitAt` and in `clone-into.ts`. `GitOptions.owner` names the folder Nolzu owns for the link walk of a call that makes a folder. Key symbols: `git`, `withFlags`, `addWithoutGitlinks`, `GitResult`, `exitCode`, `commitAt`, `gitBinary`, `GitOptions`, `GIT_FLAGS`, `NO_HOOKS`.
- `packages/runner/src/git/env.ts`: the environment git runs with. It drops the variables that point git at a repository or its files (`GIT_DIR`, `GIT_WORK_TREE`, `GIT_COMMON_DIR`, `GIT_INDEX_FILE`, `GIT_OBJECT_DIRECTORY`, `GIT_ALTERNATE_OBJECT_DIRECTORIES`, `GIT_SHALLOW_FILE`, `GIT_NAMESPACE`), `GIT_EXEC_PATH`, every `GIT_TRACE*` variable, and config handed in through the environment. Key symbols: `gitEnv`.
- `packages/runner/src/git/commands.ts`: the one table of every git subcommand Nolzu's own code runs (`GIT_COMMANDS`), each a rule that answers `read`, `write`, `network` or `network-read` (`ls-remote`) for an argument list. A read form must carry only options its rule names exactly (`readWords`), so an abbreviated option or `--output` makes a write; `branch`, `config`, `remote`, `symbolic-ref` and `worktree` also look at their words, and `config` counts only the options before its first word, as git reads them. `gitCall` reads the subcommand past a leading `-C` or `-c` (`--version` is `version`) and keeps each `-C` value as written (`moves`), `gitKind` classifies an argument list (an unlisted subcommand counts as a write), and `madeFolders` names the folders `init`, `clone` (as git names a clone) and `worktree add` make, as written. `commands.test.ts` scans the runtime source for literal git argument lists and fails on a subcommand the table does not list. Key symbols: `GIT_COMMANDS`, `GitKind`, `gitCall`, `gitKind`, `madeFolders`, `readWords`.
- `packages/runner/src/git/places.ts`: where a repository's work tree, own git folder and shared git folder are on disk, from one read-only `rev-parse`, checked against the places Nolzu's record expects inside the project (`repoPlaces`): each folder walked from the project folder with no link on any step, a linked worktree's own git folder required from the record, the `.git` file and `commondir` read as written and held to the expected folders, no `commondir` in the shared folder, and git's answer the same. Not wired into any git call yet. Key symbols: `repoPlaces`, `RepoPlaces`, `ExpectedPlaces`.
- `packages/runner/src/git/worktree.ts`: the card checkout at `<project>/worktrees/<key>`, a child repository inside it, kept out of git status by the exclude file. Its git calls name the project as `owner`, so `worktree add` refuses a link on the way from the project to the checkout. A stale entry of the card's own checkout is cleared by path, never with `worktree prune`. `addExcludes` refuses an `info` folder or `exclude` file that is a link or no plain entry, and makes its temp file new (`wx`). Key symbols: `ensureWorktree`, `worktreesFolder`, `checkoutRoot`, `checkoutPath`, `repoFolder`, `isPlainName`, `baseAdvice`.
- `packages/runner/src/git/lock.ts`: one checkout build per card at a time (`withCardLock`), and inside it one worktree build or removal per repository at a time (`withRepoLock`), because two cards' `git worktree add` or `prune` collide in `.git/worktrees`. `ensureWorktree` and `removeWorktree` take both, card first. A holder writes its time again every 30 s while its work runs (`REFRESH_MS`), so long work is never judged stale, and releases only while `held-by.json` still carries its token. A `signal` ends a wait behind another holder; `ensureWorktree` passes its own, so a Stop while a card waits for its turn takes effect. `ownFolder` removes a link found in place of a lock folder (the link only) and refuses anything else that is no folder; a link in place of one card lock goes as a link, and the record is written with `O_NOFOLLOW`. Key symbols: `withCardLock`, `ownFolder`, `withRepoLock`, `REFRESH_MS`.
- `packages/runner/src/git/branch-name.ts`: the card branch name from the template, with `{key}`, `{slug}`, `{type}` and `{desc}` (`BranchParts`, from Refine; feature and the first four title words when missing). `branchNameProblem` checks a typed name: the slug's characters, git's ref rules, the cap and the card key. Key symbols: `branchName`, `branchNameProblem`, `BranchParts`, `slugify`, `BRANCH_NAME_MAX`.
- `packages/runner/src/git/rename.ts`: renames the card branch in every checkout of the card, only while it has no upstream, no remote copy (`pushedReason`) and, unless the person picked the name (`byPerson`), no commit beyond its base. Key symbols: `renameFreshBranch`, `pushedReason`.
- `packages/runner/src/git/base.ts`: the base branch as a commit. Key symbols: `baseCommit`.
- `packages/runner/src/git/cleanup.ts`: removes a checkout and says what stayed. A forced removal moves the folder to `.git/nolzu-removing` under the locks, has git clear its entry, puts it back if git refuses, and deletes it after the locks are free, with whatever an earlier crashed removal left in that folder (listed under the lock, one log line each, `deleteLeft`); a checkout on another disk is removed in place. `sweepRemovals` does the same for one repository at boot, which `createLoop` calls in the background for every repository of every project. A removal that is not forced refuses a checkout holding files git ignores (`ignoredPaths`). A link in place of `nolzu-removing` is removed and never listed through (`asideLeft`), and a forced removal moves aside into a real folder (`ownFolder`). The ignored files refusal names up to eight with `somePaths`. Key symbols: `removeWorktree`, `sweepRemovals`.
- `packages/runner/src/git/list.ts`: the card checkouts with ahead and behind. Key symbols: `listWorktrees`.
- `packages/runner/src/git/read-only-check.ts`: the git compare after a read-only run. `gitBefore` reads HEAD, the refs (not remote ones, not branches another checkout has out) and `git status --porcelain=v1 -z --untracked-files=all` (with `--ignored` when asked, a content hash per file that is not ignored) of every repository a folder reaches, leaving out the folders the run may write, and again after git answers, because `--ignored` lists ignored paths under an excluded folder all the same. `gitAfter` names each repository inside the project folder by its path there (`placeOf`). The loop's `readBefore` leaves out every card folder, the checkouts, the knowledge folder and the project's `.nolzu/` folder, all of which other cards or Nolzu's own setting commits write while a run goes. `compareGit` is the pure diff. `gitAfter` compares, drops a ref or HEAD that moved forward only by commits inside those folders (Nolzu's own, such as another card's knowledge commit, `withoutOwnCommits`), writes the diff text, and in a repository inside `ownFolder` puts back the card's branch, HEAD, changed tracked files from the old commit and new files that git does not ignore, and names every other ref without touching it, since the person's repository holds it; elsewhere it touches nothing. From a workspace card's folder under `worktrees/`, `gitRoots` reads the workspace and the card's checkouts one level down. `gitBefore` reads only the repositories its caller names; `recordedRepos` names them from Nolzu's records (the project's repository folders, a linked `.git` followed, and the card's checkouts that are worktrees of them with their own folder in the shared folder's `worktrees`), never from a scan, and `restore` unstages with `reset`. Key symbols: `gitBefore`, `recordedRepos`, `RecordedRepos`, `gitAfter`, `compareGit`, `parseStatusZ`, `statusEntries`, `GitSnapshot`, `GitChange`.
- `packages/runner/src/git/porcelain.ts`: parsers of git output, and the progress of `git clone --progress`. `ignoredPaths` names the ignored files of a status worth naming, leaving out caches tools make again (`REMADE`, `REMADE_FILE`). `REMADE` and `REMADE_FILE` hold only tool output, never a name that can hold a person's files (`bin`, `obj`, `vendor`, `deps`, `log`, `tmp`, `.vscode`, `.idea`, and `.wrangler` with the local data of `wrangler dev`). `somePaths` names up to eight paths and how many more, for the refusals of `cleanup.ts` and `remove-repo.ts`. Key symbols: `somePaths`, `parseStatus`, `parseWorktrees`, `parseNumstat`, `parseNameStatus`, `cloneProgress`, `CloneProgress`, `ignoredPaths`.
- `packages/runner/src/git/diff.ts`: the card diff as a tree, and one file's patch. `CardDiff.branch` is the base to the folder, the change the push dialog names and the Changes tab draws, and `filePatch` with group `branch` is that file's patch. `CardDiff.uncommitted` is the Not committed view. `cardCommits` lists the branch's commits since the base with a cap, the total and each one's pushed mark from the upstream, and `commitDiff` and `filePatch` with group `commit` read one commit against its parent (`commitRange`: the id resolved as a commit, refused off the card's branch or at the base). Key symbols: `cardDiff`, `CardDiff`, `cardCommits`, `commitDiff`, `filePatch`, `fileTree`, `diffStat`, `PATCH_CAP_BYTES`.
- `packages/runner/src/git/commit.ts`: commit in the card checkout, with the repository's hooks skipped unless `hooks` is true. While a merge, cherry-pick or revert is in progress (`inProgress`), git refuses a commit of some paths, so the ticked files must be every changed file and the commit takes all of them; otherwise the sentence names the unticked ones. With `only` it commits exactly those paths (`commit --only`, a new one added first and taken out again on a refusal); without it, every tracked change and the untracked files `include` names. Also the diff a message pass reads. A failed commit takes a ticked new path out of the index with `reset`, never `rm --cached`, which runs git inside a folder whose `.git` is a file. Key symbols: `commitCard`, `messageProblem`, `splitCommitMessage`, `uncommittedDiff`.
- `packages/runner/src/git/push.ts`: push the card branch, with the pre-push hook skipped unless `hooks` is true, and never a branch whose name starts with `+` or `-`, which git reads as a forced push or a flag such as `--mirror`. The push names both sides, `refs/heads/<branch>:<to>`, with `to` the upstream's ref for the Workspace page. With no `to` the refspec is `refs/heads/<branch>` alone, so a `remote.<name>.push` in the config decides where it lands, and with none `-c push.default=current` sends it to its own name, never to an upstream such as main; the Workspace page's upstream push names `to`. Key symbols: `pushBranch`, `pushConfirmation`.
- `packages/runner/src/git/workspace.ts`: the Workspace page's git: one folder's branch, upstream, ahead and behind from `parseStatus` and its changed files from `statusEntries`, with no fetch (`readRepo`), skipping a folder that holds its own repository and the folders the caller names, each file marked `nolzu` or `yours`. `repoPatch` is `filePatch` from HEAD to the folder. `commitPaths` commits exactly the ticked files with `git commit --only`, so the person's staged and unticked files stay staged, with `messageProblem`, the conflict refusal and the hooks rule of `commitCard`. `pushUpstream` pushes the current branch to its upstream through `pushBranch`, refuses a detached head or no upstream, and turns a rejected push into "pull in your editor or terminal". `outsideRepo` refuses a path that leaves the repository. `commitPaths` undoes its add with `reset` too. Key symbols: `readRepo`, `repoPatch`, `commitPaths`, `pushUpstream`, `outsideRepo`, `WorkspaceFile`, `RepoState`.
- `packages/runner/src/git/pull.ts`: pull the branch into the checkout, hooks off as on every git call. Key symbols: `pullBranch`.
- `packages/runner/src/git/compare.ts`: the GitHub compare link, under its length limit. Key symbols: `compareUrl`.
- `packages/runner/src/git/remote.ts`: reads a remote URL into host, owner, repo. Key symbols: `parseRemote`, `parseRemoteUrl`, `withoutCredentials`.
- `packages/runner/src/git/scan.ts`: finds the repositories of a project folder, and those one level down in `~/code`, `~/projects` and `~/src` with each remote's URL. Key symbols: `scanProject`, `scanMachine`, `MACHINE_ROOTS`, `FoundRepo`.
- `packages/runner/src/git/clone.ts`: clone by URL, with URL checks. Key symbols: `cloneRepository`, `cloneTarget`.
- `packages/runner/src/github-repo.ts`: a new private repository through the person's `gh` (on `ghRunner`): the owners for the menu (`gh api user`, `user/orgs`), the name check (`gh repo view`), the create with `--private --source --push`, and the two temp repositories it pushes from (a copy of the person's with no remote, or a first commit). Key symbols: `githubOwners`, `repoNameProblem`, `createPrivateRepo`, `copyToPush`, `firstCommit`.
- `packages/runner/src/git/clone-into.ts`: clone into a named folder with a timeout. `runClone` starts git with `GIT_FLAGS` and `NO_HOOKS` from `exec.ts`, so a hook a template folder copies in does not run. With `onProgress` it asks git for `--progress` in English (`LC_ALL=C`) and hands on each move. Key symbols: `cloneInto`, `CLONE_TIMEOUT_MS`, `onProgress`.

### Ticket adapters (read only)

- `packages/runner/src/tickets/cli.ts`: one spawn for `gh` and `glab`. Key symbols: `cliRunner`.
- `packages/runner/src/tickets/gh.ts`: the real `gh`, with an output cap and a sentence for an answer over it that a caller may set (`maxStdout`, `cut`). Key symbols: `ghRunner`, `GhOptions`.
- `packages/runner/src/tickets/github.ts`: GitHub issues and pull requests through `gh`. A pull request carries its head commit and commit count when the answer holds them (`headSha`, `commits`), and in the open list the newest approving or change-asking review of the login gh runs as, with its commit (`ownReview`, picked by `ownReviewOf` from `latestOpinionatedReviews` and the top-level `viewer`). The branch lookup also reads who merged it and when, when it was opened, its size and branches (`BRANCH_FIELDS`), and the login gh runs as (`viewer`). `readPullDiscussions` reads one pull request's review threads, review bodies and issue comments with the viewer, paging every connection and the comments inside a thread (`discussionsQuery`, `threadCommentsQuery`), with each comment's link when it is a GitHub page, and throws on anything it cannot read. The branch lookup also reads why an open pull request cannot merge (`MERGE_FIELDS`, `merge` on the pull request through `mergeBlockers`), and `readMergeStates` reads linked pull requests again by number with the same fields (`mergeQuery`), twenty to a document. Key symbols: `githubIssues`, `getPull`, `readPulls`, `listOpenPulls`, `findBranchPulls`, `readPullDiscussions`, `discussionsQuery`, `threadCommentsQuery`, `PullDiscussions`, `issueKey`, `pullKey`, `mergeQuery`, `readMergeStates`, `MERGE_FIELDS`.
- `packages/runner/src/tickets/github-ci.ts`: failed CI runs of the default branch, and for Fix CI one Actions job's head commit and log (`readJobLog`, two `gh api` GET calls; a rate limit throws) and the end of a log (`trimLog`, `LOG_LINES`, `LOG_BYTES`). `ghLogRunner` is the real gh for a log, with its own 32 MB cap (`LOG_MAX_STDOUT`) and sentence (`LOG_TOO_LARGE`); `readJobLog` names a log over it as too large and goes on. Key symbols: `failedRuns`, `readRuns`, `readJobLog`, `trimLog`, `ghLogRunner`, `LOG_MAX_STDOUT`, `LOG_TOO_LARGE`.
- `packages/runner/src/tickets/gitlab.ts`: GitLab issues through `glab`. Key symbols: `gitlabIssues`, `glabRunner`.
- `packages/runner/src/tickets/jira.ts`: Jira Cloud issues. Key symbols: `jiraIssues`.
- `packages/runner/src/tickets/jira-http.ts`: the Jira calls with the keychain token. Add card reaches it through `jiraReader`; a Scout source keeps a token of its own. Key symbols: `jiraCall`, `jiraFromKeychain`, `JIRA_TOKEN_NAME`.
- `packages/runner/src/tickets/linear.ts`: Linear over GraphQL. Key symbols: `linearIssues`, `linearCall`.
- `packages/runner/src/sentry.ts`: Sentry issues over its API. Key symbols: `sentryIssues`, `readSentryIssues`, `sentryCall`.

### Project files, stack, knowledge, account

- `packages/runner/src/project/plan.ts`: the files onboarding writes into a project, as a plan. `guardSettings` is the settings file every Claude Code run is handed: the guard hook by its full path (left out for null, a project with no guard file), Claude Code's own sandbox off, and its commit and pull request attribution off (`attribution`, `includeCoAuthoredBy`). An older Nolzu's WORKSPACE.md is removed, or loses only its Nolzu section, since AGENTS.md holds that text. Key symbols: `planProjectFiles`, `GUARD_PATH`, `SETTINGS_PATH`, `guardSettings`, `OLD_WORKSPACE`.
- `packages/runner/src/project/apply.ts`: writes a plan. Key symbols: `applyPlan`.
- `packages/runner/src/project/hook.ts`: the PreToolUse guard that holds a read only phase to reading. Key symbols: `PHASE_GUARD`.
- `packages/runner/src/project/markers.ts`: the managed block inside a file. Key symbols: `readBlock`, `writeBlock`.
- `packages/runner/src/project/skill-sync.ts`: copies the phase skills into a project and keeps a record. `SKILL_FOLDERS` is the one list of the two folders the engines read (`.agents/skills`, `.claude/skills`), and `syncSkillFolders` fills each, refusing a linked one alone. It adds the workspace's own skills in `EXTRA_SKILLS` (`.nolzu/skills`) as a second source under the same record, and `syncSkills` refuses an extra skill whose folder name or SKILL.md `name:`, in any case, Nolzu ships, with a sentence in `refused`. The record keeps the Nolzu version that wrote it under `RECORD_VERSION`; a sync whose record names a newer version (`newerVersion`) writes nothing in that folder and reports it in `newer`. `dryRun` works out the same result and writes nothing. Keep mine stores the file under `RECORD_KEPT` with `shippedMark` of the shipped text; a sync reports such a file in `kept` and not in `conflicts` until the shipped text changes, and carries a project file's keep (`keptMark`) as it is. `takeShippedSkill` writes the shipped text, or removes a file Nolzu no longer ships, and records it as Nolzu's; `keepForShipped` sets or clears a keep. `personsSkillText` answers with a project skill file the person changed (neither what Nolzu ships nor what the record holds), and `personsSkillEdit` takes it from the first folder that has one, which `buildJob` uses in place of the shipped one. Key symbols: `syncSkills`, `syncSkillFolders`, `SKILL_FOLDERS`, `EXTRA_SKILLS`, `SYNC_RECORD`, `RECORD_VERSION`, `RECORD_KEPT`, `newerVersion`, `SyncOptions`, `shippedMark`, `takeShippedSkill`, `keepForShipped`, `keptMark`, `personsSkillText`, `personsSkillEdit`.
- `packages/runner/src/project/writable.ts`: whether Nolzu may write a path. Key symbols: `writablePath`.
- `packages/runner/src/stack.ts`: one command of your own run to its end: the Stop and the Status (`runStackHook`, with `commandEnv`: no tokens, `ORIGIN` or `HOST`), `{key}` expanded (`stackCommand`), the stack variables (`stackEnv`), the named address lines (`stackAddresses`), and what a Stop leaves on the card (`stackStatusAfter`). `stackCommand` takes a key starting with a letter, digit, dot or underscore, never a `.` or `..` part. Key symbols: `runStackHook`, `stackStatusAfter`, `stackAddresses`, `stackEnv`, `STACK_UNLOAD_TIMEOUT_MS`.
- `packages/runner/src/app-server.ts`: a repository's start command for one QA run or one Start on the App line: in the background with `PORT` set to a free port and Nolzu's own `ORIGIN` and `HOST` and the GitHub and npm tokens (`RUN_TOKENS`) removed, ready on the first local address it prints or the port answering, stopped with its process group. With `mayExit` (own commands), an exit with 0 is up, with the `<name> <url>` lines it printed (`named`, `exited`), and while it runs only the port answering counts, never a printed address. `installCommand` names the install a checkout's lock file asks for while it has no `node_modules`, and `installApp` runs it the same way to its end, failing with its last line. Handed a `wall`, it runs `/bin/sh -c <command>` through `walledCommand`; `appWall` builds that spec for a checkout (`appWallSpec`, with real paths, the project's wall lists checked as for a run, and the repositories one folder down). Key symbols: `startApp`, `AppServer`, `APP_START_TIMEOUT_MS`, `appWall`, `installCommand`, `installApp`.
- `packages/runner/src/knowledge.ts`: where knowledge may live. `checkKnowledgeDir` also refuses a repository folder of the knowledge folder, or `shared/`, that git ignores (`ignoredRepoFolders`, the sentence that names the `.gitignore` line), for every knowledge run, the repository files and the move. `knowledgeGit` is git in a knowledge folder held by `GIT_DIR` and `GIT_WORK_TREE` to the repository the folder was in before the run, so a repository the run made in it is never used, and its `sweep` removes every `.git` the run made there, at any depth (the loop calls it when the run ends). `knowledgeGit` pins `GIT_COMMON_DIR` as well as `GIT_DIR` and `GIT_WORK_TREE`, and `sweptAfter` calls the sweep once a run's events end, however they end. `checkKnowledgeDir` refuses a folder that is the project folder or holds it, by real path (`holds-project`), because the sweep after a knowledge run removes each new `.git` in the folder. Key symbols: `checkKnowledgeDir`, `ignoredRepoFolders`, `knowledgeGit`, `sweptAfter`.
- `packages/runner/src/knowledge-run.ts`: what a knowledge run changed, and commit (hooks off, as on every git call, and refused when a path would go in as a pointer to another repository), hold, apply, revert. Key symbols: `knowledgeState`, `knowledgeChanges`, `commitKnowledge`, `holdKnowledge`, `knowledgeDiffs`, `applyKnowledge`, `revertKnowledgeFile`.
- `packages/runner/src/hub.ts`: the outbound WebSocket to the hub, with heartbeat and retry. A close that says the cloud data was deleted (`isCloudDataDeleted`) stops it for good and calls `onDeleted`. Key symbols: `connectToHub`, `connectHub`, `readDeviceToken`, `HUB_TIMINGS`, `HubStatus`, `HubReader`.
- `packages/runner/src/login.ts`: `nolzu login`, `logout`, `status` by the device flow. The device code request sends the client id and the OS as a plain word (`osName`). Key symbols: `login`, `logout`, `status`, `accountCommand`, `cloudUrl`, `hubUrl`, `teamHubUrl`, `machineName`, `osName`, `machineAccessToken`.
- `packages/runner/src/keychain.ts`: one secret per name and data folder in the OS keychain: `security` on macOS, `secret-tool` on Linux, whose refusals name the WSL2 keyring fix (`WSL2_KEYRING`). No Windows vault and no file fall back. Key symbols: `createKeychain`, `runKeychain`, `keychainCommand`.

## `packages/ui`

Svelte 5 components and their pure rules. Imports protocol only, and never fetches: the page
does every write and hands in callbacks. The barrel `@nolzu/ui` carries components; the pure
subpaths are `@nolzu/ui/theme`, `/knowledge`, `/qa`, `/review`, `/fixture` and `/tokens.css`.

### Tokens, themes and the barrel

- `packages/ui/package.json`: the export map and the `check` script. `./plan` is `parsePlan` alone, for the loop. Key symbols: `exports`, `check`.
- `packages/ui/src/index.ts`: the barrel. Key symbols: `Board`, `Dialog`, `pushToast`, `KEYS`, `flipTheme`.
- `packages/ui/src/tokens.css`: the design tokens. Dark is the source; light by `data-theme="light"` or the system preference. Fonts ship in `src/fonts`. Key symbols: `--amber`, `--muted`, `--faint`.
- `packages/ui/src/theme.ts`: the theme switch and its storage key. Key symbols: `THEME_KEY`, `flipTheme`.
- `packages/ui/src/svelte.d.ts`: lets TypeScript import a `.svelte` file.

### The Dialog family

- `packages/ui/src/Dialog.svelte`: the one modal, a native `<dialog>` opened with `showModal`: title, sub line, footer, focus in and back. Esc and a click on the backdrop close it unless `held`. The backdrop click is `closeOnBackdrop` in `backdrop.ts`, an attachment the palette's own dialog uses too, and it counts only when the press was outside the box too, so a text drag past the edge keeps it open. Key symbols: `showModal`, `onclose`, `footer`, `held`, `closeOnBackdrop`.
- `packages/ui/src/ConfirmTyped.svelte`: a destructive confirm that asks for a typed name. `checked` ticks what holds, `gone` is the red box, and `blocked` asks for no name and keeps the button off. The bindable `typed` lets a caller keep the name while it is closed. Key symbols: `onconfirm`, `typed`, `typedName`, `checked`, `blocked`.
- `packages/ui/src/confirm.ts`: the exact match rule of that typed name. Key symbols: `typedName`.
- `packages/ui/src/StartConfirm.svelte`: what a key that starts a run shows first, the drop's own lines. With `then` (Fix it's QA) it names both runs and sums both estimates (`chainTarget`). Key symbols: `onconfirm`, `dropTarget`, `chainTarget`.
- `packages/ui/src/KeyHelp.svelte`: the keyboard help. Key symbols: `helpSections`.
- `packages/ui/src/board/AddCardDialog.svelte`: Add card. The card goes on the `board` the page hands over, with no board, kind or size to pick, titled and worded as that board's add button (`addWords`), and the next key is asked once as it opens (`next`), with the person's tickets that have no card yet (`mine`, not on PR reviews). Under an empty field Your tickets lists them, and a press on one sends its link or key through `onsubmit` as a paste would, with no start; a source that failed shows its sentence. A Jira link on a local project draws the blue Connect Jira offer, whose fields go out through the `onconnect` port; Keep it as a local card closes it. A close without a card hands what was typed (title, description, extra context; never the Jira token) back through the bindable `draft`, which Now and the board keep for the next open. Key symbols: `onsubmit`, `onconnect`, `draft`, `cardPayload`, `startOffer`, `board`, `mine`, `showMine`.

### Board parts

- `packages/ui/src/board/Board.svelte`: draws any board from its stages. Reports drags and moves up, and hands a page's `aside` (the first drag's coach note) to the column it names. A page can hand each full card a control under its meta row (`cardAction`, the web's Move forward) and turn every drag off (`draggable`); the local app passes neither. An `aside` puts the board in the first drag (`coaching`). Closed folds over its cards until `archiveOpen`, which the local page binds, and `onFix` draws Fix it on each card `fixItOf` allows. `query` narrows the cards to the filter box's text. A board with no card hands its first column `EmptyInbox` as `placeholder` (`firstSteps`), on every board. `tags` hands each card its tag through its column. Key symbols: `snapshot`, `tags`, `query`, `firstSteps`, `onMove`, `onRefuse`, `keepDrag`, `aside`, `cardAction`, `draggable`, `archiveOpen`, `onFix`, `fixable`, `shutColumns`.
- `packages/ui/src/board/EmptyInbox.svelte`: the one sentence an empty board's first column holds, one per board kind in the same shape: what goes here, how to add one and the key to press. Key symbols: `SAYS`.
- `packages/ui/src/board/BoardColumn.svelte`: one column. `take` decides the drop with `dropOutcome` and calls `onMove` or `onRefuse`. An empty column folds, unless it holds an `aside` or, while `coaching`, is Plan or Implement with its hint (`FIRST_DRAG_HINTS`). The aside's column shows a resting "Drop here" box (`resting`). A `shut` archive column folds over its cards into one button (`onOpen`). A `placeholder` keeps the column open and draws as a dashed card that is not dragged, focused or counted. A strip prints only its name and count; the open header's start line names the engine as the drop box does (`startsCommand`) and takes `estimateBrief`. Key symbols: `take`, `dropOutcome`, `dropTarget`, `refuseDrop`, `startsCommand`, `allowed`, `aside`, `coaching`, `FIRST_DRAG_HINTS`, `resting`, `shut`, `onOpen`, `fixable`, `placeholder`.
- `packages/ui/src/board/BoardCard.svelte`: one card. An epic in In delivery draws "N of M tasks done" with the share and a bar, each task key as a chip that opens its Delivery card (green with a check once done), "N not added yet" with no link, and how many are open and need you; a `finished` epic takes the green ring and "M of M done". Clicks in its `cardAction` stay there and a drag begun on it is refused. A QA FAIL draws the amber Fix it in its meta row when the page hands `onFix`. Its column (`stage`, from `BoardColumn`) gives the pill the phase it finished, so the card draws no phase pips, and a card with nothing for the meta row draws none. The meta row names the engine (a known vendor only), the model and the effort, all three in the title, and ends with the card's total (`totalUsd`), its title naming the newest run's cost. A newest run whose files the output check faulted shows the neutral Check badge in a few words from `checkWords` ("summary missing"), the full sentences in its title and to a screen reader, as a link to `#output-check` on the Overview (`outputProblems`). A Delivery card with review comments shows "3 to fix · 1 new" (`feedback`, worded by `feedbackLine`) with a hidden label for a screen reader. A `tag` draws a small word beside the key, "Question" or "Investigation" on Research. A pull request card on Incoming with a saved round says "Back for round N" beside its key (`back`), the round the next review writes. A card held for a knowledge review links "Review on Knowledge" in its meta row (`review`). A card whose pull request cannot merge draws one chip with the first reason and the count of the rest (`blockerChip`), every reason on hover and to a screen reader, linked to the overview's block. Key symbols: `card`, `tag`, `back`, `onDragStart`, `liveOf`, `notAdded`, `cardAction`, `onFix`, `stage`, `runTitle`, `review`, `blockerChip`.
- `packages/ui/src/board/PeekSheet.svelte`: the side sheet Space opens. The card page's `DecisionPanel` heads the body when the page hands its props (`decision`), with Open card (↵) as a block across the sheet right under it (`data-peek-open`). The summary so far is Markdown, its headings from h4. Its log rows are the Log tab's own `LogLine`, compact. At 760px and under it covers the screen above the rail's band. Key symbols: `peekFacts`, `trailSteps`, `LogLine`, `DecisionPanel`, `decision`.
- `packages/ui/src/board/WaitingToggle.svelte`: Waiting on me in the top bar, with the count the page took off its snapshot. Key symbols: `on`, `count`.
- `packages/ui/src/board/drag.ts`: the drag state with enter and leave counts. Key symbols: `Drag`, `Allowed`, `startDrag`, `enterColumn`, `leaveColumn`, `dropped`, `lockReason`, `keepDrag`.
- `packages/ui/src/board/drop.ts`: what a column shows while dragged over, and what a drop does. `engineName` is the one map from a profile id to its vendor's name, and `ranOn` writes "Claude Code · sonnet · high" for every screen that shows a run's model, the effort left out where the model id carries it, as `startsCommand` leaves it out too. `startsCommand` names the profile with `profileName` ("Claude Code (work)") when the start says `severalProfiles`, which core's snapshot sets with more than one engine profile. `usageLine` gives way to the near-limit warning (`nearLimits`) when a 5-hour or weekly window reads 90% or more. `goneSentence` (from protocol) is the refused drop's words for a gone model, which both settings screens say too. `chainTarget` is two runs one press starts, refused when either is, with the sum of both estimates only when both are measured. Key symbols: `dropTarget`, `chainTarget`, `dropLabel`, `startsCommand`, `engineName`, `ranOn`, `profileName`, `usageLine`, `nearLimits`, `NearLimit`, `refuseDrop`, `goneSentence`, `dropOutcome`, `refusalFromServer`, `keyStart`, `Refusal`, `RUNS_HERE`.
- `packages/ui/src/board/add-card.ts`: Add card fields and the start offer. `readInput` reads a pasted link with `ticketLink` too: a Jira link offers Connect Jira on a local project (`connect`) and reads from Jira on a Jira project, a Linear link stays a local card. `addWords` is each board's add button title and field label, which BoardPage and the dialog share. `showMine` says when Your tickets is drawn and `mineLine` is its count or its quiet line. Key symbols: `addWords`, `readInput`, `canSubmit`, `cardPayload`, `cardKey`, `startOffer`, `Tickets`, `MyTicket`, `MyTickets`, `showMine`, `mineLine`.
- `packages/ui/src/board/filter.ts`: which cards wait on a person, a `finished` epic among them, and the filter box's match by key or title. Key symbols: `waitingOnMe`, `matchesFilter`.
- `packages/ui/src/board/keys.ts`: the board's keys as pure functions, and the cards beside one card in board order for K and J on the card page. `visibleColumns` leaves out the columns `shutColumns` names, so the arrows skip a folded Closed. Key symbols: `boardKey`, `focusMove`, `visibleColumns`, `shutColumns`, `besideCard`.
- `packages/ui/src/board/quiet.ts`: what a running card shows of its run's stream, and the state pill in its words, one rule for the board card and the card page head. A `finished` epic reads "Tasks done", and an idle pull request card pushed to since its last round "1 new commit" (`newCommits`). With the card's stage, a card ready after an agent's phase reads "Plan ready" ("Implement done"), an idle card in the archive "Closed" with a check (`check`); a QA FAIL reads "QA failed"; a queued card held for a knowledge review (`runState` `waiting_review`) reads "Waits for your review". Key symbols: `liveOf`, `cardPill`, `Pill`, `newCommits`.
- `packages/ui/src/board/elapsed.ts`: run time and age words. Key symbols: `elapsed`, `age`.
- `packages/ui/src/board/peek.ts`: the peek sheet's rules. `ago` says "2s ago", or with `words` "12 seconds ago" to "3 hours ago", then "yesterday" and "4 days ago" counted in local dates, for the trail. Key symbols: `peekFacts`, `trailSteps`, `trailTotal`, `ago`.
- `packages/ui/src/board/answer.ts`: where a blocked card is answered, the question split into its lead, body and files, and the asked and waiting line. Key symbols: `answerHref`, `pickOption`, `questionParts`, `askedLine`.
- `packages/ui/src/board/ordinal.ts`: 1st, 2nd, 3rd. Key symbols: `ordinal`.
- `packages/ui/src/board/fixture.ts`: a board and cards written by hand, for tests in any package. Key symbols: `boardFixture`, `cardFixture`, `stageFixture`.

### Card page parts (`src/ticket`)

- `packages/ui/src/ticket/DecisionPanel.svelte`: "Next step" on the card page and at the top of the peek sheet: the one amber button that starts or retries a run, the engine, model and effort selects of `ModelPicker`, whose row title and model description say "Applies to this card only", the estimate and the usage window, a refused start in the toast's words with its fix link, and Stop. Every other action is in `extras`, most likely first: send back or move back, the plain move instead, Accept anyway, Run on per profile, Copy all findings, the phase thread ("Ask about the plan" on a ready card). The first is one plain button under the main one and the rest are rows of `MoreMenu`, each with its data hook and its hold (`extra`, `press`). Minimize is not here: the card page header draws it. A blocked card says when it stopped (`since`) and "Answer from your phone too" with a Solo chip, locked on Free with a Coming soon chip (`phone`). On a QA FAIL the panel opens on the amber Fix it (`fixIt`, `onfix`), with the Implement row's picker, the sum of both estimates, and the line that says which session it resumes; Move back is the plain button and Accept anyway a row of More actions, and the QA thread stays out. Accept anyway opens one line of reason for the trail (`openReason`), refuses an empty one with a sentence, and sends it with the move (`onmove`). With open diff comments (`sendBack`) the amber button is Send back to Implement with N comments (`onsendback`), with the line naming the session it goes on in, and the step it stands in for is a plain "Move to QA instead" with what it starts. Beside the Review tab it says why another round makes sense over the button (`why`) and offers Copy all findings (`findings`, `oncopy`), which writes nothing and holds nothing. Any press holds every button until the snapshot moves the card. Nothing for a closed card. Its heading is an h2, or the h3 a page asks for under its own h2 (`level`). The peek sheet passes `inPeek`, which leaves out the block's Stop and its Send back or Move back, because the sheet's foot has both. With `ondelete` the last row of More actions is a red "Delete card", never the plain button. Key symbols: `ondelete`, `decisionOf`, `level`, `inPeek`, `onmove`, `onretry`, `onfix`, `fixIt`, `sendBack`, `since`, `phone`, `onsendback`, `onpick`, `ModelPicker`, `MoreMenu`, `extras`, `openReason`, `startsCommand`, `chainTarget`, `why`, `findings`, `oncopy`.
- `packages/ui/src/ticket/DeleteCardDialog.svelte`: Delete card's confirm: what goes, the warnings (the red tint only on one that loses something), the "Also delete the card folder" box off by default with what a later card on the key picks up, the refusal with Check again in place of the button, and the red Delete card button, off until the check holds. Key symbols: `onconfirm`, `onrecheck`, `check`, `held`.
- `packages/ui/src/ticket/MoreMenu.svelte`: the "More actions" menu button of Next step: `aria-haspopup`, `aria-expanded`, the arrow keys, Home and End over its `role="menuitem"` rows, Escape closes it and gives the focus back, Tab or a press outside closes it, and a press on a row closes it first. The menu sits in the flow under the button, and its rows stay in the page, hidden, while it is shut. Key symbols: `show`, `close`, `onmenukey`.
- `packages/ui/src/ticket/decision.ts`: the panel's rule per card state, and `fixItOf`, Fix it on a QA FAIL: its label naming both runs, and the Implement and QA stages with the card's own models. The card page, Now and R read it. `sendBackOf` is Send back with comments, by the server's rule (ready or idle, Implement or after it, short of the last column, an open comment), and `decisionOf` makes it the start after Fix it. Key symbols: `decisionOf`, `Decision`, `DecisionKind`, `fixItOf`, `FixIt`, `sendBackOf`, `SendBack`.
- `packages/ui/src/ticket/TicketActions.svelte`: the old head's Move forward, Retry, Run on, Stop and Minimize. No screen draws it now; `DecisionPanel` took its place. Key symbols: `onmove`, `onretry`, `onrunon`.
- `packages/ui/src/ticket/actions.ts`: the press rules the panel reads. Key symbols: `moveView`, `runView`, `runOnView`, `settle`.
- `packages/ui/src/ticket/TabBar.svelte`: the tabs, with what each holds (`counts`). A "+60 -8" count draws its added lines in green and its removed lines in red, as the diff does, and a screen reader hears "60 lines added, 8 removed" (`lines`). A tab named in `pending` draws a pill skeleton until its count lands. No key hint: the keys help lists the tab keys. Key symbols: `cardKey`, `tabs`, `counts`, `pending`, `lines`.
- `packages/ui/src/ticket/Overview.svelte`: the left column of the card page, in three zones under muted labels (`needs`): Needs you with the question box (`AnswerComposer`) on a card that asks, the red QA FAIL box (`QaPanels` with `box`), the read-only mark, `GateBlock` on an open card (`gate`, `onchoose`, `showGate`) and on an open epic the Progress and Tasks blocks (`epicNeeds`, `epicParts`); What happened with the plain summary in a `Block` whose header names which phase wrote it and when and links the plan as written on a card ready after Plan (`summaryPhase`) or the diff, on a QA FAIL the build's summary and not the QA run's (`built`), an epic's solution excerpt and document chips (`docs`, `docHref`), and `ClosedCard` on a closed card; Read more with the QA record and the stack line (`QaPanels`, the verdict on any card QA ran on, the stack on an open card in QA or with a stack not down), the plan steps with their file chips and Covers `AC` chips (`parsePlan`), open while `planOpen` and folded after, on a QA FAIL and on a closed card, the ticket folded with its meta ("local, refined 20 minutes ago") and Add context at the end that opens the extra context under it (`ticketOpen`), related cards folded on an open card (`linkStatus`), and on a closed card the page's checkouts snippet last. A zone with nothing in it is not drawn; the blocks of What happened and Read more have a hairline between them (`lined`). On an epic the plan's steps are the task table (`epicTaskRows`): each row links to its Delivery card with its live state, or keeps its key, title, description (the task of `share/PLAN.md` with the step's number, `shareTasks`, which gives the title too), internal notes (the step's body, `stepBodies`) and linked ticket (`link` through `onreadticket`, refused when another row has it; the key field hides then) behind a row toggle with `aria-expanded` that opens `TaskDetail`, editable until Add N tasks to Delivery, a plain button that sends refs and notes too, with Remove on a row and Add a task for an empty one, all page state (`drafts`, `dropped`, `blanks`); before Plan ran one line says what a task is and no box shows; after Plan, a plan with no steps to read lets the person type the list. What changed reads at body size. A walled run's mark (`walled`) says the wall kept the run from writing, so the change came from another program or from the person. The open pull requests that cannot merge draw `MergeBlock` above a closed card's part. Key symbols: `onanswer`, `laterHref`, `onaddtasks`, `onreadticket`, `oncopy`, `sharePlan`, `linkSystem`, `parsePlan`, `epicTaskRows`, `epicProgress`, `ClosedCard`, `checkouts`, `summaryPhase`, `summaryAt`, `now`, `docHref`, `readOnlyBreak`, `planOpen`, `needs`, `epicNeeds`, `MergeBlock`. Its `clock` starts at `now`, the server's time, and draws the summary's and Refine's times ago with `Ago`. `readOnlyBreak` draws the read-only mark in Needs you under a question or QA FAIL: a neutral `Banner` warning naming the phase and engine, what Nolzu put back or could not, and the diff behind Show the diff. `commitWarning` draws the newest Implement run's commit or push sentences under it, in the same box (`data-commit-warning`).
- `packages/ui/src/ticket/epic.ts`: `epicTaskRows`, the live-state join of an epic's task table: each step of the plan matched by the step its link kept, or by title, to the Delivery card it became (`matchPlan`, the rule the board's count uses too), its state read off the Delivery snapshot (Done, the stage with the run time, Inbox), then the linked tasks the plan does not name, and a proposed key for each task not added yet. `epicProgress` is the Progress panel read off those rows. `landLine` is the line beside Add N tasks to Delivery: the tasks counted as local tickets and as tickets of the system with their keys, singular or plural, and that nothing starts and nothing is written to that system. Key symbols: `epicTaskRows`, `TaskRow`, `TaskInput`, `epicProgress`, `landLine`.
- `packages/ui/src/ticket/plan.ts`: `parsePlan`, which reads the steps of `plan/plan.md` and the files each names, and the `AC` ids of its Covers line (`covers`, left out without one), in the form the plan skill teaches, or hands the file back as text when it drifted. `stepBodies` gives each step as Markdown, its sentence and its fields as a list (Uses, Copy and Check too), which an epic's task takes as its description, and with an `EpicSeed` (the epic's criteria and decisions) the criteria of its Covers line and the `D` ids it names copied under it as `- From APP-5: AC2 (must): ...`. A plan with the review block, decisions and Read first reads the same. `shareTasks` reads the `### <n>. <title>` tasks under `## Tasks` of `share/PLAN.md` by number, each with the text up to the next heading. Key symbols: `parsePlan`, `PlanView`, `PlanStep`, `stepBodies`, `EpicSeed`, `shareTasks`, `ShareTask`.
- `packages/ui/src/ticket/TaskDetail.svelte`: the open row of an epic's task not added yet, a property list in the look of the Settings rows: Ticket (Link to the system, the key and the ticket's own title with Unlink, or a refusal), Description and Internal notes, each drawn as Markdown with Edit, Save and Cancel, one field at a time, the Description read only with a muted line on a linked row (the card takes the ticket's own text), and Copy for the system with a status line. A Link read answered after Cancel is dropped (`attempt`, `still`), and the Overview's `link` also drops one a plan rewrite overtook. It writes nothing; the Overview keeps the edits, and shows the `note` of an add that could not write some notes after its status. Key symbols: `onedit`, `onlink`, `onunlink`, `oncopy`.
- `packages/ui/src/ticket/ClosedCard.svelte`: a closed card under its summary: the pull request on a Delivery card as one line, asked once the page has drawn and never on the server (`onpulls`), "#42 merged by you, 2 hours ago" as an `h2` like every block title (`headline` and `Ago` on `now`, the page's clock), the repository, head into base and size (`where`) and a plain Open on GitHub, all in one row like a block header; then What Nolzu learned as an open `Block` with when it was applied and a plain Revert (`onrevert`). A skeleton line stands for the pull request while gh answers, and a block for each patch while `diffsWaiting`. The checkouts are the Overview's last line. Key symbols: `onpulls`, `onrevert`, `diffsWaiting`, `headline`, `where`.
- `packages/ui/src/ticket/MergeBlock.svelte`: the Overview's "Why it cannot merge": each reason on its own line with its links, checks still running, and when it was read; a card with several pull requests names each one. Beside the first failed checks it draws Fix CI (`fixCi`): the go button while it would start Implement, what it runs on, the estimate when measured, the line on where the logs go, and the refusal; a press holds until the board shows the card moved. Key symbols: `pulls`, `now`, `fixCi`.
- `packages/ui/src/ticket/CardRecord.svelte`: a closed card's right column: the totals with the cost marked estimated, then every move with the done check, who made it and when, each phase's newest run on the newest move that started it with its engine, model, effort and wall (`ranWith`, no effort on an engine that reads it off the model id, `effortInModel`), a line the page read later in place of the mover on the newest move into a stage (`notes`, the Pull request row), every time drawn as a time ago on `now` (`Ago`), and the reason under a move that went on anyway, or Sent back with the note under a move back. Key symbols: `runOf`, `ranWith`, `figures`, `notes`, `partsOf`, `now`.
- `packages/ui/src/ticket/GateBlock.svelte`: "Before you approve" in the Overview's Needs you, a `Block` boxed and open while a question is open, folded with no box once every question is settled: which phase wrote it and Open <file> on the Files tab, the review block lines as Markdown (or a line saying the file has none), each `blocks: yes` question with Take the default (when it names one) and Answer it, or the choice it was settled with, and the output check as a neutral Check badge over its list with the project instructions note, the list at `#output-check`, which opens the block and the list when the address ends in it. The page does the write (`onchoose`). Once every question is settled and `next` names a column, a line under it says that column gets the answers, with Start <column> (`onstart`), and focus goes there. Key symbols: `gate`, `fileHref`, `onchoose`, `next`, `onstart`.
- `packages/ui/src/ticket/Block.svelte`: one block of the Overview's left column with the header every block shares: the title, the grey meta (`meta`), one plain link or button at the end (`end`). With `fold` the title is a chevron button with `aria-expanded` that shows and hides the body, and a one line preview (`peek`) shows while it is folded; a list of steps reads as sentences, each with its own full stop. `open` is the start state the page hands in, page state only. Key symbols: `fold`, `open`, `peek`, `end`, `meta`.
- `packages/ui/src/ticket/ResearchAnswer.svelte`: the answer or the report of a Research card in the Overview's What happened zone: the file rendered with its first paragraph at the lede size, the sources as chips, Make a Delivery card with its own pending state and its outcome line (`ondeliver`), and Ask a follow-up, a link to the phase's thread on the Discussion tab (`followHref`).
- `packages/ui/src/ticket/AnswerComposer.svelte`: the question box of a blocked card (`id="asks"`, where the Answer link lands): the "Plan asks you" chip and the question at the lede size (`--text-lg`), then, in the same violet box, Your answer over the options, the field, Send and resume in amber with Cmd Enter (Send answer, plain, when no session is left), Answer later, and the line naming the session it resumes and what it runs on. Beside the chip "asked 15:02 · waiting 12m" from the Now snapshot's wait start (`since`, `askedLine`); under the question its other paragraphs and file chips (`questionParts`). No estimate, which nothing reads. Key symbols: `onanswer`, `laterHref`, `startsCommand`, `since`, `questionParts`.
- `packages/ui/src/ticket/PhaseStrip.svelte`: the trail ("Trail"), one phase under the other, in the card page's right column: the total beside the heading with the cost marked estimated, each phase with a log link at the row's end, one detail line (`detail`: engine, model, effort, the wall that held the run with `wallLabel`, time), and who moved the card into it and when (`moves`). Its mark is a check in a soft green circle when done, a violet "?" ring with "asked you 5 minutes ago" (`since`), a red x ring with "FAIL, <reason>, <time ago>" (`failLine`), and an amber ring with "waits for your approval" on the first phase still to run after a done one (`next`); each later phase says what it will do (`WILL`). A move that went on with a reason (Accept anyway) shows under the phase it left, with who and when (`goneOn`), and a move back with a note reads Sent back. Every time is a time ago on `now`, the page's clock (`Ago`). Key symbols: `strip`, `moves`, `WILL`, `goneOn`, `next`, `since`, `now`, `failLine`, `detail`.
- `packages/ui/src/ticket/Ago.svelte`: one time on the trail and the Overview, a `<time>` with the words of `ago` ("5 minutes ago") on the clock it is given and the full date and time as its title. A stamp that is no time draws nothing. Key symbols: `at`, `now`.
- `packages/ui/src/ticket/QaPanels.svelte`: the QA parts of the Overview. With `box` and `failed`, on a card that waits in QA on a FAIL, the red box of Needs you (`id="qa-panel"`): the verdict with how many checks failed and the QA run's line (engine first, `ranOn`) ending in how long ago it ended, the reason, and the failed rows with their screenshots. Without `box` the record of Read more: on a FAIL "What QA checked", open, with every screenshot and check; otherwise "QA verdict" folded with the verdict word and the count (`checkCount`) in its header and the reason or note as its preview, the steps when a pass gave neither, and inside the reason or note, the checks, the screenshots and the run's log link (`verdict` hides it). Both end their header in Open the QA report. Under it the stack as one line (`stack`, always on a FAIL) with its pill, sentence and only the buttons that fit, and what the status command printed folded under it. The rows (`row`, `checks`) carry their `AC` id as a mono chip before the step, a word beside every colour, a short fail reason beside it and a longer reason or the `Ran:` line under it (`checkWord`), section 2 as written when it drifted, nothing with no section 2; the screenshots (`shots`) are small thumbnails in a wrapping grid, each with its name, its check and result word, a red edge on a fail, and a plain link to the Files tab with the file open (`shotHref`, the image itself from `fileHref`). Key symbols: `onstack`, `failed`, `box`, `verdict`, `shotHref`, `shots`, `row`, `checks`, `checkOfShot`, `checkWord`, `checkCount`.
- `packages/ui/src/ticket/qa.ts`: their rules, and `parseQaChecklist`, which reads section 2 of `qa/report.md` as rows (a step, its result `pass`, `fail` or `not reached`, a reason, what ran from the `How:` line or an older report's `Ran:` line, the `Evidence:`, `Expected:` and `Actual:` lines when there are any, and the `AC` id the step starts with, `ac` through `splitAc`, which also takes a level after the id, left out without one) or hands the section back as text when it drifted. An unknown word is never a pass. The format is taught in the qa skill. `qaView` reads the checklist off the report text the page sends (`reportText`), and `checkOfShot` reads the check a screenshot shows off the number its name starts with. The stack view carries the line's pill word, its sentence (`StackSaid`, the load command where the row holds `TAKEN_DOWN`) and the actions that fit (`fits`); the verdict view carries the first address of `qa/addresses.md`. `checkWord` is a row's result with a short reason and the line under it, `checkCount` the count line over the rows. Key symbols: `checkCount`, `qaView`, `QaView`, `StackAction`, `NOT_REPORTED`, `parseQaChecklist`, `QA_RESULTS`, `QaChecklist`, `checkOfShot`, `checkWord`, `StackSaid`.
- `packages/ui/src/ticket/ChangesTab.svelte`: one repository's change: the conflict box, the diff head (files, lines, the stat squares, the comment count; none on an empty diff), the file list, and the open file's diff with both line numbers and a gutter, its patch asked from `group` (the base to the folder by default) and its label naming `against`. A press on the gutter opens a comment field under that line (`oncomment`); saved comments show under their line (`placeComments`), an outdated one at the end of its file, and one whose file left the diff under its file name. Key symbols: `onopen`, `oncomment`, `onedit`, `ondelete`, `placeComments`, `numberedLines`.
- `packages/ui/src/ticket/ChangeGroups.svelte`: the Not committed and Whole card switch (`view`, `onview`; no switch without `onview`), with Not committed drawing the uncommitted group, no comments, and with nothing to commit "Nothing to commit" and the commit list (`history`), a commit opening its own read-only diff (`oncommitdiff`, `opened`). The repository switcher over only the repositories with something in the view (`inView`: `hasUncommitted` or a commit ahead on Not committed, `hasWork` on Whole card, `tabs`), drawn with one markup for one repository or several so a switch keeps each panel and its typed message: one small tab each with its name and "+12 -3" (`lines`), and one panel shown at a time (`shown`, the arrow keys in `arrows`), each opening with the facts line: the name, the branch in full (wrapped, never cut), what is not committed, base, ahead and behind (`facts`). One such repository draws its facts line with no switcher, and with none the first repository shows with its reason. The others fold into "N without changes" at the end (`rest`, `showRest`). A group's `name` from the page names it there and in the Commit and push header, and the root falls back to "project folder" only when the page sends none. Each panel is two columns: the diff, and on the right the page's `next` snippet (shown once, in the shown repository, while a comment is not sent) and one "Commit and push" panel holding `CommitComposer` and `PublishPanel`, with a plain line on how many of that repository's comments are not sent yet (`unsent`). It draws the panel for commits ahead or work in the folder; Push in it is on by the commits the remote has not got (`unpushed`, else `ahead`) and commits nothing. Key symbols: `oncommit`, `onpush`, `uncommitted`, `standing`, `tabs`, `rest`, `shown`, `arrows`, `next`, `unsent`, `view`, `onview`, `oncommitdiff`, `history`, `opened`.
- `packages/ui/src/ticket/LineComment.svelte`: one comment under its diff line, or the field of a new one: who, its line (and the line it was made on when an edit moved it), the text, Edit, Delete, and "Goes with the next send back". An outdated one shows the line it was made on in a neutral box. Cmd Enter saves, Esc cancels. Key symbols: `onsave`, `ondelete`, `oncancel`, `outdated`.
- `packages/ui/src/ticket/comments.ts`: exported as `@nolzu/ui/comments` too, so the loop can read it. `placeComments`, where each comment of a file shows in its current diff: matched by the saved line text nearest the saved number (within `NEAR` lines), so an edit above moves it with its line, and outdated when the text is gone. Key symbols: `placeComments`, `NEAR`.
- `packages/ui/src/ticket/SendBackPanel.svelte`: "Next step" on the Changes tab while a comment is open: Send back to Implement with N comments in amber, what it runs on, the estimate where measured, the session it goes on in, or a line that the comments wait while the card cannot go back. Holds until the card in the snapshot moves. Key symbols: `onsend`, `onrefuse`, `sendBack`.
